not tracked no coverage low coverage * * * * * * * * high coverage 
// Command initiative-api serves the Initiative! API.
package main

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "log"
        "log/slog"
        "net/http"
        "os"
        "os/signal"
        "sync/atomic"
        "syscall"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-contrib/cors"
        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/gomodule/redigo/redis"
        "github.com/joho/godotenv"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/api-core.go/ratelimit"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/api-core.go/tracing"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/api-core.go/util"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/common.go/logging"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/config"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/constants"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/entitlements"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/events"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/events/stream"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/relations"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/server"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/startup"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/mongodb.go/database"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson/primitive"
)

func main() {
        _ = godotenv.Load(".env")

        cfg, err := config.FromEnv()
        if err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("invalid configuration:\n%v", err)
        }

        // common.go/logging reads LOG_LEVEL itself; pass the validated, defaulted value.
        _ = os.Setenv(constants.LOG_LEVEL, cfg.LogLevel)
        logging.Init()

        tracing.SetupTracing(constants.ServiceName)
        defer tracing.TeardownTracing()

        // Reads the MongoDB connection settings from the environment; waits out a brief resolver or
        // network failure instead of crashing the pod.
        retry := startup.DefaultPolicy()
        if err := startup.ConnectDatabase(retry); err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("database: %v", err)
        }
        defer database.TeardownDatabase()
        db := store.MongoDatabase{DB: database.Db}

        fanout := db.Collection(stream.FanoutCollection)
        if err := startup.Do("create indexes", startup.IndexPolicy(retry), func() error { // a conflicting index never resolves by waiting
                ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
                defer cancel()
                if err := data.EnsureIndexes(ctx, db); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                return fanout.EnsureIndexes(ctx, stream.FanoutIndexes)
        }); err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("indexes: %v", err)
        }

        themes, err := domain.NewThemes(db)
        if err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("themes: %v", err)
        }
        creds := domain.NewCredentials(db)
        pool := util.RedisPool() // non-nil: config requires REDIS_HOST
        rosters, encounters, sessions := domain.NewRosters(db), domain.NewEncounters(db), domain.NewSessions(db)
        // Fail-open: the hub publishes from its own queue and drops an event it cannot send.
        var pub *events.Publisher
        if natsURL := os.Getenv("NATS_URL"); natsURL != "" {
                var err error
                if pub, err = events.NewPublisher(natsURL); err != nil {
                        log.Fatalf("event publisher: %v", err)
                }
                defer pub.Close() // deferred before hub.Close, so it runs after the queue that feeds it stops
        }
        hub := stream.New(encounters)
        defer hub.Close()
        if pub != nil {
                hub.AttachBus(pub, 1024)
        }
        encounters.SetSink(hub)
        sessions.SetSink(hub)
        sessions.SetEvictor(hub)
        host, _ := os.Hostname()
        pod := host + "-" + primitive.NewObjectID().Hex() // unique per process: a pod never mistakes another's rows for its own
        relay := hub.AttachRelay(fanout, pod, 1024)
        relay.Run(context.Background())
        defer relay.Stop()
        // Credentials stay off (503) until the auth-event consumer is bound and caught up, so a deny
        // published while the service was down is applied before any secret authenticates (PADR-0039).
        // The consumer starts after the hub, the bus and the relay exist, so an event it applies first (a
        // deny) already finds the hub to evict from and nothing is acked into a void. It starts in the
        // background, so the rest of the API does not wait on NATS; if NATS
        // stays unreachable past CREDENTIAL_EVENTS_MAX_DISCONNECT the gate closes again.
        if natsURL := os.Getenv("NATS_URL"); natsURL == "" {
                logging.Logger.Warn("NATS_URL is not set; integration credentials are disabled")
        } else {
                var live atomic.Pointer[events.Consumer]
                consumerCtx, stopConsumer := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
                go runConsumer(consumerCtx, creds, hub, natsURL, os.Getenv("NATS_DURABLE_NAME"), cfg.CredentialEventsMaxDisconnect, retry, &live)
                defer func() {
                        stopConsumer() // first, so runConsumer does not rebuild what Stop closes
                        if c := live.Load(); c != nil {
                                c.Stop()
                        }
                }()
        }
        rosters.SetSink(hub)
        sessions.OwnerRecordMaxAge = cfg.OwnerEntitlementsMaxAge
        sessions.MaxMembers = cfg.SessionMembersMax
        streams := domain.NewStreams(db, sessions, encounters)
        prefs := domain.NewNotificationPrefs(db, sessions)
        changeRequests := domain.NewChangeRequests(db, encounters, sessions)
        changeRequests.Notifier = hub
        hub.SetPreferences(prefs)
        hub.SetManagers(sessions)
        hub.SetMembers(sessions)
        streams.TTL = cfg.StreamTicketTTL
        ready := server.NewReadiness(5*time.Second, 2*time.Second, server.Check{Name: "database", Fn: func(ctx context.Context) error { return database.Db.Client().Ping(ctx, nil) }})
        svc := server.Services{
                Ready: ready,
                Locks: encounters.Locks(),
                Limits: domain.NewLimits(
                        domain.Kind{Name: "themes", Key: domain.KeyThemeSlots, Repo: themes.Items()},
                        domain.Kind{Name: "rosters", Key: domain.KeySavedRosters, Repo: rosters.Items()},
                        domain.Kind{Name: "encounters", Key: domain.KeySavedEncounters, Repo: encounters.Items()},
                        domain.Kind{Name: "sessions", Key: domain.KeySessionsPerGM, Repo: sessions.Items()},
                        domain.Kind{Name: "credentials", Key: domain.KeyIntegrationCredentials, Repo: creds.Items()},
                ),
                Authz:             authz.NewClient(cfg.AuthAPIURL, cfg.UsersAPIURL),
                Entitlements:      entitlements.New(cfg.EntitlementsAPIURL),
                OwnerLimits:       data.NewOwnerEntitlements(db, cfg.OwnerEntitlementsMaxAge),
                Themes:            themes,
                Systems:           data.NewSystemRepo(db),
                Credentials:       creds,
                Rosters:           rosters,
                Encounters:        encounters,
                Sessions:          sessions,
                ChangeRequests:    changeRequests,
                NotificationPrefs: prefs,
                Relations:         relations.New(cfg.AuthAPIURL),
                Hub:               hub,
                Streams:           streams,
                Leases:            data.NewStreamLeases(db),
                Stream: server.StreamOptions{
                        AllowedOrigins: cfg.StreamAllowedOrigins, MaxAge: cfg.StreamMaxAge, Heartbeat: cfg.StreamHeartbeat, RecheckInterval: cfg.StreamRecheckInterval,
                        ConcurrentMax: cfg.StreamConcurrentMax, CredentialMax: cfg.StreamCredentialMax, MaxPerPod: cfg.StreamMaxPerPod, LeaseTTL: cfg.StreamLeaseTTL, Grace: cfg.StreamGrace, Pod: pod,
                },
        }
        srv := &http.Server{Addr: cfg.BindAddress, Handler: newRouter(cfg, svc, pool), ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second}

        go func() {
                if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
                        logging.Logger.Error("server failed", "error", err.Error())
                        os.Exit(1)
                }
        }()

        stop := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
        signal.Notify(stop, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
        <-stop

        // http.Server.Shutdown does not wait for upgraded connections, so end the event streams first
        // (each closes with 1001 and its client reconnects to another pod), give their close frames a
        // moment to go out, then drain the ordinary requests.
        hub.Shutdown()
        ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
        defer cancel()
        drain, drainCancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 3*time.Second)
        hub.Drain(drain)
        drainCancel()
        if err := srv.Shutdown(ctx); err != nil {
                logging.Logger.Error("shutdown failed", "error", err.Error())
        }
}

func newRouter(cfg config.Config, svc server.Services, pool *redis.Pool) *gin.Engine {
        httpLogger := slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(os.Stdout, nil))

        r := gin.New()
        r.Use(server.Middleware(httpLogger, cfg.RequestTimeout)...)

        if len(cfg.AllowedOrigins) > 0 {
                r.Use(cors.New(cors.Config{
                        AllowOrigins:     cfg.AllowedOrigins,
                        AllowMethods:     []string{"GET", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"},
                        AllowHeaders:     []string{"Origin", "Content-Type", "Accept", "Authorization", constants.CorrelationHeader},
                        ExposeHeaders:    []string{constants.CorrelationHeader},
                        AllowCredentials: true,
                        MaxAge:           12 * time.Hour,
                }))
        }

        r.Use(server.RateLimit(pool, ratelimit.DefaultOptions(), svc.Credentials)...)

        server.SetupHandlers(r, svc)
        return r
}

// Pacing for runConsumer: the wait between attempts, and how long a consumer must live before a
// loss counts as new trouble rather than a flap.
const (
        consumerFirstWait = 5 * time.Second
        consumerMaxWait   = 2 * time.Minute
        consumerMinLife   = time.Minute
)

// runConsumer keeps an auth-event consumer running until ctx ends: it starts one (retrying with a
// capped backoff, an error logged each round, so a NATS that is down at boot is picked up when it
// returns) and builds a new one if the connection is lost for good. Until a consumer has started and
// caught up, credentials stay closed (503) and the rest of the API serves.
func runConsumer(ctx context.Context, creds *domain.Credentials, evict events.Evictor, natsURL, durable string, maxDisconnect time.Duration, retry startup.Policy, live *atomic.Pointer[events.Consumer]) {
        wait := consumerFirstWait
        for ctx.Err() == nil {
                var c *events.Consumer
                err := startup.Do("start the auth-event consumer", retry, func() error {
                        c = events.New(creds, creds)
                        c.Evictor = evict
                        c.MaxDisconnect = maxDisconnect
                        return c.Start(ctx, natsURL, durable)
                })
                if err != nil {
                        logging.Logger.Error("the auth-event consumer did not start; integration credentials stay closed; retrying", "error", err.Error(), "retry_in", wait.String())
                        select {
                        case <-ctx.Done():
                                return
                        case <-time.After(wait):
                        }
                        wait = min(wait*2, consumerMaxWait)
                        continue
                }
                started := time.Now()
                live.Store(c)
                select {
                case <-c.Dead():
                        logging.Logger.Error("the auth-event consumer lost its connection for good; starting a new one", "retry_in", wait.String())
                        c.Stop()
                case <-ctx.Done():
                        return
                }
                if time.Since(started) >= consumerMinLife {
                        wait = consumerFirstWait // it ran long enough to count as healthy
                }
                select {
                case <-ctx.Done():
                        return
                case <-time.After(wait):
                }
                wait = min(wait*2, consumerMaxWait)
        }
}

// Command reconciler runs one OpenFGA tuple reconciliation pass and exits (PADR-0035, PADR-0038).
// It authenticates to auth-api as an Auth0 machine-to-machine client and caches the token in Redis
// (PADR-0041). Exit codes: 1 pass failed, Auth0/Redis failed, or the database stayed unreachable after retries, 2 missing configuration, 3 auth-api
// rejected the token. Any non-zero exit shows on the CronJob and fires the alert.
package main

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "log"
        "net/url"
        "os"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/api-core.go/util"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/common.go/logging"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/reconcile"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/startup"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/mongodb.go/database"
)

func main() { os.Exit(run()) }

func run() int {
        logging.Init()
        tokens, authURL, ok := tokenSource()
        if !ok {
                return 2
        }

        if err := startup.ConnectDatabase(startup.DefaultPolicy()); err != nil {
                logging.Logger.Error("cannot reach the database", "error", err.Error())
                return 1
        }
        defer database.TeardownDatabase()

        // Under the CronJob's activeDeadlineSeconds (270); the last minute is reserved for mint
        // garbage collection (reconcile.Reconciler.GCReserve).
        ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 4*time.Minute)
        defer cancel()
        sessions := data.NewSessions(store.MongoDatabase{DB: database.Db})
        api := reconcile.NewAuthAPIWithSource(authURL, tokens)
        r := reconcile.Reconciler{Members: sessions, Tuples: api, Mints: api, Sessions: sessions}
        res, err := r.Run(ctx)
        logging.Logger.Info("reconcile finished", "sessions", res.Sessions, "written", res.Written, "deleted", res.Deleted, "failed", res.Failed,
                "mints_closed", res.MintsClosed, "mints_skipped", res.MintsSkipped)
        switch {
        case errors.Is(err, reconcile.ErrTokenRejected):
                logging.Logger.Error("auth-api rejected the reconciler token (401, 403, or a rejection recorded in the last hour); check the initiative-reconciler role, deny entries, login_profiles mapping and audience (PADR-0041)")
                return 3
        case err != nil:
                logging.Logger.Error("reconcile failed", "error", err.Error())
                return 1
        }
        return 0
}

// settings is the reconciler's token configuration.
type settings struct {
        authURL, clientID, clientSecret, tokenURL, audience string
}

// loadSettings reads and validates the environment. The token URL must be https so the client
// secret never crosses the network in clear text. The secret is read here and never logged.
func loadSettings(get func(string) string) (settings, error) {
        s := settings{
                authURL: get("AUTH_API_URL"), clientID: get("RECONCILER_CLIENT_ID"), clientSecret: get("RECONCILER_CLIENT_SECRET"),
                tokenURL: get("RECONCILER_TOKEN_URL"), audience: get("RECONCILER_AUDIENCE"),
        }
        for _, f := range []struct{ name, value string }{
                {"AUTH_API_URL", s.authURL}, {"RECONCILER_CLIENT_ID", s.clientID}, {"RECONCILER_CLIENT_SECRET", s.clientSecret},
                {"RECONCILER_TOKEN_URL", s.tokenURL}, {"RECONCILER_AUDIENCE", s.audience}, {"REDIS_HOST", get("REDIS_HOST")},
        } {
                if f.value == "" {
                        return settings{}, errors.New(f.name + " is required")
                }
        }
        if u, err := url.Parse(s.tokenURL); err != nil || u.Scheme != "https" || u.Host == "" || u.User != nil {
                return settings{}, errors.New("RECONCILER_TOKEN_URL must be an https URL without credentials")
        }
        return s, nil
}

// tokenSource builds the Auth0 client-credentials source from the environment.
func tokenSource() (src reconcile.TokenSource, authURL string, ok bool) {
        s, err := loadSettings(os.Getenv)
        if err != nil {
                log.Print(err.Error())
                return nil, "", false
        }
        pool := util.RedisPool()
        if pool == nil {
                log.Print("REDIS_HOST is required for the token cache")
                return nil, "", false
        }
        return reconcile.NewClientCredentials(s.tokenURL, s.clientID, s.clientSecret, s.audience, pool), s.authURL, true
}

// Command seed applies the checked-in game-system definitions to the configured database. It is
// idempotent and safe to run at every deploy.
package main

import (
        "context"
        "log"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/common.go/logging"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/seed"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/mongodb.go/database"
)

func main() {
        logging.Init()
        database.SetupDatabase() // reads the MongoDB connection settings from the environment
        defer database.TeardownDatabase()

        ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
        defer cancel()

        db := store.MongoDatabase{DB: database.Db}
        if err := data.EnsureIndexes(ctx, db); err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("indexes: %v", err)
        }
        res, err := seed.Load(ctx, data.NewSystemRepo(db))
        if err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("seed: %v", err)
        }
        log.Printf("game systems seeded: created=%d updated=%d unchanged=%d", res.Created, res.Updated, res.Unchanged)
}

// Package config loads and validates the service configuration from the environment.
package config

import (
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "net"
        "net/url"
        "os"
        "path"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/constants"
)

// Config is the validated runtime configuration.
type Config struct {
        BindAddress string
        Env         string
        Version     string
        LogLevel    string
        // OTLPEndpoint is the trace collector base URL; empty leaves the exporter default.
        OTLPEndpoint string
        // AllowedOrigins is the CORS allowlist; empty disables CORS (fail closed).
        AllowedOrigins []string
        // AuthAPIURL is auth-api's base URL. Bearer tokens are verified there via /authz/check
        // (signature, expiry, iss, aud, roles); this service holds no signing keys or credentials.
        AuthAPIURL string
        // UsersAPIURL is users-api's base URL, used to resolve the verified caller to users._id.
        UsersAPIURL string
        // EntitlementsAPIURL is entitlements-api's base URL for gating checks.
        EntitlementsAPIURL string
        // RedisHost is the rate limiter's counter store; the limiter fails closed without it
        // (PADR-0036), so it is required. Port and password are read by the Redis pool itself.
        RedisHost string
        // OwnerEntitlementsMaxAge is how long a recorded owner entitlement value stays usable for
        // integration credentials, which cannot read it live. Past it the credential gets
        // limits_not_synced until the owner opens the app.
        OwnerEntitlementsMaxAge time.Duration
        // SessionMembersMax is the maximum of a session's member limit: an entitled members_per_session
        // above it has no effect (initiative-entitlement-gating, "Session member limit").
        SessionMembersMax int
        // CredentialEventsMaxDisconnect is how long NATS may be unreachable before integration
        // credentials fail closed (503); the rest of the API keeps serving.
        CredentialEventsMaxDisconnect time.Duration
        // StreamAllowedOrigins are the browser origins (host patterns) that may open an event stream; a
        // request with no Origin header (a native client) is always allowed.
        StreamAllowedOrigins []string
        // StreamTicketTTL is how long a stream ticket can be redeemed; StreamMaxAge ends every stream
        // after that long, so a revoked credential or a missed eviction cannot outlive it;
        // StreamHeartbeat is the interval of the server's heartbeat frames.
        StreamTicketTTL, StreamMaxAge, StreamHeartbeat time.Duration
        // StreamRecheckInterval is how often an open stream re-checks membership and credentials; it
        // never exceeds StreamHeartbeat.
        StreamRecheckInterval time.Duration
        // RequestTimeout bounds every request except the event stream's upgrade.
        RequestTimeout time.Duration
        // StreamConcurrentMax is the most event streams one user may hold open across all pods and
        // StreamCredentialMax the most one integration credential may (its own count, not the owner's);
        // StreamMaxPerPod caps one pod's connections. StreamLeaseTTL is how long a stream's seat lasts
        // without a renewal (a crashed pod's seats lapse after it), and StreamGrace how long a dropped
        // client keeps its seat so its reconnect does not need a free one.
        StreamConcurrentMax, StreamCredentialMax, StreamMaxPerPod int
        StreamLeaseTTL, StreamGrace                               time.Duration
}

// defaultOwnerEntitlementsMaxAge is seven days.
const defaultOwnerEntitlementsMaxAge = "168h"

var logLevels = map[string]bool{"DEBUG": true, "INFO": true, "WARN": true, "ERROR": true}

// Load reads the environment through lookup (os.LookupEnv in production) and returns every
// validation problem at once, so a misconfigured deploy reports all of them in one start attempt.
func Load(lookup func(string) (string, bool)) (Config, error) {
        get := func(key, def string) string {
                if v, ok := lookup(key); ok && strings.TrimSpace(v) != "" {
                        return strings.TrimSpace(v)
                }
                return def
        }

        c := Config{
                BindAddress:        get("BIND_ADDRESS", ":8000"),
                Env:                get("ENV", "dev"),
                Version:            get("VERSION", "v0.0.0"),
                LogLevel:           strings.ToUpper(get(constants.LOG_LEVEL, "WARN")),
                OTLPEndpoint:       get(constants.OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT, ""),
                AuthAPIURL:         strings.TrimRight(get(constants.AUTH_API_URL, ""), "/"),
                UsersAPIURL:        strings.TrimRight(get(constants.USERS_API_URL, ""), "/"),
                EntitlementsAPIURL: strings.TrimRight(get(constants.ENTITLEMENTS_API_URL, ""), "/"),
                RedisHost:          get("REDIS_HOST", ""),
        }
        if origins := get(constants.ALLOWED_ORIGINS, ""); origins != "" {
                c.AllowedOrigins = strings.Split(origins, ",")
        }

        var errs []error
        maxMembers := get(constants.SESSION_MEMBERS_MAX, strconv.Itoa(constants.DefaultSessionMembersMax))
        if n, err := strconv.Atoi(maxMembers); err != nil || n < 1 {
                errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %q: must be a positive integer", constants.SESSION_MEMBERS_MAX, maxMembers))
        } else {
                c.SessionMembersMax = n
        }
        maxAge := get(constants.OWNER_ENTITLEMENTS_MAX_AGE, defaultOwnerEntitlementsMaxAge)
        if d, err := time.ParseDuration(maxAge); err != nil || d <= 0 {
                errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %q: must be a positive duration such as 168h", constants.OWNER_ENTITLEMENTS_MAX_AGE, maxAge))
        } else {
                c.OwnerEntitlementsMaxAge = d
        }
        for _, d := range []struct {
                key      string
                dst      *time.Duration
                def      time.Duration
                min, max time.Duration
        }{
                {constants.REQUEST_TIMEOUT, &c.RequestTimeout, constants.DefaultRequestTimeout, time.Second, time.Minute},
                {constants.STREAM_TICKET_TTL, &c.StreamTicketTTL, constants.DefaultStreamTicketTTL, time.Second, constants.MaxStreamTicketTTL},
                {constants.STREAM_MAX_AGE, &c.StreamMaxAge, constants.DefaultStreamMaxAge, time.Minute, 24 * time.Hour},
                {constants.STREAM_HEARTBEAT, &c.StreamHeartbeat, constants.DefaultStreamHeartbeat, time.Second, time.Minute},
                // How often a stream re-checks membership and credentials: with the check timeout it bounds
                // how long a revocation can go unseen (see server.StreamOptions.RevocationBound).
                {constants.STREAM_RECHECK_INTERVAL, &c.StreamRecheckInterval, constants.DefaultStreamRecheckInterval, time.Second, time.Minute},
        } {
                v := get(d.key, d.def.String())
                if n, err := time.ParseDuration(v); err != nil || n < d.min || n > d.max {
                        errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %q: must be a duration between %s and %s", d.key, v, d.min, d.max))
                } else {
                        *d.dst = n
                }
        }
        c.StreamRecheckInterval = min(c.StreamRecheckInterval, c.StreamHeartbeat) // a re-check slower than the heartbeat would not be the bound
        for _, d := range []struct {
                key      string
                dst      *int
                def      int
                min, max int
        }{
                {constants.STREAM_CONCURRENT_MAX, &c.StreamConcurrentMax, 10, 1, 100},
                {constants.STREAM_CREDENTIAL_MAX, &c.StreamCredentialMax, 3, 1, 20},
                {constants.STREAM_MAX_PER_POD, &c.StreamMaxPerPod, 1000, 1, 100000},
        } {
                v := get(d.key, strconv.Itoa(d.def))
                if n, err := strconv.Atoi(v); err != nil || n < d.min || n > d.max {
                        errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %q: must be an integer between %d and %d", d.key, v, d.min, d.max))
                } else {
                        *d.dst = n
                }
        }
        for _, d := range []struct {
                key      string
                dst      *time.Duration
                def      time.Duration
                min, max time.Duration
        }{
                {constants.STREAM_LEASE_TTL, &c.StreamLeaseTTL, constants.DefaultStreamLeaseTTL, 2 * time.Second, 10 * time.Minute},
                {constants.STREAM_GRACE, &c.StreamGrace, constants.DefaultStreamGrace, time.Second, 10 * time.Minute},
        } {
                v := get(d.key, d.def.String())
                if n, err := time.ParseDuration(v); err != nil || n < d.min || n > d.max {
                        errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %q: must be a duration between %s and %s", d.key, v, d.min, d.max))
                } else {
                        *d.dst = n
                }
        }
        // A seat is renewed on every heartbeat, so it must outlast at least one missed renewal.
        if c.StreamHeartbeat > 0 && c.StreamLeaseTTL > 0 && c.StreamLeaseTTL < 2*c.StreamHeartbeat {
                errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %s: must be at least twice %s (%s)", constants.STREAM_LEASE_TTL, c.StreamLeaseTTL, constants.STREAM_HEARTBEAT, c.StreamHeartbeat))
        }
        if origins := get(constants.STREAM_ALLOWED_ORIGINS, ""); origins != "" {
                for _, o := range strings.Split(origins, ",") {
                        if o = strings.TrimSpace(o); o == "*" {
                                errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s: wildcard origin is not allowed", constants.STREAM_ALLOWED_ORIGINS))
                        } else if matchesArbitraryHost(o) {
                                errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %q: pattern would match any host", constants.STREAM_ALLOWED_ORIGINS, o))
                        } else if _, err := path.Match(o, ""); err != nil {
                                errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %q: not a valid host pattern", constants.STREAM_ALLOWED_ORIGINS, o))
                        } else if o != "" {
                                c.StreamAllowedOrigins = append(c.StreamAllowedOrigins, o)
                        }
                }
        }
        maxDisc := get(constants.CREDENTIAL_EVENTS_MAX_DISCONNECT, "10m")
        if d, err := time.ParseDuration(maxDisc); err != nil || d < 30*time.Second || d > time.Hour {
                errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %q: must be a duration between 30s and 1h", constants.CREDENTIAL_EVENTS_MAX_DISCONNECT, maxDisc))
        } else {
                c.CredentialEventsMaxDisconnect = d
        }
        if _, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(c.BindAddress); err != nil {
                errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("BIND_ADDRESS %q: %w", c.BindAddress, err))
        }
        if !logLevels[c.LogLevel] {
                errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s %q: must be one of DEBUG, INFO, WARN, ERROR", constants.LOG_LEVEL, c.LogLevel))
        }
        errs = append(errs, checkURL(constants.OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT, c.OTLPEndpoint, false)...)
        errs = append(errs, checkURL(constants.AUTH_API_URL, c.AuthAPIURL, true)...)
        errs = append(errs, checkURL(constants.USERS_API_URL, c.UsersAPIURL, true)...)
        errs = append(errs, checkURL(constants.ENTITLEMENTS_API_URL, c.EntitlementsAPIURL, true)...)
        if c.RedisHost == "" {
                errs = append(errs, errors.New("REDIS_HOST: required"))
        }
        for _, o := range c.AllowedOrigins {
                if strings.TrimSpace(o) == "*" {
                        errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%s: wildcard origin is not allowed", constants.ALLOWED_ORIGINS))
                }
        }

        return c, errors.Join(errs...)
}

// FromEnv is Load against the process environment.
func FromEnv() (Config, error) { return Load(os.LookupEnv) }

// checkURL validates an absolute http(s) URL; an empty value is an error only when required.
func checkURL(key, value string, required bool) []error {
        if value == "" {
                if required {
                        return []error{fmt.Errorf("%s: required", key)}
                }
                return nil
        }
        if u, err := url.Parse(value); err != nil || (u.Scheme != "http" && u.Scheme != "https") || u.Host == "" {
                return []error{fmt.Errorf("%s %q: must be an absolute http(s) URL", key, value)}
        }
        return nil
}

// matchesArbitraryHost reports whether pattern matches hosts it does not name (such as "**" or
// "*?*"), which would defeat the ban on a wildcard origin.
func matchesArbitraryHost(pattern string) bool {
        for _, host := range []string{"a.b", "zz.example.test", "q"} {
                if ok, _ := path.Match(pattern, host); ok {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

package data

import (
        "fmt"
        "strings"
        "unicode/utf8"
)

// Size bounds on stored text and collections. Every write path checks them in the data layer, so no
// route can store an unbounded document; a request over a bound is a typed 422 (ErrInvalid).
const (
        MaxNameLen    = 200  // runes: roster, encounter, combatant and character names, theme_id
        MaxNotesLen   = 4000 // runes
        MaxCombatants = 200  // per encounter, removed ones included
        MaxCharacters = 200  // per roster
        MaxConditions = 20   // per combatant
        MaxMapEntries = 32   // health values and external refs per combatant or character
        MaxThemeIDLen = 64
)

// CheckText requires s to be at most max runes. what names the field in the error.
func CheckText(what, s string, max int) error {
        if utf8.RuneCountInString(s) > max {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s is longer than %d characters", ErrInvalid, what, max)
        }
        return nil
}

// CheckName requires a non-empty (after trimming) name of at most MaxNameLen runes.
func CheckName(what, s string) error {
        if strings.TrimSpace(s) == "" {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s is required", ErrInvalid, what)
        }
        return CheckText(what, s, MaxNameLen)
}

// CheckMaps bounds the health and external-ref maps of a combatant or character: at most
// MaxMapEntries entries each, and keys and values no longer than maxRefLen.
func CheckMaps(what string, health map[string]int, refs map[string]string) error {
        if len(health) > MaxMapEntries || len(refs) > MaxMapEntries {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s has more than %d health values or external references", ErrInvalid, what, MaxMapEntries)
        }
        for k := range health {
                if err := CheckText(what+" health key", k, maxRefLen); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        for k, v := range refs {
                if err := CheckText(what+" external reference key", k, maxRefLen); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                if err := CheckText(what+" external reference value", v, maxRefLen); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        return nil
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
)

// Change request statuses.
const (
        ChangePending  = "pending"
        ChangeApproved = "approved"
        ChangeRejected = "rejected"
        // ChangeApproving is an approval's claim: the request is taken but the encounter write has not
        // finished. Views show it as pending. It becomes approved when the write succeeds, goes back to
        // pending if the write fails, and can be resumed by a GM once the claim is older than
        // ClaimTimeout.
        ChangeApproving = "approving"
        // ChangeCancelled marks a pending request whose requester left the session or lost their
        // player role before it was decided.
        ChangeCancelled = "cancelled"
)

// ClaimTimeout is how long an approving claim is left alone before another approval may resume it.
const ClaimTimeout = 60 * time.Second

// MaxPendingChangeRequests bounds one player's open requests in a session, so a client cannot
// grow the collection without limit while the GM is away.
const MaxPendingChangeRequests = 50

// maxCancelSweeps bounds how many requests one session deletion cancels in a pass: far more
// members than a session may have (20 plus the GM) times each one's pending cap. A session over
// it fails the deletion, which a retry continues.
const maxCancelSweeps = 100 * MaxPendingChangeRequests

// ChangeRequest is a player's proposed change to one combatant's health values or conditions
// (initiative-player-access). It changes nothing until a GM or co-GM approves it. Health holds the
// values to set (merged into the combatant's health, like a combatant patch) and is absolute, so
// applying it twice is harmless. Conditions are a delta by name, applied to the combatant as it
// is when the request is applied: RemoveConditions drops conditions by name, AddConditions sets
// (adds, or replaces the rounds of) a condition by name. A condition added by the GM after the
// request was submitted therefore survives approval.
type ChangeRequest struct {
        ID               string         `bson:"_id" json:"id"`
        SessionID        string         `bson:"session_id" json:"session_id"`
        EncounterID      string         `bson:"encounter_id" json:"encounter_id"`
        CombatantID      string         `bson:"combatant_id" json:"combatant_id"`
        RequestedBy      string         `bson:"requested_by" json:"requested_by"`
        Health           map[string]int `bson:"health,omitempty" json:"health,omitempty"`
        AddConditions    []Condition    `bson:"add_conditions,omitempty" json:"add_conditions,omitempty"`
        RemoveConditions []string       `bson:"remove_conditions,omitempty" json:"remove_conditions,omitempty"`
        Status           string         `bson:"status" json:"status"`
        // BaseRevision is the encounter revision the player saw when submitting.
        BaseRevision int64      `bson:"base_revision" json:"base_revision"`
        CreatedAt    time.Time  `bson:"created_at" json:"created_at"`
        DecidedBy    string     `bson:"decided_by,omitempty" json:"decided_by,omitempty"`
        DecidedAt    *time.Time `bson:"decided_at,omitempty" json:"decided_at,omitempty"`
        // Reason is why a rejected request was rejected.
        Reason string `bson:"reason,omitempty" json:"reason,omitempty"`
        // ClaimedAt is when an approval took the request (status approving).
        ClaimedAt *time.Time `bson:"claimed_at,omitempty" json:"-"`
        // AppliedRevision is the encounter revision an approved request produced.
        AppliedRevision int64 `bson:"applied_revision,omitempty" json:"applied_revision,omitempty"`
}

// MarshalJSON shows an approving request as pending: the claim is an internal step of an approval,
// and until it completes the change has not been applied.
func (r ChangeRequest) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {
        type plain ChangeRequest
        if r.Status == ChangeApproving {
                r.Status, r.DecidedBy = ChangePending, ""
        }
        return json.Marshal(plain(r))
}

// ChangeRequests is the repository for change requests. Every function takes the Viewer or
// Decision that proves the caller's standing in the session (PADR-0035) and scopes by it.
type ChangeRequests struct {
        c   store.Collection
        now func() time.Time
}

// NewChangeRequests builds the repository on db.
func NewChangeRequests(db store.Database) *ChangeRequests {
        return &ChangeRequests{c: db.Collection(CollectionChangeRequests), now: func() time.Time { return time.Now().UTC() }}
}

// Create stores req as the viewer's pending request. Only a player Viewer may: an observer has no
// change requests, and a GM edits directly. The session, requester, status and time are set here.
func (r *ChangeRequests) Create(ctx context.Context, v Viewer, req ChangeRequest) (ChangeRequest, error) {
        if v.level != ViewPlayer || v.session == "" || checkUserID(v.actor) != nil {
                return ChangeRequest{}, ErrForbidden
        }
        n, err := r.c.CountDocuments(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: v.session}, {Key: "requested_by", Value: v.actor}, {Key: "status", Value: ChangePending}})
        if err != nil {
                return ChangeRequest{}, err
        }
        // Count-then-insert: concurrent submissions can overrun the cap by at most the number of
        // requests in flight at once, which one player's connection count bounds.
        if n >= MaxPendingChangeRequests {
                return ChangeRequest{}, invalid("you have %d change requests waiting for the GM; wait for some to be decided", n)
        }
        req.ID, req.SessionID, req.RequestedBy = newID(), v.session, v.actor
        req.Status, req.CreatedAt = ChangePending, r.now()
        return req, r.c.InsertOne(ctx, req)
}

// List returns one page of the encounter's change requests, oldest first. A GM or co-GM sees all
// of them; a player only their own; an observer none.
func (r *ChangeRequests) List(ctx context.Context, v Viewer, encounterID, status string, q Query) (Page[ChangeRequest], error) {
        encounterID, ok := cleanID(encounterID)
        if !ok {
                return Page[ChangeRequest]{}, ErrNotFound
        }
        base := bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: v.session}, {Key: "encounter_id", Value: encounterID}}
        switch v.level {
        case ViewGM:
        case ViewPlayer:
                base = append(base, bson.E{Key: "requested_by", Value: v.actor})
        default:
                return Page[ChangeRequest]{}, ErrForbidden
        }
        if v.session == "" {
                return Page[ChangeRequest]{}, ErrForbidden
        }
        q = byCreated(q)
        switch status {
        case "":
        case ChangePending:
                q.Filters = append(q.Filters, Filter{Field: "status", Op: OpIn, Values: []any{ChangePending, ChangeApproving}})
        default:
                q.Filters = append(q.Filters, Filter{Field: "status", Op: OpEq, Values: []any{status}})
        }
        return runQuery[ChangeRequest](ctx, r.c, ChangeRequestSpec, base, q)
}

// Load reads one change request of the decision's session and encounter. d must grant can_edit.
func (r *ChangeRequests) Load(ctx context.Context, d Decision, encounterID, id string) (ChangeRequest, error) {
        var req ChangeRequest
        if err := d.require(relEdit); err != nil {
                return req, err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        encounterID, eok := cleanID(encounterID)
        if !ok || !eok {
                return req, ErrNotFound
        }
        err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "encounter_id", Value: encounterID}}, &req)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return req, ErrNotFound
        }
        return req, err
}

// Reject settles a pending request as rejected with reason, in one compare-and-set on status, so
// it cannot win against an approval that has claimed the request. A request in any other state is
// ErrConflict.
func (r *ChangeRequests) Reject(ctx context.Context, d Decision, encounterID, id, reason string) (ChangeRequest, error) {
        return r.settle(ctx, d, encounterID, id, bson.E{Key: "status", Value: ChangePending},
                bson.D{{Key: "status", Value: ChangeRejected}, {Key: "decided_by", Value: d.actor}, {Key: "decided_at", Value: r.now()}, {Key: "reason", Value: reason}})
}

// Claim takes a request for approval: pending becomes approving (claimed_at, decided_by), and an
// approving request whose claim is older than ClaimTimeout is taken over, so an approval that
// crashed can be resumed. A younger claim, and any decided request, is ErrConflict.
func (r *ChangeRequests) Claim(ctx context.Context, d Decision, encounterID, id string) (ChangeRequest, error) {
        now := r.now()
        open := bson.E{Key: "$or", Value: bson.A{
                bson.D{{Key: "status", Value: ChangePending}},
                bson.D{{Key: "status", Value: ChangeApproving}, {Key: "claimed_at", Value: bson.D{{Key: "$lte", Value: now.Add(-ClaimTimeout)}}}},
        }}
        return r.settle(ctx, d, encounterID, id, open,
                bson.D{{Key: "status", Value: ChangeApproving}, {Key: "decided_by", Value: d.actor}, {Key: "claimed_at", Value: now}})
}

// Finish records that the claimed approval's encounter write succeeded at revision: approving
// becomes approved. It only matches the claim d took; a request taken over since is ErrConflict
// (the change itself is idempotent, so the takeover's write does no harm).
func (r *ChangeRequests) Finish(ctx context.Context, d Decision, encounterID, id string, revision int64) (ChangeRequest, error) {
        return r.settle(ctx, d, encounterID, id, bson.E{Key: "$and", Value: bson.A{
                bson.D{{Key: "status", Value: ChangeApproving}}, bson.D{{Key: "decided_by", Value: d.actor}}}},
                bson.D{{Key: "status", Value: ChangeApproved}, {Key: "decided_at", Value: r.now()}, {Key: "applied_revision", Value: revision}})
}

// Unclaim puts a request this decision claimed back to pending, for an approval whose encounter
// write failed.
func (r *ChangeRequests) Unclaim(ctx context.Context, d Decision, encounterID, id string) error {
        if err := d.require(relEdit); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        encounterID, eok := cleanID(encounterID)
        if !ok || !eok {
                return ErrNotFound
        }
        var discard ChangeRequest
        err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx,
                bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "encounter_id", Value: encounterID},
                        {Key: "status", Value: ChangeApproving}, {Key: "decided_by", Value: d.actor}},
                bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "status", Value: ChangePending}}},
                        {Key: "$unset", Value: bson.D{{Key: "decided_by", Value: ""}, {Key: "claimed_at", Value: ""}}}}, &discard)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return ErrNotFound
        }
        return err
}

// settle applies set to the request if it is in the state open describes, in one atomic write. When
// nothing matched, a request that exists is ErrConflict and one that does not is ErrNotFound.
func (r *ChangeRequests) settle(ctx context.Context, d Decision, encounterID, id string, open bson.E, set bson.D) (ChangeRequest, error) {
        var req ChangeRequest
        if err := d.require(relEdit); err != nil {
                return req, err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        encounterID, eok := cleanID(encounterID)
        if !ok || !eok {
                return req, ErrNotFound
        }
        filter := bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "encounter_id", Value: encounterID}}
        err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, append(filter, open), bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: set}}, &req)
        if !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return req, err
        }
        if ferr := r.c.FindOne(ctx, filter, &req); ferr == nil {
                return req, ErrConflict
        }
        return req, ErrNotFound
}

// CancelSession cancels every request still waiting (pending or approving) in the session, for a
// session that is being deleted. d must be the GM's. Idempotent; it fails rather than stop silently
// if more than maxCancelSweeps requests are waiting.
func (r *ChangeRequests) CancelSession(ctx context.Context, d Decision) error {
        if err := d.require(relGM); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        for range maxCancelSweeps {
                var discard ChangeRequest
                err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx,
                        bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "status", Value: bson.D{{Key: "$in", Value: bson.A{ChangePending, ChangeApproving}}}}},
                        bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "status", Value: ChangeCancelled}, {Key: "decided_by", Value: d.actor}, {Key: "decided_at", Value: r.now()}}}}, &discard)
                if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                        return nil
                }
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        return fmt.Errorf("%w: more than %d change requests waiting in one session; retry delete", ErrInvalid, maxCancelSweeps)
}

// CancelPending cancels userID's pending and approving requests in the session, for a member who left, was
// removed, or lost the player role. d grants can_edit, or can_view for one's own leave. The
// per-player cap bounds the loop.
func (r *ChangeRequests) CancelPending(ctx context.Context, d Decision, userID string) error {
        need := relEdit
        if userID == d.actor {
                need = relView
        }
        if err := pairOK(d, need, userID); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        for range MaxPendingChangeRequests + 1 {
                var discard ChangeRequest
                err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx,
                        bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "requested_by", Value: userID},
                                {Key: "status", Value: bson.D{{Key: "$in", Value: bson.A{ChangePending, ChangeApproving}}}}},
                        bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "status", Value: ChangeCancelled}, {Key: "decided_by", Value: d.actor}, {Key: "decided_at", Value: r.now()}}}}, &discard)
                if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                        return nil
                }
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        return nil
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "log/slog"
        "regexp"
        "time"
)

// Event types an encounter commit can produce. The default for a write whose caller named none is
// EventUpdated, which carries no ids, so an unannotated write is safe: subscribers refetch.
const (
        EventCreated           = "encounter.created"
        EventUpdated           = "encounter.updated"
        EventDeleted           = "encounter.deleted"
        EventReleased          = "encounter.released"
        EventStarted           = "encounter.started"
        EventEnded             = "encounter.ended"
        EventReset             = "encounter.reset"
        EventTurn              = "turn.advanced"
        EventCombatantAdded    = "combatant.added"
        EventCombatantUpdated  = "combatant.updated"
        EventCombatantRemoved  = "combatant.removed"
        EventCombatantRestored = "combatant.restored"
        EventControllers       = "controllers.updated"
)

// Origin says who made a change, so a client can recognise the echo of its own write. The actor
// is the originating user (never serialized); ClientID is the opaque value the client sent in
// X-Initiative-Client-Id, already validated.
type Origin struct {
        Kind     string // user, integration, or system
        ClientID string
        ActorID  string
}

// Origin kinds.
const (
        OriginUser        = "user"
        OriginIntegration = "integration"
        OriginSystem      = "system"
)

var clientIDPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,64}$`)

// ValidClientID reports whether v may be recorded as an origin client id. A bad value is ignored,
// never rejected.
func ValidClientID(v string) bool { return clientIDPattern.MatchString(v) }

type opKey struct{}
type originKey struct{}

type op struct {
        typ string
        ids []string
}

// WithOp names the event type the next encounter commit on ctx produces, and the combatants it
// changed. Domain entry points set it; a commit with none is EventUpdated with no ids.
func WithOp(ctx context.Context, eventType string, combatantIDs ...string) context.Context {
        return context.WithValue(ctx, opKey{}, op{eventType, combatantIDs})
}

// WithOrigin records who is making the writes on ctx.
func WithOrigin(ctx context.Context, o Origin) context.Context {
        return context.WithValue(ctx, originKey{}, o)
}

func opOf(ctx context.Context) op {
        o, _ := ctx.Value(opKey{}).(op)
        if o.typ == "" {
                o.typ = EventUpdated
        }
        return o
}

func originOf(ctx context.Context) Origin {
        o, _ := ctx.Value(originKey{}).(Origin)
        if o.Kind == "" {
                o.Kind = OriginSystem
        }
        return o
}

// Change is one committed encounter write, handed to the Sink right after the commit. After is the
// stored document as the write left it, so a sink can render it for each subscriber without a
// read; SessionID is the session the encounter belonged to before the write (it differs from
// After.SessionID only for a release), so the audience of a release or delete is the session's.
// OwnerID is the encounter's owner after the write.
type Change struct {
        Type  string
        After Encounter
        // Before is the document as it was just before the write, when the write path had it at the
        // revision the write was made against; nil when unknown. A subscriber's view is compared before
        // and after, so a change it cannot see produces no event for it.
        Before      *Encounter
        SessionID   string
        OwnerID     string
        CombatantID []string
        // GMOnly marks a change only GM-level viewers may hear of: a limit or entitlement flag (lock,
        // inactive) changed. It never reaches a player or observer, and never the bus.
        GMOnly bool
        Origin Origin
        At     time.Time
}

// Sink receives every committed encounter change after the write has succeeded. Emit must not
// block. Changes made by concurrent writers can reach the sink out of commit order, so clients
// order what they receive by the revision (GM and co-GM) or the view_tag (everyone else) in each
// event, not by arrival.
type Sink interface {
        Emit(ctx context.Context, c Change)
}

// SetSink installs the sink for every encounter commit made through r. It is set once at
// startup; with none, commits emit nothing.
func (r *Encounters) SetSink(s Sink) {
        r.sink = s
        r.commit = func(ctx context.Context, kind commitKind, before, after *Encounter) {
                r.emitCommit(ctx, kind, before, *after)
        }
}

type commitKind int

const (
        commitCreate commitKind = iota
        commitWrite
        commitDelete
        commitFlag
)

func (r *Encounters) emitCommit(ctx context.Context, kind commitKind, before *Encounter, after Encounter) {
        if r.sink == nil {
                return
        }
        c := Change{After: after, Before: before, SessionID: after.SessionID, OwnerID: after.OwnerUserID, Origin: originOf(ctx), At: r.now()}
        switch kind {
        case commitCreate:
                c.Type = EventCreated
        case commitDelete:
                c.Type = EventDeleted
        case commitFlag:
                c.Type, c.GMOnly = EventUpdated, true
        default:
                o := opOf(ctx)
                c.Type, c.CombatantID = o.typ, o.ids
        }
        r.deliver(ctx, c)
}

// deliver hands c to the sink. A sink that panics must never fail a write that already
// committed, so the panic is recovered and logged.
func (r *Encounters) deliver(ctx context.Context, c Change) {
        defer func() {
                if p := recover(); p != nil {
                        slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "an encounter event sink panicked", "encounter", c.After.ID, "type", c.Type, "panic", p)
                }
        }()
        r.sink.Emit(ctx, c)
}

// emit sends a change the caller built itself (scrub, release, retract), for the cases the
// generic commit hook does not cover.
func (r *Encounters) emit(ctx context.Context, c Change) {
        if r.sink == nil {
                return
        }
        c.Origin, c.At = originOf(ctx), r.now()
        r.deliver(ctx, c)
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "regexp"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
)

// CredentialDeletion is the append-only record of an administrator's permanent deletion of an
// integration credential. Its _id is the deleted credential's id, so a retried deletion cannot
// write a second entry. Nothing in the service updates or removes these (PADR-0027).
type CredentialDeletion struct {
        CredentialID string    `bson:"_id" json:"credential_id"`
        OwnerUserID  string    `bson:"owner_user_id" json:"owner_user_id"`
        Label        string    `bson:"label" json:"label"`
        Scopes       []string  `bson:"scopes" json:"scopes"`
        Reason       string    `bson:"reason" json:"reason"`
        CreatedBy    string    `bson:"created_by" json:"deleted_by"`
        CreatedAt    time.Time `bson:"created_at" json:"deleted_at"`
}

// ownerPattern is the shape of a user id (Auth0 subject or users-api id). An owner filter taken
// from a request must match it, so only a plain id can reach a query.
var ownerPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9_.|:-]{1,128}$`)

func checkOwner(owner string) error {
        if owner != "" && !ownerPattern.MatchString(owner) {
                return invalid("owner is not a valid user id")
        }
        return nil
}

// ListAll returns one page of credentials across owners, newest first. Administrator use only:
// callers gate it on the admin role. Its filter has no request input; one owner's credentials go
// through the owner-scoped List instead.
func (r *Credentials) ListAll(ctx context.Context, q Query) (Page[IntegrationCredential], error) {
        if len(q.Sort) == 0 {
                q.Sort = []SortKey{{Field: "created_at", Desc: true}}
        }
        return runQuery[IntegrationCredential](ctx, r.c, r.spec, bson.D{{Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}}, q)
}

// ListOwned is the owner-scoped List for an owner id taken from an administrator's request.
func (r *Credentials) ListOwned(ctx context.Context, owner string, q Query) (Page[IntegrationCredential], error) {
        if err := checkOwner(owner); err != nil {
                return Page[IntegrationCredential]{}, err
        }
        if len(q.Sort) == 0 {
                q.Sort = []SortKey{{Field: "created_at", Desc: true}}
        }
        return r.List(ctx, owner, q)
}

// Live reports whether the credential can still authenticate: present, unrevoked, not over the
// owner's limit. Open streams re-check it so a revoke or deletion ends them.
func (r *Credentials) Live(ctx context.Context, id string) (bool, error) {
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return false, nil
        }
        var c IntegrationCredential
        filter := bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}, {Key: "revoked_at", Value: nil}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}, {Key: "inactive_at", Value: nil}}
        err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, filter, &c)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return false, nil
        }
        return err == nil, err
}

// DeletePermanently removes any owner's credential and records who did it and why. The audit
// entry is written first, so a failure after it leaves the credential in place and a retry
// completes the deletion without a second entry. Returns the entry.
func (r *Credentials) DeletePermanently(ctx context.Context, actor, id, reason string) (CredentialDeletion, error) {
        var out CredentialDeletion
        if strings.TrimSpace(actor) == "" || strings.TrimSpace(reason) == "" {
                return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: administrator and reason are required", ErrInvalid)
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return out, ErrNotFound
        }
        var c IntegrationCredential
        err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}}, &c)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return out, ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return out, err
        }
        out = CredentialDeletion{
                CredentialID: c.ID, OwnerUserID: c.OwnerUserID, Label: c.Label, Scopes: c.Scopes,
                Reason: strings.TrimSpace(reason), CreatedBy: actor, CreatedAt: r.now(),
        }
        // A duplicate means an earlier attempt wrote the entry: return that stored row, so the
        // response and log match what the audit collection holds.
        if err := r.deletions.InsertOne(ctx, out); errors.Is(err, store.ErrDuplicate) {
                if err := r.deletions.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}}, &out); err != nil {
                        return CredentialDeletion{}, err
                }
        } else if err != nil {
                return CredentialDeletion{}, err
        }
        if err := r.c.DeleteOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}}); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return CredentialDeletion{}, err
        }
        return out, nil
}

// DeletionSpec pages the audit entries, newest first.
var DeletionSpec = Spec{
        Name:     CollectionCredentialDeletions,
        Sortable: []string{"created_at"},
}

// Deletions returns one page of audit entries, newest first; a non-empty NextCursor means more.
// owner narrows it to one owner's deleted credentials.
func (r *Credentials) Deletions(ctx context.Context, owner string, q Query) (Page[CredentialDeletion], error) {
        if err := checkOwner(owner); err != nil {
                return Page[CredentialDeletion]{}, err
        }
        if len(q.Sort) == 0 {
                q.Sort = []SortKey{{Field: "created_at", Desc: true}}
        }
        if owner == "" {
                return runQuery[CredentialDeletion](ctx, r.deletions, DeletionSpec, bson.D{}, q)
        }
        return runQuery[CredentialDeletion](ctx, r.deletions, DeletionSpec, bson.D{{Key: "owner_user_id", Value: owner}}, q)
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "strings"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
)

// Credentials is the repository for integration credentials: the owner-scoped operations come
// from Owned, plus the state changes (revoke, use) and the one lookup that is not owner-scoped.
type Credentials struct {
        *Owned[IntegrationCredential, *IntegrationCredential]
        gate, deletions store.Collection
}

// NewCredentials builds the credential repository for db.
func NewCredentials(db store.Database) *Credentials {
        return &Credentials{NewOwned[IntegrationCredential](db, CredentialSpec), db.Collection(CollectionCredentialGate), db.Collection(CollectionCredentialDeletions)}
}

// activeScope is owner's credentials that are neither revoked nor deleted.
func activeScope(owner string) bson.D {
        return append(scope(owner), bson.E{Key: "revoked_at", Value: nil})
}

// ItemStates lists owner's unrevoked credentials; a revoked one no longer counts toward the limit.
func (r *Credentials) ItemStates(ctx context.Context, owner string) ([]ItemState, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(owner); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return r.itemStates(ctx, activeScope(owner))
}

// CountActive counts owner's credentials that can still authenticate.
func (r *Credentials) CountActive(ctx context.Context, owner string) (int64, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(owner); err != nil {
                return 0, err
        }
        return r.c.CountDocuments(ctx, activeScope(owner))
}

// Revoke ends owner's credential at once. Revoking an already revoked credential succeeds and
// returns it unchanged. Another owner's credential is ErrForbidden, an unknown one ErrNotFound.
func (r *Credentials) Revoke(ctx context.Context, owner, actor, id string) (IntegrationCredential, error) {
        var out IntegrationCredential
        if err := requireOwnerActor(owner, actor); err != nil {
                return out, err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return out, ErrNotFound
        }
        now := r.now()
        update := bson.D{
                {Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revoked_at", Value: now}, {Key: "updated_at", Value: now}, {Key: "updated_by", Value: actor}}},
                {Key: "$inc", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revision", Value: 1}}},
        }
        filter := append(scopeID(owner, id), bson.E{Key: "revoked_at", Value: nil})
        err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &out)
        if !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return out, err
        }
        // Nothing matched: already revoked (fine), someone else's, or missing.
        cur, gerr := r.Get(ctx, owner, id)
        if gerr == nil {
                return cur, nil
        }
        if errors.Is(gerr, ErrNotFound) {
                _, cerr := r.classify(ctx, owner, id)
                if cerr != nil && !errors.Is(cerr, ErrRevisionConflict) {
                        return out, cerr
                }
        }
        return out, gerr
}

// Authenticate resolves a presented secret's hash to its live, unrevoked credential and stamps
// last_used_at in the same write, so a revocation is seen by the very next request. This is the
// one lookup that is not owner-scoped: the secret is what identifies the owner. secretHash must
// be the hash the service computed, never a client-supplied value.
func (r *Credentials) Authenticate(ctx context.Context, secretHash string) (IntegrationCredential, error) {
        var out IntegrationCredential
        if secretHash == "" {
                return out, ErrNotFound
        }
        filter := bson.D{{Key: "secret_hash", Value: secretHash}, {Key: "revoked_at", Value: nil}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}, {Key: "inactive_at", Value: nil}}
        update := bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "last_used_at", Value: r.now()}}}}
        err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &out)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return out, ErrNotFound
        }
        return out, err
}

// Revoke reasons the platform sets (PADR-0039).
const (
        ReasonOwnerDenied      = "owner_denied"
        ReasonOwnerRoleRemoved = "owner_role_removed"
)

// RevokeAllByOwner ends every active credential of owner, recording reason, and returns how many
// it ended. Each pass takes one credential, so a retry after a failure finishes the rest. This is
// the auth-event path, so it sets updated_by to the reason rather than a user.
func (r *Credentials) RevokeAllByOwner(ctx context.Context, owner, reason string) (int, error) {
        if strings.TrimSpace(owner) == "" || strings.TrimSpace(reason) == "" {
                return 0, fmt.Errorf("%w: owner and reason are required", ErrInvalid)
        }
        n := 0
        for {
                now := r.now()
                update := bson.D{
                        {Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revoked_at", Value: now}, {Key: "revoke_reason", Value: reason}, {Key: "updated_at", Value: now}, {Key: "updated_by", Value: reason}}},
                        {Key: "$inc", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revision", Value: 1}}},
                }
                var out IntegrationCredential
                err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, activeScope(owner), update, &out)
                if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                        return n, nil
                }
                if err != nil {
                        return n, err
                }
                n++
        }
}

const gateID = "gate"

// GateClosed reports whether the persisted gate is closed. No document means open.
func (r *Credentials) GateClosed(ctx context.Context) (bool, error) {
        var doc struct {
                Closed bool `bson:"closed"`
        }
        err := r.gate.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: gateID}}, &doc)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return false, nil
        }
        return doc.Closed, err
}

// SetGate persists the gate state so a restart cannot reopen it before a reconcile.
func (r *Credentials) SetGate(ctx context.Context, closed bool, actor string) error {
        col := r.gate
        filter := bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: gateID}}
        update := bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "closed", Value: closed}, {Key: "updated_at", Value: r.now()}, {Key: "updated_by", Value: actor}}}}
        var out bson.M
        err := col.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &out)
        if !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return err
        }
        err = col.InsertOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: gateID}, {Key: "closed", Value: closed}, {Key: "updated_at", Value: r.now()}, {Key: "updated_by", Value: actor}})
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrDuplicate) {
                return col.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &out)
        }
        return err
}

package data

import (
        "context"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/relations"
)

// Decision is a granted authorization for one user on one session (PADR-0035). Every session data
// function takes one and has no unchecked form: the only way to get a granted Decision is
// Authorize, which asks auth-api (OpenFGA) with the caller's own bearer token. The zero value
// grants nothing.
type Decision struct {
        actor, session, relation string
}

// Actor returns the user the decision was made for.
func (d Decision) Actor() string { return d.actor }

// SessionID returns the session the decision covers.
func (d Decision) SessionID() string { return d.session }

// Relations on a session, weakest first. The model (auth-api fga/model.fga) makes can_edit and
// can_approve the same set (gm or co_gm), gm a subset of that, and can_view everyone.
const (
        relView    = "can_view"
        relEdit    = "can_edit"
        relApprove = "can_approve"
        relGM      = "gm"
)

var relationRank = map[string]int{relView: 1, relEdit: 2, relApprove: 2, relGM: 3}

// require refuses the zero Decision and any decision made for a weaker relation than min, so a
// can_view grant (any observer) cannot be spent on a write that needs can_edit.
func (d Decision) require(min string) error {
        if d.actor == "" || d.session == "" || relationRank[d.relation] == 0 || relationRank[d.relation] < relationRank[min] {
                return ErrForbidden
        }
        return nil
}

// RelationChecker answers whether the holder of token has relation on object.
// *relations.Client implements it.
type RelationChecker interface {
        Check(ctx context.Context, user, token, object, relation string) (bool, error)
}

type gate struct {
        checker RelationChecker
        token   string
}

type gateKey struct{}

// WithGate puts the checker and the caller's bearer token on ctx for Authorize. Per PADR-0037 the
// token is the caller's own; there is no service token.
func WithGate(ctx context.Context, c RelationChecker, token string) context.Context {
        return context.WithValue(ctx, gateKey{}, gate{c, token})
}

// Authorize asks whether actor holds relation on the session. A malformed session id is
// ErrNotFound; a denial is ErrForbidden and carries no session data. When no decision can be
// obtained (no gate on ctx, auth-api or OpenFGA down) the error is relations.ErrUnavailable, or
// relations.ErrUnauthorized for a rejected token: session data fails closed.
func Authorize(ctx context.Context, actor, sessionID, relation string) (Decision, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(actor); err != nil {
                return Decision{}, err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(sessionID)
        if !ok {
                return Decision{}, ErrNotFound
        }
        g, ok := ctx.Value(gateKey{}).(gate)
        if !ok || g.checker == nil {
                return Decision{}, relations.ErrUnavailable
        }
        allowed, err := g.checker.Check(ctx, actor, g.token, "session:"+id, relation)
        if err != nil {
                return Decision{}, err
        }
        if !allowed {
                return Decision{}, ErrForbidden
        }
        return Decision{actor: actor, session: id, relation: relation}, nil
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "encoding/hex"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "maps"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
)

var (
        orderings   = []string{OrderHighToLow, OrderLowToHigh, OrderPCVsAdversary, OrderPlayerManaged}
        tieBreakers = []string{TieRandom, TieAlphabetical, TieQuery}
        statuses    = []string{StatusDraft, StatusActive, StatusEnded}
)

// Encounters is the owner-scoped encounter repository. It validates enumerated values and
// queue references before any write reaches the collection.
type Encounters struct {
        *Owned[Encounter, *Encounter]
        systems *SystemRepo
        sink    Sink
}

// NewEncounters builds the encounter repository for db.
func NewEncounters(db store.Database) *Encounters {
        o := NewOwned[Encounter](db, EncounterSpec)
        o.extra = bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: nil}}
        o.exclude = func(e *Encounter) bool { return e.SessionID != "" }
        return &Encounters{Owned: o, systems: NewSystemRepo(db)}
}

// ValidateEncounter checks a whole encounter document: ordering, tie breaker, and status come
// from their constants, and every turn_queue entry names one of the encounter's combatants.
func ValidateEncounter(e *Encounter) error {
        if err := CheckText("encounter name", e.Name, MaxNameLen); err != nil { // required-ness is the domain's rule
                return err
        }
        if err := CheckText("theme_id", e.ThemeID, MaxThemeIDLen); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if e.Position < 0 {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: position %d", ErrInvalid, e.Position)
        }
        if !slices.Contains(orderings, e.Ordering) {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: ordering %q", ErrInvalid, e.Ordering)
        }
        if !slices.Contains(tieBreakers, e.TieBreaker) {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: tie_breaker %q", ErrInvalid, e.TieBreaker)
        }
        return ValidateState(EncounterState{Status: e.Status, Round: e.Round,
                CurrentCombatantID: e.CurrentCombatantID, TurnQueue: e.TurnQueue, Combatants: e.Combatants})
}

// EncounterState is the live combat state of an encounter, written as one unit.
type EncounterState struct {
        Status             string
        Round              int
        CurrentCombatantID string
        TurnQueue          []string
        Combatants         []Combatant
}

// ValidateState checks status, a non-negative round, unique combatant IDs, and that the queue and
// the current combatant refer to combatants of this state.
func ValidateState(st EncounterState) error {
        if !slices.Contains(statuses, st.Status) {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: status %q", ErrInvalid, st.Status)
        }
        if st.Round < 0 {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: round %d", ErrInvalid, st.Round)
        }
        if len(st.Combatants) > MaxCombatants {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: an encounter holds at most %d combatants", ErrInvalid, MaxCombatants)
        }
        ids := make(map[string]bool, len(st.Combatants))
        for _, c := range st.Combatants {
                if err := validateCombatant(c); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                if ids[c.ID] {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: duplicate combatant id %q", ErrInvalid, c.ID)
                }
                ids[c.ID] = true
        }
        if st.CurrentCombatantID != "" && !ids[st.CurrentCombatantID] {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: current combatant %q is not a combatant", ErrInvalid, st.CurrentCombatantID)
        }
        for _, id := range st.TurnQueue {
                if !ids[id] {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: turn_queue entry %q is not a combatant", ErrInvalid, id)
                }
        }
        return nil
}

func validateEncounterSet(set map[string]any) error {
        if v, ok := set["name"]; ok {
                s, isStr := v.(string)
                if !isStr {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: name must be a string", ErrInvalid)
                }
                if err := CheckName("encounter name", s); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        if v, ok := set["theme_id"]; ok {
                s, isStr := v.(string)
                if !isStr {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: theme_id must be a string", ErrInvalid)
                }
                if err := CheckText("theme_id", s, MaxThemeIDLen); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        if v, ok := set["position"]; ok {
                if n, isInt := v.(int); !isInt || n < 0 {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: position must be a non-negative integer", ErrInvalid)
                }
        }
        if v, ok := set["game_system_id"]; ok {
                if s, isStr := v.(string); !isStr || strings.TrimSpace(s) == "" {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: game_system_id must be a non-empty string", ErrInvalid)
                }
        }
        if v, ok := set["ordering"]; ok {
                if s, isStr := v.(string); !isStr || !slices.Contains(orderings, s) {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: ordering %v", ErrInvalid, v)
                }
        }
        if v, ok := set["tie_breaker"]; ok {
                if s, isStr := v.(string); !isStr || !slices.Contains(tieBreakers, s) {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: tie_breaker %v", ErrInvalid, v)
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// Create resets e's combat state to a fresh draft, validates it, then stores it as owner's new
// encounter. Callers cannot create an encounter that is already running.
func (r *Encounters) Create(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID string, e *Encounter) error {
        e.Status, e.Round, e.CurrentCombatantID, e.TurnQueue = StatusDraft, 0, "", nil
        if err := ValidateEncounter(e); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if err := r.checkHealth(ctx, e.GameSystemID, e.Combatants, nil); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        e.RefKeys = refKeys(e.Combatants)
        return r.Owned.Create(ctx, ownerUserID, actingUserID, e)
}

// CountAll returns how many live encounters owner holds, private and in a session alike. Both
// count against the saved-encounters limit.
func (r *Encounters) CountAll(ctx context.Context, owner string) (int64, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(owner); err != nil {
                return 0, err
        }
        return r.c.CountDocuments(ctx, scope(owner))
}

// Update validates the enumerated values in set, then applies it with the revision check. A change
// of game system also checks every combatant's health against the new system.
func (r *Encounters) Update(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id string, expectedRevision int64, set map[string]any) (Encounter, error) {
        if err := validateEncounterSet(set); err != nil {
                return Encounter{}, err
        }
        if sysID, ok := set["game_system_id"].(string); ok {
                if err := r.requireSystem(ctx, sysID); err != nil {
                        return Encounter{}, err
                }
                // Every combatant's health must fit the new system before the switch is stored.
                cur, err := r.Load(ctx, ownerUserID, id)
                if err != nil {
                        return Encounter{}, err
                }
                if err := r.checkHealth(ctx, sysID, cur.Combatants, nil); err != nil {
                        return Encounter{}, err
                }
        }
        return r.Owned.Update(ctx, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id, expectedRevision, set)
}

func validateCombatant(c Combatant) error {
        if strings.TrimSpace(c.Name) == "" {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: combatant %q has no name", ErrInvalid, c.ID)
        }
        if err := errors.Join(CheckText("combatant name", c.Name, MaxNameLen), CheckText("combatant notes", c.Notes, MaxNotesLen),
