                CheckMaps("combatant", c.Health, c.ExternalRefs)); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if len(c.Conditions) > MaxConditions || len(c.ControllerUserIDs) > MaxCombatants {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: combatant %q has too many conditions or controllers", ErrInvalid, c.ID)
        }
        if !slices.Contains(Kinds, c.Kind) {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: combatant %q kind %q", ErrInvalid, c.ID, c.Kind)
        }
        if !slices.Contains([]string{"", HealthFull, HealthStatus, HealthHidden}, c.HealthVisibility) {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: combatant %q health visibility %q", ErrInvalid, c.ID, c.HealthVisibility)
        }
        for _, cond := range c.Conditions {
                if CheckText("condition name", cond.Name, MaxNameLen) != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: combatant %q has a condition name over %d characters", ErrInvalid, c.ID, MaxNameLen)
                }
                if strings.TrimSpace(cond.Name) == "" || (cond.RoundsRemaining != nil && *cond.RoundsRemaining < 1) {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: combatant %q has an invalid condition", ErrInvalid, c.ID)
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// requireSystem rejects a game system that has no definition. The No System fallback applies only
// to an encounter whose existing system lost its definition, never to choosing one: creating an
// encounter and switching its system both require the definition to exist.
func (r *Encounters) requireSystem(ctx context.Context, sysID string) error {
        _, err := r.systems.Get(ctx, sysID)
        if errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
                return invalid("the game system is not defined")
        }
        return err
}

// checkHealth validates the health of each combatant in cs against the encounter's game system
// definition (the default system when the encounter's has none). Only combatants that are new or
// whose health differs from existing are checked, so a stored value that a later definition change
// made invalid does not block unrelated edits. The system is looked up only when something needs
// checking.
func (r *Encounters) checkHealth(ctx context.Context, sysID string, cs, existing []Combatant) error {
        before := make(map[string]map[string]int, len(existing))
        for _, c := range existing {
                before[c.ID] = c.Health
        }
        var changed []Combatant
        for _, c := range cs {
                if prev, ok := before[c.ID]; len(c.Health) > 0 && (!ok || !maps.Equal(prev, c.Health)) {
                        changed = append(changed, c)
                }
        }
        if len(changed) == 0 {
                return nil
        }
        sys, err := r.systems.GetOrDefault(ctx, sysID)
        if errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
                return invalid("the encounter's game system has no definition and no default system is defined")
        }
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        for _, c := range changed {
                if err := CheckHealth(sys, c.Health); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// SetState validates st and writes it as the encounter's combat state with the revision check.
func (r *Encounters) SetState(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id string, expectedRevision int64, st EncounterState) (Encounter, error) {
        return r.Write(ctx, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id, expectedRevision, nil, st)
}

// Write validates settings (Updatable fields) and st (structure, and combatant health against the
// encounter's game system), then writes both in one atomic write with the revision check. Use it
// when a setting change, such as the ordering, comes with recomputed state.
func (r *Encounters) Write(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id string, expectedRevision int64, settings map[string]any, st EncounterState) (Encounter, error) {
        if err := validateEncounterSet(settings); err != nil {
                return Encounter{}, err
        }
        if err := ValidateState(st); err != nil {
                return Encounter{}, err
        }
        if sysID, ok := settings["game_system_id"].(string); ok {
                if err := r.requireSystem(ctx, sysID); err != nil {
                        return Encounter{}, err
                }
        }
        if slices.ContainsFunc(st.Combatants, func(c Combatant) bool { return len(c.Health) > 0 }) {
                cur, err := r.Load(ctx, ownerUserID, id)
                if err != nil {
                        return Encounter{}, err
                }
                sysID := cur.GameSystemID
                if v, ok := settings["game_system_id"].(string); ok {
                        sysID = v
                }
                existing := cur.Combatants
                if _, switched := settings["game_system_id"]; switched {
                        existing = nil // a new system: every combatant's health is checked afresh
                }
                if err := r.checkHealth(ctx, sysID, st.Combatants, existing); err != nil {
                        return Encounter{}, err
                }
        }
        state := map[string]any{
                "status": st.Status, "round": st.Round, "current_combatant_id": st.CurrentCombatantID,
                "turn_queue": nonNil(st.TurnQueue), "combatants": nonNilC(st.Combatants),
                "ref_keys": nonNil(refKeys(st.Combatants)),
        }
        return r.updateBoth(ctx, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id, expectedRevision, settings, state)
}

func nonNil(s []string) []string {
        if s == nil {
                return []string{}
        }
        return s
}

func nonNilC(s []Combatant) []Combatant {
        if s == nil {
                return []Combatant{}
        }
        return s
}

// Load reads owner's encounter; a miss is classified 403 (another owner's) or 404 (absent).
func (r *Encounters) Load(ctx context.Context, owner, id string) (Encounter, error) {
        cur, err := r.Get(ctx, owner, id)
        if errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
                if cerr := r.Missing(ctx, owner, id); cerr != nil {
                        return Encounter{}, cerr
                }
        }
        return cur, err
}

const (
        maxRefLen     = 200
        maxRefMatches = 100
)

// RefKey is the fixed-shape lookup key for one external reference: the hex SHA-256 of the
// length-prefixed key and value. Lookups filter on this alone, so request text never becomes part
// of a query filter.
func RefKey(key, value string) string {
        sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte(fmt.Sprintf("%d:%s%s", len(key), key, value)))
        return hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// refKeys derives Encounter.RefKeys from the live (not removed) combatants.
func refKeys(cs []Combatant) []string {
        var out []string
        for _, c := range cs {
                if c.Removed {
                        continue
                }
                for k, v := range c.ExternalRefs {
                        out = append(out, RefKey(k, v))
                }
        }
        slices.Sort(out)
        return slices.Compact(out)
}

// RefMatch is a combatant whose external_refs hold a looked-up pair, with its encounter's ID.
type RefMatch struct {
        EncounterID string    `json:"encounter_id"`
        Combatant   Combatant `json:"combatant"`
}

// FindByExternalRef returns owner's live combatants in private encounters whose external_refs map key to value. The
// database matches on the derived RefKey; the stored values are then compared exactly, so a hash
// collision cannot return a wrong combatant.
func (r *Encounters) FindByExternalRef(ctx context.Context, owner, key, value string) ([]RefMatch, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(owner); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if key == "" || value == "" || len(key) > maxRefLen || len(value) > maxRefLen {
                return nil, invalid("ref_key and ref_value are required, at most %d bytes", maxRefLen)
        }
        // Private encounters only: a session encounter is read through the session's role-aware view,
        // so an owner who has left or been demoted cannot reach hidden combatants or GM-only notes here.
        filter := append(scope(owner), bson.E{Key: "ref_keys", Value: RefKey(key, value)}, bson.E{Key: "session_id", Value: nil})
        var encs []Encounter
        if err := r.c.Find(ctx, filter, store.FindOptions{Limit: maxRefMatches}, &encs); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        var out []RefMatch
        for _, e := range encs {
                for _, c := range e.Combatants {
                        if !c.Removed && c.ExternalRefs[key] == value {
                                out = append(out, RefMatch{EncounterID: e.ID, Combatant: c})
                        }
                }
        }
        return out, nil
}

// LockGated locks owner's encounters that use a game system other than No System, and returns how
// many it locked. A locked encounter keeps all its data; only writes stop (spec
// initiative-game-systems, "Losing the entitlement locks gated-system items"). Each pass takes one
// encounter, so a retry after a failure finishes the rest.
func (r *Encounters) LockGated(ctx context.Context, owner string) (int, error) {
        filter := append(scope(owner),
                bson.E{Key: "game_system_id", Value: bson.D{{Key: "$ne", Value: DefaultSystemID}}},
                bson.E{Key: "locked_at", Value: nil})
        return r.flip(ctx, filter, bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "locked_at", Value: r.now()}}}})
}

// UnlockAll restores owner's locked encounters exactly as they were.
func (r *Encounters) UnlockAll(ctx context.Context, owner string) (int, error) {
        filter := append(scope(owner), bson.E{Key: "locked_at", Value: bson.D{{Key: "$ne", Value: nil}}})
        return r.flip(ctx, filter, bson.D{{Key: "$unset", Value: bson.D{{Key: "locked_at", Value: ""}}}})
}

func (r *Encounters) flip(ctx context.Context, filter, update bson.D) (int, error) {
        n := 0
        for {
                var out Encounter
                err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &out)
                if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                        return n, nil
                }
                if err != nil {
                        return n, err
                }
                r.emitCommit(ctx, commitFlag, nil, out)
                n++
        }
}

package data

import (
        "fmt"
        "maps"
        "slices"
)

// HealthError reports the health field that failed validation. It wraps ErrInvalid.
type HealthError struct {
        Field  string
        Reason string
        // Unknown is set when the game system defines no such field, as opposed to a value out of bounds.
        Unknown bool
}

func (e *HealthError) Error() string { return fmt.Sprintf("health field %q: %s", e.Field, e.Reason) }

// Unwrap lets errors.Is match ErrInvalid.
func (e *HealthError) Unwrap() error { return ErrInvalid }

// CheckHealth validates h against sys's tracker definition: every key must be a defined field, a
// flag must be 0 or 1, and each value must be at least the field's Min and at most its Max (or,
// for MaxRef, the value of the referenced field in h when present). Keys are checked in sorted
// order so the reported field is stable.
func CheckHealth(sys GameSystem, h map[string]int) error {
        return checkHealthKeys(sys, h, slices.Sorted(maps.Keys(h)))
}

// checkHealthKeys is CheckHealth for only the named keys of h; the other keys of h are read only as
// the MaxRef values the named keys are bounded by.
func checkHealthKeys(sys GameSystem, h map[string]int, keys []string) error {
        fields := make(map[string]TrackerField, len(sys.Fields))
        for _, f := range sys.Fields {
                fields[f.Key] = f
        }
        for _, k := range keys {
                v := h[k]
                f, ok := fields[k]
                if !ok {
                        return &HealthError{Field: k, Reason: "not defined by the game system", Unknown: true}
                }
                if f.Kind == FieldFlag && v != 0 && v != 1 {
                        return &HealthError{Field: k, Reason: fmt.Sprintf("%d is not 0 or 1", v)}
                }
                if f.Min != nil && v < *f.Min {
                        return &HealthError{Field: k, Reason: fmt.Sprintf("%d is below the minimum %d", v, *f.Min)}
                }
                if f.Max != nil && v > *f.Max {
                        return &HealthError{Field: k, Reason: fmt.Sprintf("%d is above the maximum %d", v, *f.Max)}
                }
                if ref, ok := h[f.MaxRef]; f.MaxRef != "" && ok && v > ref {
                        return &HealthError{Field: k, Reason: fmt.Sprintf("%d is above %s (%d)", v, f.MaxRef, ref)}
                }
        }
        return nil
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
)

// ItemState is what limit enforcement needs to know about one stored item.
type ItemState struct {
        ID        string
        CreatedAt time.Time
        KeepRank  int
        Inactive  bool
}

// ItemStates lists owner's live items oldest first, for deciding which stay active.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) ItemStates(ctx context.Context, owner string) ([]ItemState, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(owner); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return r.itemStates(ctx, r.scoped(owner))
}

func (r *Owned[T, P]) itemStates(ctx context.Context, filter bson.D) ([]ItemState, error) {
        var docs []T
        opts := store.FindOptions{
                Sort:       bson.D{{Key: "created_at", Value: 1}, {Key: "_id", Value: 1}},
                Projection: bson.D{{Key: "created_at", Value: 1}, {Key: "keep_rank", Value: 1}, {Key: "inactive_at", Value: 1}},
        }
        if err := r.c.Find(ctx, filter, opts, &docs); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        out := make([]ItemState, 0, len(docs))
        for i := range docs {
                m := P(&docs[i]).GetMeta()
                out = append(out, ItemState{ID: m.ID, CreatedAt: m.CreatedAt, KeepRank: m.KeepRank, Inactive: m.InactiveAt != nil})
        }
        return out, nil
}

// SetInactive marks owner's item inactive or active. It changes neither the revision nor the
// audit stamp: the owner did not edit the item.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) SetInactive(ctx context.Context, owner, id string, inactive bool) error {
        if err := checkUserID(owner); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return ErrNotFound
        }
        var update bson.D
        if inactive {
                update = bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "inactive_at", Value: r.now()}}}}
        } else {
                update = bson.D{{Key: "$unset", Value: bson.D{{Key: "inactive_at", Value: ""}}}}
        }
        var after T
        err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, r.scopedID(owner, id), update, &after)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return ErrNotFound
        }
        if err == nil {
                r.committed(ctx, commitFlag, nil, &after)
        }
        return err
}

// SetKeepRanks records the owner's choice: ids get ranks 1..n in order, every other item loses
// its rank. Items already in that state are not written.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) SetKeepRanks(ctx context.Context, owner string, ids []string) error {
        states, err := r.ItemStates(ctx, owner)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        want := make(map[string]int, len(ids))
        for i, id := range ids {
                want[id] = i + 1
        }
        for _, st := range states {
                if st.KeepRank == want[st.ID] {
                        continue
                }
                update := bson.D{{Key: "$unset", Value: bson.D{{Key: "keep_rank", Value: ""}}}}
                if n := want[st.ID]; n > 0 {
                        update = bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "keep_rank", Value: n}}}}
                }
                var discard T
                if err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, r.scopedID(owner, st.ID), update, &discard); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                        return fmt.Errorf("set keep rank: %w", err)
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// Package data is initiative-api's data layer. Every collection that stores user data goes
// through Owned, which scopes each read and write by the caller's user ID (docs/coding-
// conventions.md, "Owner-Scoped Data Access") and pushes filter, sort, projection, and limit
// into the query ("Push Filtering, Sorting, and Counting to the Query Layer").
package data

import (
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/model-core.go/models"
)

// Meta is embedded (inline) in every owner-scoped document: identity, owner, revision, and the
// platform audit fields (PADR-0001).
type Meta struct {
        ID          string `bson:"_id" json:"id"`
        OwnerUserID string `bson:"owner_user_id" json:"owner_user_id"`
        // Revision starts at 1 and advances on every update; writes compare-and-set on it.
        Revision int64 `bson:"revision" json:"revision"`
        // InactiveAt is set while the record is over the owner's limit: readable and deletable, not
        // usable. KeepRank is the owner's choice order (1 first); zero means no choice was made.
        InactiveAt *time.Time `bson:"inactive_at,omitempty" json:"inactive_at,omitempty"`
        KeepRank   int        `bson:"keep_rank,omitempty" json:"-"`
        // LockedAt is set while the record uses a gated game system its owner is not entitled to.
        LockedAt         *time.Time `bson:"locked_at,omitempty" json:"locked_at,omitempty"`
        models.Auditable `bson:",inline"`
}

// GetMeta lets generic code reach the embedded Meta through the document pointer.
func (m *Meta) GetMeta() *Meta { return m }

package data

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "slices"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
)

// CollectionNotificationPrefs holds each user's muted notice types per session.
const CollectionNotificationPrefs = "notification_preferences"

// NotificationPrefSpec: one document per user and session, addressed only by the caller's own id.
var NotificationPrefSpec = Spec{
        Name:    CollectionNotificationPrefs,
        Indexes: []store.IndexSpec{{Name: "user_session", Keys: asc("user_id", "session_id"), Unique: true}},
}

type notificationPrefDoc struct {
        UserID    string    `bson:"user_id"`
        SessionID string    `bson:"session_id"`
        Muted     []string  `bson:"muted"`
        UpdatedAt time.Time `bson:"updated_at"`
}

// NotificationPrefs stores which notice types a user has turned off for a session. Every read and
// write is filtered by the owning user's id (docs/coding-conventions.md, owner-scoped data
// access); there is no function that reaches another user's preferences. Whether the user belongs
// to the session is the caller's check.
//
// A document is never deleted: it is at most one short list per session a user has joined, a
// deleted session is only soft-deleted (PADR-0027), and keeping the list means a member who is
// removed and later rejoins keeps their mutes. The event path only reads it for current members.
type NotificationPrefs struct {
        c   store.Collection
        now func() time.Time
}

// NewNotificationPrefs builds the repository on db.
func NewNotificationPrefs(db store.Database) *NotificationPrefs {
        return &NotificationPrefs{c: db.Collection(CollectionNotificationPrefs), now: func() time.Time { return time.Now().UTC() }}
}

// Muted returns the notice types ownerUserID has turned off for sessionID; none when nothing is
// stored.
func (r *NotificationPrefs) Muted(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, sessionID string) ([]string, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(ownerUserID); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(sessionID)
        if !ok {
                return nil, invalid("session id is not valid")
        }
        var doc notificationPrefDoc
        err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, prefScope(ownerUserID, id), &doc)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return []string{}, nil
        }
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return doc.Muted, nil
}

// SetMuted replaces ownerUserID's muted types for sessionID in one atomic upsert. Unknown types
// are rejected; duplicates collapse.
func (r *NotificationPrefs) SetMuted(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, sessionID string, muted []string) ([]string, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(ownerUserID); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(sessionID)
        if !ok {
                return nil, invalid("session id is not valid")
        }
        if len(muted) > len(NoticeTypes) {
                return nil, invalid("at most %d notification types can be muted", len(NoticeTypes))
        }
        clean := make([]string, 0, len(muted))
        for _, m := range muted {
                if !slices.Contains(NoticeTypes, m) {
                        return nil, invalid("%q is not a notification type", m)
                }
                if !slices.Contains(clean, m) {
                        clean = append(clean, m)
                }
        }
        slices.Sort(clean)
        set := bson.D{{Key: "muted", Value: clean}, {Key: "updated_at", Value: r.now()}}
        if err := r.c.UpsertOne(ctx, prefScope(ownerUserID, id), bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: set}}); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return clean, nil
}

func prefScope(ownerUserID, sessionID string) bson.D {
        return bson.D{{Key: "user_id", Value: ownerUserID}, {Key: "session_id", Value: sessionID}}
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "maps"
        "sort"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/model-core.go/models"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson/primitive"
)

// Doc constrains P to a pointer to a document type that embeds Meta.
type Doc[T any] interface {
        *T
        GetMeta() *Meta
}

// Owned is the owner-scoped repository for one collection of user-owned documents. Every method
// requires the owner's user ID and folds it, plus the soft-delete filter, into the query itself.
// There is deliberately no method that reads or writes by ID alone.
type Owned[T any, P Doc[T]] struct {
        c     store.Collection
        spec  Spec
        now   func() time.Time
        newID func() string
        // extra narrows every owner-scoped read and write beyond ownership, and exclude says which
        // stored documents that narrowing hides, so a miss is classified 403 instead of reading as a
        // revision conflict that would return the hidden document. Encounters use them to keep the
        // owner path to private encounters: a session encounter is reached only through its session
        // (PADR-0035), so an owner who left or was demoted cannot read or change it here.
        extra   bson.D
        exclude func(*T) bool
        // commit, when set, is told about every successful create, write, soft delete and flag change
        // with the stored document, so one place can announce them (see Encounters.SetSink).
        commit func(ctx context.Context, kind commitKind, before, after *T)
}

func (r *Owned[T, P]) committed(ctx context.Context, kind commitKind, before, after *T) {
        if r.commit != nil {
                r.commit(ctx, kind, before, after)
        }
}

// NewOwned builds the repository for spec's collection in db.
func NewOwned[T any, P Doc[T]](db store.Database, spec Spec) *Owned[T, P] {
        return &Owned[T, P]{
                c:     db.Collection(spec.Name),
                spec:  spec,
                now:   func() time.Time { return time.Now().UTC() },
                newID: func() string { return primitive.NewObjectID().Hex() },
        }
}

// scope is the filter every owner-scoped query starts from.
func scope(owner string) bson.D {
        return bson.D{{Key: "owner_user_id", Value: owner}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}}
}

// scopeID is the owner-scoped filter for one record. id must already have passed cleanID.
func scopeID(owner, id string) bson.D {
        return append(bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}}, scope(owner)...)
}

// scoped is scope plus the repository's extra narrowing.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) scoped(owner string) bson.D {
        return append(scope(owner), r.extra...)
}

// scopedID is scopeID plus the repository's extra narrowing.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) scopedID(owner, id string) bson.D {
        return append(bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}}, r.scoped(owner)...)
}

// cleanID returns the canonical form of a client-supplied record ID, or false when it is not a
// 24-character hex ObjectID. Every ID that reaches a query filter goes through here, so a request
// value can only ever be a plain ObjectID string, never an operator document or arbitrary text.
func cleanID(id string) (string, bool) {
        oid, err := primitive.ObjectIDFromHex(id)
        if err != nil {
                return "", false
        }
        return oid.Hex(), true
}

// requireOwnerActor refuses a write with no valid owner or acting user id.
func requireOwnerActor(owner, actor string) error {
        if err := checkUserID(owner); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return checkUserID(actor)
}

// Create stores doc as owner's new record: it assigns the ID, owner, revision 1, and the audit
// stamp (created_by and updated_by are actor). Any ID or owner already on doc is overwritten.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) Create(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID string, doc P) error {
        return r.createWithID(ctx, ownerUserID, actingUserID, "", doc)
}

// createWithID is Create with a caller-chosen ID; an empty id means generate one.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) createWithID(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id string, doc P) error {
        if err := requireOwnerActor(ownerUserID, actingUserID); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        m := doc.GetMeta()
        m.ID = id
        if id == "" {
                m.ID = r.newID()
        }
        m.OwnerUserID = ownerUserID
        m.Revision = 1
        m.DeletedAt, m.DeletedBy = nil, nil
        models.StampCreate(&m.Auditable, actingUserID, r.now())
        if err := r.mapDuplicate(r.c.InsertOne(ctx, doc)); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        r.committed(ctx, commitCreate, nil, (*T)(doc))
        return nil
}

// mapDuplicate turns a unique-index rejection into ErrConflict for specs that opt in.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) mapDuplicate(err error) error {
        if r.spec.ConflictOnDuplicate && errors.Is(err, store.ErrDuplicate) {
                return ErrConflict
        }
        return err
}

// Get returns owner's live record. Another owner's record is indistinguishable from a missing
// one on reads.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) Get(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, id string) (T, error) {
        var out T
        if err := checkUserID(ownerUserID); err != nil {
                return out, err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return out, ErrNotFound
        }
        err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, r.scopedID(ownerUserID, id), &out)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return out, ErrNotFound
        }
        return out, err
}

// reservedFields are managed by the data layer and rejected in Update's set.
var reservedFields = map[string]bool{
        "_id": true, "owner_user_id": true, "revision": true,
        "created_at": true, "created_by": true, "updated_at": true, "updated_by": true,
        "deleted_at": true, "deleted_by": true,
}

func validateSet(spec Spec, set map[string]any) error {
        if len(set) == 0 {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: nothing to update", ErrInvalid)
        }
        for k := range set {
                if reservedFields[k] || strings.HasPrefix(k, "$") || strings.Contains(k, ".") || k == "" {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: field %q cannot be set", ErrInvalid, k)
                }
                if !in(spec.Updatable, k) {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w: field %q is not updatable on %s", ErrInvalid, k, spec.Name)
                }
        }
        return nil
}

func setDoc(set map[string]any, extra ...bson.E) bson.D {
        keys := make([]string, 0, len(set))
        for k := range set {
                keys = append(keys, k)
        }
        sort.Strings(keys)
        d := make(bson.D, 0, len(keys)+len(extra))
        for _, k := range keys {
                d = append(d, bson.E{Key: k, Value: set[k]})
        }
        return append(d, extra...)
}

// Update sets fields on owner's record if its revision is still expectedRevision, bumping the
// revision and stamping updated_*. The match is one atomic write on {_id, owner, live, revision},
// so a stale revision or another owner's record modifies nothing. When nothing matched, the
// cause is classified afterwards: ErrNotFound, ErrForbidden, or ErrRevisionConflict (with the
// current record returned).
//
// Update applies set as given and checks nothing about its values. Size and name bounds on a
// roster's characters live in the domain layer (domain.Rosters), so a caller that reaches this
// method directly bypasses them; go through the domain.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) Update(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id string, expectedRevision int64, set map[string]any) (T, error) {
        var out T
        if err := requireOwnerActor(ownerUserID, actingUserID); err != nil {
                return out, err
        }
        if err := validateSet(r.spec, set); err != nil {
                return out, err
        }
        return r.apply(ctx, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id, expectedRevision, set)
}

// updateState is Update for the spec's StateFields: values computed by domain rules, never taken
// from client input. Everything else about the write is identical (owner-scoped, compare-and-set,
// classified failures).
func (r *Owned[T, P]) updateState(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id string, expectedRevision int64, set map[string]any) (T, error) {
        var out T
        if err := requireOwnerActor(ownerUserID, actingUserID); err != nil {
                return out, err
        }
        if len(set) == 0 {
                return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: nothing to update", ErrInvalid)
        }
        for k := range set {
                if !in(r.spec.StateFields, k) {
                        return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: field %q is not a state field of %s", ErrInvalid, k, r.spec.Name)
                }
        }
        return r.apply(ctx, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id, expectedRevision, set)
}

// updateBoth writes settings (Updatable fields) and state (StateFields) in one atomic,
// compare-and-set write, for changes where a setting and the state derived from it must land
// together. Either map may be empty, not both.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) updateBoth(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id string, expectedRevision int64, settings, state map[string]any) (T, error) {
        var out T
        if err := requireOwnerActor(ownerUserID, actingUserID); err != nil {
                return out, err
        }
        merged := make(map[string]any, len(settings)+len(state))
        if len(settings) > 0 {
                if err := validateSet(r.spec, settings); err != nil {
                        return out, err
                }
                maps.Copy(merged, settings)
        }
        for k, v := range state {
                if !in(r.spec.StateFields, k) {
                        return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: field %q is not a state field of %s", ErrInvalid, k, r.spec.Name)
                }
                merged[k] = v
        }
        if len(merged) == 0 {
                return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: nothing to update", ErrInvalid)
        }
        return r.apply(ctx, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id, expectedRevision, merged)
}

// readBefore reads the document a revision-guarded write is about to change, for the event the
// commit emits. It is kept only if it is at the revision the write is made against: the write
// matches that revision or fails, so a kept document is exactly what the write changed. Anything
// else (no sink, a miss, a concurrent edit) is nil, and the event is built without a before.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) readBefore(ctx context.Context, owner, id string, expectedRevision int64) *T {
        if r.commit == nil {
                return nil
        }
        var b T
        if err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, r.scopedID(owner, id), &b); err != nil || P(&b).GetMeta().Revision != expectedRevision {
                return nil
        }
        return &b
}

func (r *Owned[T, P]) apply(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id string, expectedRevision int64, set map[string]any) (T, error) {
        var out T
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return out, ErrNotFound
        }
        filter := append(r.scopedID(ownerUserID, id), bson.E{Key: "revision", Value: expectedRevision}, bson.E{Key: "inactive_at", Value: nil}, bson.E{Key: "locked_at", Value: nil})
        update := bson.D{
                {Key: "$set", Value: setDoc(set,
                        bson.E{Key: "updated_at", Value: r.now()}, bson.E{Key: "updated_by", Value: actingUserID})},
                {Key: "$inc", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revision", Value: 1}}},
        }
        before := r.readBefore(ctx, ownerUserID, id, expectedRevision)
        err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &out)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                cur, cerr := r.classify(ctx, ownerUserID, id)
                if errors.Is(cerr, ErrRevisionConflict) {
                        var zero T
                        switch m := P(&cur).GetMeta(); {
                        case m.LockedAt != nil:
                                return zero, ErrLocked
                        case m.InactiveAt != nil:
                                return zero, ErrInactive
                        }
                }
                return cur, cerr
        }
        if err = r.mapDuplicate(err); err == nil {
                r.committed(ctx, commitWrite, before, &out)
        }
        return out, err
}

// SoftDelete tombstones owner's record if its revision is still expectedRevision (deleted_at/
// deleted_by, plus the spec's UnsetOnDelete fields), per PADR-0027 for user-owned domain data.
// Failures are classified like Update's, including ErrRevisionConflict.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) SoftDelete(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID, actingUserID, id string, expectedRevision int64) error {
        if err := requireOwnerActor(ownerUserID, actingUserID); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        now := r.now()
        update := bson.D{
                {Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{
                        {Key: "deleted_at", Value: now}, {Key: "deleted_by", Value: actingUserID},
                        {Key: "updated_at", Value: now}, {Key: "updated_by", Value: actingUserID}}},
                {Key: "$inc", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revision", Value: 1}}},
        }
        if len(r.spec.UnsetOnDelete) > 0 {
                unset := make(bson.D, 0, len(r.spec.UnsetOnDelete))
                for _, f := range r.spec.UnsetOnDelete {
                        unset = append(unset, bson.E{Key: f, Value: ""})
                }
                update = append(update, bson.E{Key: "$unset", Value: unset})
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return ErrNotFound
        }
        var after T
        filter := append(r.scopedID(ownerUserID, id), bson.E{Key: "revision", Value: expectedRevision})
        err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &after)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                _, err = r.classify(ctx, ownerUserID, id)
        }
        if err == nil {
                r.committed(ctx, commitDelete, nil, &after)
        }
        return err
}

// Missing explains why owner's scoped read of id found nothing: ErrNotFound when no live record
// exists, ErrForbidden when it belongs to another owner. Call it only after a scoped read or write
// has already failed; it never grants access. It returns nil if the record is visible to owner.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) Missing(ctx context.Context, owner, id string) error {
        if err := checkUserID(owner); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if _, err := r.classify(ctx, owner, id); !errors.Is(err, ErrRevisionConflict) {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

// classify runs only after an owner-scoped write matched nothing. It does an owner-agnostic
// lookup to tell 404 from 403 from 409; it never grants access.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) classify(ctx context.Context, owner, id string) (T, error) {
        var cur T
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return cur, ErrNotFound
        }
        err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}}, &cur)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return cur, ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return cur, err
        }
        m := P(&cur).GetMeta()
        switch {
        case m.DeletedAt == nil && m.OwnerUserID == owner && r.exclude != nil && r.exclude(&cur):
                var zero T
                return zero, ErrForbidden
        case m.DeletedAt != nil:
                var zero T
                return zero, ErrNotFound
        case m.OwnerUserID != owner:
                var zero T
                return zero, ErrForbidden
        }
        return cur, ErrRevisionConflict
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
)

// CollectionOwnerEntitlements holds the last entitlement values read with each user's own bearer.
const CollectionOwnerEntitlements = "owner_entitlements"

// OwnerEntitlement is one recorded key: a limit or a flag, and when it was read.
type OwnerEntitlement struct {
        Key    string    `bson:"key"`
        Type   string    `bson:"type"`
        Limit  int       `bson:"limit"`
        Flag   bool      `bson:"flag"`
        ReadAt time.Time `bson:"read_at"`
}

// ownerEntitlementDoc keys entries by encoded entitlement key so each is written on its own.
type ownerEntitlementDoc struct {
        UserID  string                      `bson:"_id"`
        Entries map[string]OwnerEntitlement `bson:"entries"`
}

// entryField encodes a key for use as a document field name, which cannot contain ".".
func entryField(key string) string {
        return strings.NewReplacer("%", "%25", ".", "%2E", "$", "%24").Replace(key)
}

// OwnerEntitlements records a user's entitlement values so a request that cannot read them (an
// integration credential carries no user bearer, PADR-0034/0037) can still enforce the owner's
// limits. The record is written only by first-party requests that just read the values with the
// user's own token, and read back for that same user: one document per user, addressed by the
// caller's own ID, never by a client-supplied record ID.
type OwnerEntitlements struct {
        c      store.Collection
        maxAge time.Duration
        now    func() time.Time
}

// NewOwnerEntitlements builds the repository on db. A recorded value older than maxAge is no
// longer fresh (see Fresh).
func NewOwnerEntitlements(db store.Database, maxAge time.Duration) *OwnerEntitlements {
        return &OwnerEntitlements{c: db.Collection(CollectionOwnerEntitlements), maxAge: maxAge, now: func() time.Time { return time.Now().UTC() }}
}

// Record upserts each entry under its own field (entries.<key>) with a single atomic write, so
// concurrent recorders of different keys never revert one another.
func (r *OwnerEntitlements) Record(ctx context.Context, userID string, entries ...OwnerEntitlement) error {
        if err := checkUserID(userID); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if len(entries) == 0 {
                return nil
        }
        at := r.now()
        set := make(bson.D, 0, len(entries))
        for _, e := range entries {
                e.ReadAt = at
                set = append(set, bson.E{Key: "entries." + entryField(e.Key), Value: e})
        }
        return r.c.UpsertOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: userID}}, bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: set}})
}

// Fresh reports whether e was read no longer than the configured max age ago.
func (r *OwnerEntitlements) Fresh(e OwnerEntitlement) bool {
        return r.now().Sub(e.ReadAt) <= r.maxAge
}

// Get returns the recorded value of key for userID, or ErrNotFound when the user has no record
// or the record lacks that key. It does not judge age; see Fresh.
func (r *OwnerEntitlements) Get(ctx context.Context, userID, key string) (OwnerEntitlement, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(userID); err != nil {
                return OwnerEntitlement{}, err
        }
        var doc ownerEntitlementDoc
        err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: userID}}, &doc)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return OwnerEntitlement{}, ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return OwnerEntitlement{}, err
        }
        e, ok := doc.Entries[entryField(key)]
        if !ok {
                return OwnerEntitlement{}, ErrNotFound
        }
        return e, nil
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "encoding/base64"
        "fmt"
        "regexp"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson/primitive"
)

// List bounds.
const (
        DefaultLimit = 50
        MaxLimit     = 200
        maxInValues  = 100
        maxValueLen  = 128
)

// Op is a filter operator. Clients choose from this closed set; a client string never becomes an
// operator key or a live regular expression (PADR-0030).
type Op string

// Filter operators.
const (
        OpEq       Op = "eq"
        OpIn       Op = "in"
        OpContains Op = "contains"
)

// Filter restricts a list to records whose Field matches. eq takes one value, in takes up to
// maxInValues, contains takes one non-empty string and matches case-insensitively as a literal.
type Filter struct {
        Field  string
        Op     Op
        Values []any
}

// SortKey orders a list. _id is always appended as the final tie-break so paging is stable.
type SortKey struct {
        Field string
        Desc  bool
}

// Query is the query-layer shape of a list call: everything here is applied by the database.
// Limit 0 means DefaultLimit; larger than MaxLimit is clamped. Fields is an optional projection
// (empty returns whole documents). Cursor is the opaque NextCursor of the previous page.
type Query struct {
        Filters []Filter
        Sort    []SortKey
        Fields  []string
        Limit   int
        Cursor  string
}

// Page is one page of results; NextCursor is empty on the last page.
type Page[T any] struct {
        Items      []T
        NextCursor string
}

func invalid(format string, a ...any) error {
        return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s", ErrInvalid, fmt.Sprintf(format, a...))
}

func in(list []string, s string) bool {
        for _, x := range list {
                if x == s {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

// scalar admits only plain values, so a nested document can never smuggle in an operator.
func scalar(v any) (any, bool) {
        switch x := v.(type) {
        case string, bool, int64:
                return x, true
        case int:
                return int64(x), true
        case float64:
                if x == float64(int64(x)) {
                        return int64(x), true
                }
        }
        return nil, false
}

func filterClause(spec Spec, f Filter) (bson.D, error) {
        if !in(spec.Filterable, f.Field) {
                return nil, invalid("cannot filter by %q", f.Field)
        }
        vals := make(bson.A, 0, len(f.Values))
        for _, v := range f.Values {
                s, ok := scalar(v)
                if !ok {
                        return nil, invalid("filter %q has a non-scalar value", f.Field)
                }
                vals = append(vals, s)
        }
        switch f.Op {
        case OpEq:
                if len(vals) != 1 {
                        return nil, invalid("eq on %q needs exactly one value", f.Field)
                }
                return bson.D{{Key: f.Field, Value: vals[0]}}, nil
        case OpIn:
                if len(vals) == 0 || len(vals) > maxInValues {
                        return nil, invalid("in on %q needs 1 to %d values", f.Field, maxInValues)
                }
                return bson.D{{Key: f.Field, Value: bson.D{{Key: "$in", Value: vals}}}}, nil
        case OpContains:
                s, ok := firstString(vals)
                if len(vals) != 1 || !ok || s == "" || len(s) > maxValueLen {
                        return nil, invalid("contains on %q needs one non-empty string up to %d bytes", f.Field, maxValueLen)
                }
                return bson.D{{Key: f.Field, Value: bson.D{{Key: "$regex", Value: regexp.QuoteMeta(s)}, {Key: "$options", Value: "i"}}}}, nil
        }
        return nil, invalid("unknown operator %q", f.Op)
}

func firstString(v bson.A) (string, bool) {
        if len(v) == 0 {
                return "", false
        }
        s, ok := v[0].(string)
        return s, ok
}

func sortKeys(spec Spec, in2 []SortKey) ([]SortKey, error) {
        keys := append([]SortKey(nil), in2...)
        seen := map[string]bool{}
        for _, k := range keys {
                if k.Field != "_id" && !in(spec.Sortable, k.Field) {
                        return nil, invalid("cannot sort by %q", k.Field)
                }
                if seen[k.Field] {
                        return nil, invalid("sort field %q repeated", k.Field)
                }
                seen[k.Field] = true
        }
        if !seen["_id"] {
                keys = append(keys, SortKey{Field: "_id"})
        }
        return keys, nil
}

func projection(spec Spec, fields []string, keys []SortKey) (bson.D, error) {
        if len(fields) == 0 {
                return nil, nil
        }
        p := bson.D{}
        added := map[string]bool{"_id": true}
        for _, f := range fields {
                if !in(spec.Projectable, f) {
                        return nil, invalid("cannot project %q", f)
                }
                if !added[f] {
                        added[f] = true
                        p = append(p, bson.E{Key: f, Value: 1})
                }
        }
        for _, k := range keys { // the cursor needs the sort values of the last row
                if !added[k.Field] {
                        added[k.Field] = true
                        p = append(p, bson.E{Key: k.Field, Value: 1})
                }
        }
        return p, nil
}

type plan struct {
        filter bson.D
        opts   store.FindOptions
        keys   []SortKey
        limit  int
}

func buildPlan(spec Spec, base bson.D, q Query) (plan, error) {
        if q.Limit < 0 {
                return plan{}, invalid("limit must not be negative")
        }
        limit := q.Limit
        if limit == 0 {
                limit = DefaultLimit
        }
        limit = min(limit, MaxLimit)

        keys, err := sortKeys(spec, q.Sort)
        if err != nil {
                return plan{}, err
        }
        proj, err := projection(spec, q.Fields, keys)
        if err != nil {
                return plan{}, err
        }
        clauses, err := clausesFor(spec, base, q)
        if err != nil {
                return plan{}, err
        }
        if q.Cursor != "" {
                vals, err := decodeCursor(q.Cursor, len(keys))
                if err != nil {
                        return plan{}, err
                }
                clauses = append(clauses, after(keys, vals))
        }
        sortD := make(bson.D, 0, len(keys))
        for _, k := range keys {
                dir := 1
                if k.Desc {
                        dir = -1
                }
                sortD = append(sortD, bson.E{Key: k.Field, Value: dir})
        }
        // Fetch one extra row to learn whether another page exists.
        return plan{
                filter: filterOf(clauses),
                opts:   store.FindOptions{Sort: sortD, Projection: proj, Limit: int64(limit + 1)},
                keys:   keys,
                limit:  limit,
        }, nil
}

// filterOf ANDs clauses. MongoDB rejects an empty $and, so no clauses means an empty filter.
func filterOf(clauses bson.A) bson.D {
        if len(clauses) == 0 {
                return bson.D{}
        }
        return bson.D{{Key: "$and", Value: clauses}}
}

func clausesFor(spec Spec, base bson.D, q Query) (bson.A, error) {
        clauses := bson.A{}
        if len(base) > 0 {
                clauses = append(clauses, base)
        }
        for _, f := range q.Filters {
                c, err := filterClause(spec, f)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                clauses = append(clauses, c)
        }
        return clauses, nil
}

// after builds the keyset condition for rows strictly after vals in sort order.
func after(keys []SortKey, vals []any) bson.D {
        ors := make(bson.A, 0, len(keys))
        for i := range keys {
                and := bson.D{}
                for j := 0; j < i; j++ {
                        and = append(and, bson.E{Key: keys[j].Field, Value: vals[j]})
                }
                op := "$gt"
                if keys[i].Desc {
                        op = "$lt"
                }
                and = append(and, bson.E{Key: keys[i].Field, Value: bson.D{{Key: op, Value: vals[i]}}})
                ors = append(ors, and)
        }
        return bson.D{{Key: "$or", Value: ors}}
}

func encodeCursor(vals []any) (string, error) {
        b, err := bson.MarshalExtJSON(bson.M{"v": bson.A(vals)}, true, false)
        return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b), err
}

func decodeCursor(c string, want int) ([]any, error) {
        b, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(c)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, invalid("malformed cursor")
        }
        var m bson.M
        if err := bson.UnmarshalExtJSON(b, true, &m); err != nil {
                return nil, invalid("malformed cursor")
        }
        arr, ok := m["v"].(primitive.A)
        if !ok || len(arr) != want {
                return nil, invalid("cursor does not match this query's sort")
        }
        for _, v := range arr {
                switch v.(type) {
                case string, int32, int64, float64, bool, primitive.DateTime:
                default:
                        return nil, invalid("malformed cursor")
                }
        }
        return arr, nil
}

// cursorFor encodes the sort values of the last row on a page.
func cursorFor(item any, keys []SortKey) (string, error) {
        raw, err := bson.Marshal(item)
        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        var m bson.M
        if err := bson.Unmarshal(raw, &m); err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        vals := make([]any, len(keys))
        for i, k := range keys {
                v, ok := m[k.Field]
                if !ok || v == nil {
                        return "", invalid("cannot page: sort field %q is missing on a record", k.Field)
                }
                vals[i] = v
        }
        return encodeCursor(vals)
}

// runQuery applies q entirely in the database: filter, sort, projection, limit, and keyset
// cursor. base is the mandatory scope (owner and live-record filters) every caller supplies.
func runQuery[T any](ctx context.Context, c store.Collection, spec Spec, base bson.D, q Query) (Page[T], error) {
        p, err := buildPlan(spec, base, q)
        if err != nil {
                return Page[T]{}, err
        }
        var items []T
        if err := c.Find(ctx, p.filter, p.opts, &items); err != nil {
                return Page[T]{}, err
        }
        if len(items) <= p.limit {
                return Page[T]{Items: items}, nil
        }
        items = items[:p.limit]
        next, err := cursorFor(items[p.limit-1], p.keys)
        if err != nil {
                return Page[T]{}, err
        }
        return Page[T]{Items: items, NextCursor: next}, nil
}

// List returns one page of owner's live records.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) List(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID string, q Query) (Page[T], error) {
        if err := checkUserID(ownerUserID); err != nil {
                return Page[T]{}, err
        }
        return runQuery[T](ctx, r.c, r.spec, r.scoped(ownerUserID), q)
}

// Count returns how many of owner's live records match filters, counted by the database. For
// encounters that is the private ones only: an encounter in a session counts against the session's
// limits, not the owner's saved-encounter limit.
func (r *Owned[T, P]) Count(ctx context.Context, ownerUserID string, filters []Filter) (int64, error) {
        if err := checkUserID(ownerUserID); err != nil {
                return 0, err
        }
        clauses, err := clausesFor(r.spec, r.scoped(ownerUserID), Query{Filters: filters})
        if err != nil {
                return 0, err
        }
        return r.c.CountDocuments(ctx, filterOf(clauses))
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "slices"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
)

// maxScrubRetries bounds how often a scrub re-reads an encounter that changed under it.
const maxScrubRetries = 3

// A scrub reads scrubPageSize encounters at a time and gives up after maxScrubPages pages.
const (
        scrubPageSize = 100
        maxScrubPages = 1000
)

// UpdateInSession writes set to the session's encounter id if its revision is still
// expectedRevision, bumping the revision and stamping updated_*. d must grant can_edit (a GM or
// co-GM); the match is one atomic write on {_id, session_id, revision}, so another session's
// encounter, a private one, and a stale revision all modify nothing. Failures are classified like
// Owned.Update: ErrNotFound, ErrLocked, ErrInactive, or ErrRevisionConflict (with the current
// record returned).
//
// Only "combatants" may be set today: it is the field session edits (controller assignment,
// approved change requests) change. Each combatant is validated and ref_keys is recomputed.
func (r *Encounters) UpdateInSession(ctx context.Context, d Decision, id string, expectedRevision int64, set map[string]any) (Encounter, error) {
        var out Encounter
        if err := d.require(relEdit); err != nil {
                return out, err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return out, ErrNotFound
        }
        cs, ok := set["combatants"].([]Combatant)
        if !ok || len(set) != 1 {
                return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: a session write sets combatants only", ErrInvalid)
        }
        if len(cs) > MaxCombatants {
                return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: more than %d combatants", ErrInvalid, MaxCombatants)
        }
        for _, c := range cs {
                if err := validateCombatant(c); err != nil {
                        return out, err
                }
        }
        filter := bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil},
                {Key: "revision", Value: expectedRevision}, {Key: "inactive_at", Value: nil}, {Key: "locked_at", Value: nil}}
        update := bson.D{
                {Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "combatants", Value: nonNilC(cs)}, {Key: "ref_keys", Value: nonNil(refKeys(cs))},
                        {Key: "updated_at", Value: r.now()}, {Key: "updated_by", Value: d.actor}}},
                {Key: "$inc", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revision", Value: 1}}},
        }
        var before *Encounter
        if r.commit != nil {
                var b Encounter
                if r.c.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}}, &b) == nil && b.Revision == expectedRevision {
                        before = &b
                }
        }
        err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &out)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return r.classifyInSession(ctx, d, id)
        }
        if err == nil {
                r.emitCommit(ctx, commitWrite, before, out)
        }
        return out, err
}

// classifyInSession explains why a session write matched nothing.
func (r *Encounters) classifyInSession(ctx context.Context, d Decision, id string) (Encounter, error) {
        var cur Encounter
        err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}}, &cur)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return Encounter{}, ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return Encounter{}, err
        }
        switch {
        case cur.LockedAt != nil:
                return Encounter{}, ErrLocked
        case cur.InactiveAt != nil:
                return Encounter{}, ErrInactive
        }
        return cur, ErrRevisionConflict
}

// ScrubController removes userID from every combatant's controller_user_ids in the session's
// encounters. It keeps a user who left, was removed, blocked, or demoted from regaining control
// of a combatant by rejoining or being promoted, and keeps the GM's view free of stale ids. d
// grants can_edit, or can_view when the user is scrubbing their own leave. Only encounters that
// list the user are read, a page at a time by _id until none is left, so no encounter is skipped;
// if the session has more than maxScrubPages pages the scrub fails rather than stop silently.
// Each changed encounter is written under its revision (the revision advances, so clients
// re-read) and re-read up to maxScrubRetries times if it changed under the scrub. Idempotent.
func (r *Encounters) ScrubController(ctx context.Context, d Decision, userID string) error {
        need := relEdit
        if userID == d.actor {
                need = relView
        }
        if err := pairOK(d, need, userID); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        var errs []error
        after := ""
        for page := 0; ; page++ {
                if page == maxScrubPages {
                        return errors.Join(append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%w: more than %d encounters list this controller", ErrInvalid, maxScrubPages*scrubPageSize))...)
                }
                filter := bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil},
                        {Key: "combatants.controller_user_ids", Value: userID}}
                if after != "" {
                        filter = append(filter, bson.E{Key: "_id", Value: bson.D{{Key: "$gt", Value: after}}})
                }
                var encs []Encounter
                if err := r.c.Find(ctx, filter, store.FindOptions{Sort: bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: 1}}, Limit: scrubPageSize}, &encs); err != nil {
                        return errors.Join(append(errs, err)...)
                }
                for _, e := range encs {
                        if err := r.scrubOne(ctx, d, e, userID); err != nil {
                                errs = append(errs, err)
                        }
                        after = e.ID
                }
                if len(encs) < scrubPageSize {
                        return errors.Join(errs...)
                }
        }
}

func (r *Encounters) scrubOne(ctx context.Context, d Decision, e Encounter, userID string) error {
        for range maxScrubRetries {
                cs, changed := withoutController(e.Combatants, userID)
                if !changed {
                        return nil
                }
                filter := bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: e.ID}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}, {Key: "revision", Value: e.Revision}}
                update := bson.D{
                        {Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "combatants", Value: cs}, {Key: "updated_at", Value: r.now()}, {Key: "updated_by", Value: d.actor}}},
                        {Key: "$inc", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revision", Value: 1}}},
                }
                var after Encounter
                err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &after)
                if err == nil {
                        was := e
                        r.emit(ctx, Change{Type: EventControllers, Before: &was, After: after, SessionID: after.SessionID, OwnerID: after.OwnerUserID, CombatantID: controllerIDsChanged(e.Combatants, cs)})
                }
                if !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                        return err
                }
                // The encounter changed or was deleted since it was read: re-read and try again.
                if err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: e.ID}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}}, &e); err != nil {
                        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                                return nil
                        }
                        return err
                }
        }
        return fmt.Errorf("encounter %s kept changing while scrubbing a controller", e.ID)
}

// withoutController returns cs with userID removed from every controller list, and whether
// anything changed. The input is not modified.
func withoutController(cs []Combatant, userID string) ([]Combatant, bool) {
        out := slices.Clone(cs)
        changed := false
        for i := range out {
                if !slices.Contains(out[i].ControllerUserIDs, userID) {
                        continue
                }
                changed = true
                out[i].ControllerUserIDs = slices.DeleteFunc(slices.Clone(out[i].ControllerUserIDs), func(u string) bool { return u == userID })
        }
        return out, changed
}

// ReleaseSession turns every encounter of the session into a private encounter of its GM (the
// session's owner, d.actor, including encounters a co-GM created): session_id and every combatant's controller_user_ids are cleared,
// the revision advances, and owner_user_id becomes the GM (initiative-sessions, "Session
// deletion"). d must be the GM's. It reads a page at a time by _id until none is left, and an
// encounter that changed under it is re-read, so no encounter is skipped; running it again after a
// partial failure finishes the rest (a released encounter no longer matches). Position and ref_keys
// are kept. The GM's saved-encounter limit reconciles afterwards like any other count change.
func (r *Encounters) ReleaseSession(ctx context.Context, d Decision) error {
        if err := d.require(relGM); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        var errs []error
        after := ""
        for page := 0; ; page++ {
                if page == maxScrubPages {
                        return errors.Join(append(errs, fmt.Errorf("%w: more than %d encounters in one session; retry delete", ErrInvalid, maxScrubPages*scrubPageSize))...)
                }
                filter := bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}}
                if after != "" {
                        filter = append(filter, bson.E{Key: "_id", Value: bson.D{{Key: "$gt", Value: after}}})
                }
                var encs []Encounter
                if err := r.c.Find(ctx, filter, store.FindOptions{Sort: bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: 1}}, Limit: scrubPageSize}, &encs); err != nil {
                        return errors.Join(append(errs, err)...)
                }
                for _, e := range encs {
                        if err := r.releaseOne(ctx, d, e); err != nil {
                                errs = append(errs, err)
                        }
                        after = e.ID
                }
                if len(encs) < scrubPageSize {
                        return errors.Join(errs...)
                }
        }
}

func (r *Encounters) releaseOne(ctx context.Context, d Decision, e Encounter) error {
        for range maxScrubRetries {
                cs := slices.Clone(e.Combatants)
                for i := range cs {
                        cs[i].ControllerUserIDs = nil
                }
                filter := bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: e.ID}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}, {Key: "revision", Value: e.Revision}}
                update := bson.D{
                        {Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "combatants", Value: nonNilC(cs)}, {Key: "owner_user_id", Value: d.actor},
                                {Key: "updated_at", Value: r.now()}, {Key: "updated_by", Value: d.actor}}},
                        {Key: "$unset", Value: bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: ""}}},
                        {Key: "$inc", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revision", Value: 1}}},
                }
                var after Encounter
                err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, &after)
                if err == nil {
                        // The audience is the session the encounter belonged to before this write.
                        r.emit(ctx, Change{Type: EventReleased, After: after, SessionID: d.session, OwnerID: after.OwnerUserID})
                }
                if !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                        return err
                }
                if err := r.c.FindOne(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: e.ID}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}}, &e); err != nil {
                        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                                return nil
                        }
                        return err
                }
        }
        return fmt.Errorf("encounter %s kept changing while its session was being deleted; retry delete", e.ID)
}

// ValidateCombatantChange checks after, a combatant as it would be once an edit applies to before
// (the combatant as stored), against the rules a write enforces: its own fields, and the health
// keys the edit changed against the game system's definition (the default system when the
// encounter's has none). A stored value that a later definition change made invalid, in a key the
// edit does not touch, does not block the edit; an edit that leaves health alone checks none.
func (r *Encounters) ValidateCombatantChange(ctx context.Context, gameSystemID string, before, after Combatant) error {
        if err := validateCombatant(after); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        var changed []string
        for k, v := range after.Health {
                if old, ok := before.Health[k]; !ok || old != v {
                        changed = append(changed, k)
                }
        }
        if len(changed) == 0 {
                return nil
        }
        sys, err := r.systems.GetOrDefault(ctx, gameSystemID)
        if errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
                return invalid("the encounter's game system has no definition and no default system is defined")
        }
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        slices.Sort(changed)
        return checkHealthKeys(sys, after.Health, changed)
}

// RetractNew soft-deletes an encounter just created in the session, for a create that turned out
// to be over the owner's limit. d must grant can_edit; only a live encounter of that session
// matches. An encounter that is already gone (deleted by someone else since) is not live, so
// there is nothing to retract and that is not an error.
func (r *Encounters) RetractNew(ctx context.Context, d Decision, id string) error {
        if err := d.require(relEdit); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return ErrNotFound
        }
        now := r.now()
        var after Encounter
        err := r.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, bson.D{{Key: "_id", Value: id}, {Key: "session_id", Value: d.session}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}},
                bson.D{{Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "deleted_at", Value: now}, {Key: "deleted_by", Value: d.actor}, {Key: "updated_at", Value: now}, {Key: "updated_by", Value: d.actor}}},
                        {Key: "$inc", Value: bson.D{{Key: "revision", Value: 1}}}}, &after)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return nil
        }
        if err == nil {
                r.emit(ctx, Change{Type: EventDeleted, After: after, SessionID: d.session, OwnerID: after.OwnerUserID})
        }
        return err
}

// controllerIDsChanged returns the ids of combatants whose controllers differ between before and
// after (same order and length).
func controllerIDsChanged(before, after []Combatant) []string {
        var ids []string
        for i := range after {
                if i < len(before) && !slices.Equal(before[i].ControllerUserIDs, after[i].ControllerUserIDs) {
                        ids = append(ids, after[i].ID)
                }
        }
        return ids
}

package data

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "time"

        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/store"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson/primitive"
)

// Session roles.
const (
        RoleGM       = "gm"
        RoleCoGM     = "co_gm"
        RolePlayer   = "player"
        RoleObserver = "observer"
)

// Join request states.
const (
        RequestPending  = "pending"
        RequestApproved = "approved"
        RequestRejected = "rejected"
)

// Session is a named group owned by one GM (the owner).
type Session struct {
        Meta `bson:",inline"`
        Name string `bson:"name" json:"name"`
        // OwnerLimits holds the owner's entitled values for the session-related keys as last read with
        // the owner's own token, and OwnerLimitsAt when. Members' checks use these because a member's
        // token cannot read the owner's entitlements (initiative-entitlement-gating).
        OwnerLimits   map[string]int `bson:"owner_limits,omitempty" json:"-"`
        OwnerLimitsAt *time.Time     `bson:"owner_limits_at,omitempty" json:"-"`
        // OwnerFlags holds the owner's entitled flags (the game-systems flag) as read at OwnerLimitsAt,
        // for the same reason as OwnerLimits. Never client-writable.
        OwnerFlags map[string]bool `bson:"owner_flags,omitempty" json:"-"`
        // AllowCoGMCreateEncounter lets a co-GM create encounters in the session. Only the GM changes
        // it. It is never serialized from here: SessionView shows it to the GM and co-GMs only.
        AllowCoGMCreateEncounter bool `bson:"allow_co_gm_create_encounter" json:"-"`
}

// Member is one user's place in a session. This collection is the source of truth for who holds
// which role (initiative-session-authorization).
type Member struct {
        ID        string    `bson:"_id" json:"-"`
        SessionID string    `bson:"session_id" json:"session_id"`
        UserID    string    `bson:"user_id" json:"user_id"`
        Role      string    `bson:"role" json:"role"`
        CreatedAt time.Time `bson:"created_at" json:"joined_at"`
        CreatedBy string    `bson:"created_by" json:"-"`
        // Live is set while the record is not deleted, so unique indexes can ignore deleted ones.
        Live      bool       `bson:"live,omitempty" json:"-"`
        DeletedAt *time.Time `bson:"deleted_at,omitempty" json:"-"`
        DeletedBy *string    `bson:"deleted_by,omitempty" json:"-"`
}

// Invite is a join code. Only its hash is stored, so the code is shown once, at creation.
type Invite struct {
        ID        string     `bson:"_id" json:"id"`
        SessionID string     `bson:"session_id" json:"session_id"`
        CodeHash  string     `bson:"code_hash" json:"-"`
        ExpiresAt time.Time  `bson:"expires_at" json:"expires_at"`
        RevokedAt *time.Time `bson:"revoked_at,omitempty" json:"revoked_at,omitempty"`
        RevokedBy string     `bson:"revoked_by,omitempty" json:"revoked_by,omitempty"`
        CreatedAt time.Time  `bson:"created_at" json:"created_at"`
        CreatedBy string     `bson:"created_by" json:"created_by"`
        // Live is set while the record is not deleted, so unique indexes can ignore deleted ones.
        Live      bool       `bson:"live,omitempty" json:"-"`
        DeletedAt *time.Time `bson:"deleted_at,omitempty" json:"-"`
        DeletedBy *string    `bson:"deleted_by,omitempty" json:"-"`
}

// JoinRequest is a user's pending, approved, or rejected request to join.
type JoinRequest struct {
        ID        string     `bson:"_id" json:"id"`
        SessionID string     `bson:"session_id" json:"session_id"`
        UserID    string     `bson:"user_id" json:"user_id"`
        Status    string     `bson:"status" json:"status"`
        Role      string     `bson:"role,omitempty" json:"role,omitempty"`
        Reason    string     `bson:"reason,omitempty" json:"reason,omitempty"`
        DecidedBy string     `bson:"decided_by,omitempty" json:"decided_by,omitempty"`
        DecidedAt *time.Time `bson:"decided_at,omitempty" json:"decided_at,omitempty"`
        CreatedAt time.Time  `bson:"created_at" json:"created_at"`
        // Live is set while the record is not deleted, so unique indexes can ignore deleted ones.
        Live      bool       `bson:"live,omitempty" json:"-"`
        DeletedAt *time.Time `bson:"deleted_at,omitempty" json:"-"`
        DeletedBy *string    `bson:"deleted_by,omitempty" json:"-"`
}

// Block keeps a user from rejoining a session.
type Block struct {
        ID        string    `bson:"_id" json:"-"`
        SessionID string    `bson:"session_id" json:"session_id"`
        UserID    string    `bson:"user_id" json:"user_id"`
        CreatedAt time.Time `bson:"created_at" json:"created_at"`
        CreatedBy string    `bson:"created_by" json:"created_by"`
        // Live is set while the record is not deleted, so unique indexes can ignore deleted ones.
        Live      bool       `bson:"live,omitempty" json:"-"`
        DeletedAt *time.Time `bson:"deleted_at,omitempty" json:"-"`
        DeletedBy *string    `bson:"deleted_by,omitempty" json:"-"`
}

// Sessions is the repository for sessions and their membership records. The session document is
// owner-scoped (its owner is the GM); membership records are looked up by session, and the domain
// layer decides from them who may do what.
//
// The owner-scoped repository is not embedded: its Get, Update, List and the rest would be session
// reads and writes with no Decision (PADR-0035). Only Open, Count, SoftDelete and Limits expose it.
type Sessions struct {
        owned                              *Owned[Session, *Session]
        c                                  store.Collection
        members, invites, requests, blocks store.Collection
        now                                func() time.Time
}

// NewSessions builds the repository on db.
func NewSessions(db store.Database) *Sessions {
        o := NewOwned[Session](db, SessionSpec)
        return &Sessions{
                owned: o, c: o.c,
                members: db.Collection(CollectionSessionMembers), invites: db.Collection(CollectionSessionInvites),
                requests: db.Collection(CollectionSessionRequests), blocks: db.Collection(CollectionSessionBlocks),
                now: func() time.Time { return time.Now().UTC() },
        }
}

func newID() string { return primitive.NewObjectID().Hex() }

// Every read of a membership collection filters deleted_at (PADR-0027: these records are soft
// deleted), so the two scope helpers carry it.
func sessionScope(sessionID string) bson.D {
        return bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: sessionID}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}}
}

func sessionUser(sessionID, userID string) bson.D {
        return bson.D{{Key: "session_id", Value: sessionID}, {Key: "user_id", Value: userID}, {Key: "deleted_at", Value: nil}}
}

// softDelete marks the first live record matching filter deleted by actor and returns ErrNotFound
// when there is none.
func (r *Sessions) softDelete(ctx context.Context, c store.Collection, filter bson.D, actor string) error {
        var gone bson.M
        err := c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, bson.D{
                {Key: "$set", Value: bson.D{{Key: "deleted_at", Value: r.now()}, {Key: "deleted_by", Value: actor}}},
                {Key: "$unset", Value: bson.D{{Key: "live", Value: ""}}},
        }, &gone)
        if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
                return ErrNotFound
        }
        return err
}

// pairOK validates the user id a call acts on.
func pairOK(d Decision, min, userID string) error {
        if err := d.require(min); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return checkUserID(userID)
}

// checkUserID requires a plain user id, so an id from a request cannot reach a query as anything
// else.
func checkUserID(u string) error {
        if u == "" || !ownerPattern.MatchString(u) {
                return invalid("user id is not valid")
        }
        return nil
}

// Open creates the session owned by actor with the ID auth-api minted for them and records actor
// as its GM. Creating is not a session read: it is gated by the sessions_per_gm entitlement, which
// the caller checks first, and by the mint that put the GM tuple on id.
func (r *Sessions) Open(ctx context.Context, actor, id string, s *Session) error {
        id, ok := cleanID(id)
        if !ok {
                return invalid("session id is not valid")
        }
        if err := r.owned.createWithID(ctx, actor, actor, id, s); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        m := Member{ID: newID(), SessionID: s.ID, UserID: actor, Role: RoleGM, CreatedAt: r.now(), CreatedBy: actor, Live: true}
        if err := r.members.InsertOne(ctx, m); err != nil {
                // Without its GM the session could never be deleted, and it would count against
                // sessions_per_gm. Remove it; if even that fails, say so, since the caller ends the mint.
                if derr := r.owned.SoftDelete(ctx, actor, actor, s.ID, s.Revision); derr != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%w (and removing the session failed: %v)", err, derr)
                }
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

// Count returns how many sessions owner has, including inactive ones.
func (r *Sessions) Count(ctx context.Context, owner string) (int64, error) {
        return r.owned.Count(ctx, owner, nil)
}

// SoftDelete deletes the session at expectedRevision. Only the GM may.
