        }
        status := c.Query("status")
        if status != "" && status != data.ChangePending && status != data.ChangeApproved && status != data.ChangeRejected && status != data.ChangeCancelled {
                badRequest(c, "status must be pending, approved, rejected or cancelled")
                return
        }
        p, err := h.svc.List(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("id"), c.Param("eid"), status, q)
        writePage(c, p.Items, p.NextCursor, err)
}

func (h changeRequestHandlers) approve(c *gin.Context) {
        req, err := h.svc.Approve(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("id"), c.Param("eid"), c.Param("rid"))
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, req)
}

func (h changeRequestHandlers) reject(c *gin.Context) {
        var b struct {
                Reason string `json:"reason"`
        }
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        req, err := h.svc.Reject(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("id"), c.Param("eid"), c.Param("rid"), b.Reason)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, req)
}

package server

import (
        "crypto/rand"
        "encoding/hex"
        "log/slog"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        sloggin "github.com/samber/slog-gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/constants"
)

const correlationKey = "correlation_id"

// Correlation reuses the caller's X-Correlation-ID or mints one, and echoes it on the response
// so a request can be followed across services and log lines.
func Correlation() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                id := c.GetHeader(constants.CorrelationHeader)
                if id == "" || len(id) > 128 {
                        id = newID()
                }
                c.Set(correlationKey, id)
                c.Header(constants.CorrelationHeader, id)
                sloggin.AddCustomAttributes(c, slog.String("correlation_id", id))
                c.Next()
        }
}

// CorrelationID returns the request's correlation ID, or "" outside the Correlation middleware.
func CorrelationID(c *gin.Context) string {
        return c.GetString(correlationKey)
}

func newID() string {
        b := make([]byte, 16)
        _, _ = rand.Read(b)
        return hex.EncodeToString(b)
}

package server

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "log/slog"
        "net/http"
        "slices"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
)

const (
        credentialContextKey = "initiative.credential"
        // Keys authz-client.go uses for the caller's identity; authz.Viewer and authz.Roles read them,
        // so a credential principal looks like its owner to every handler. TestCredentialPrincipal
        // fails if the library changes them.
        authzViewerKey = "authz.viewer"
        authzRolesKey  = "authz.roles"
)

// authenticate lets an integration secret ("ic_..." bearer) stand in for the user token. A secret
// resolves to its owner on every request, so a revoke applies to the next one. The principal gets
// the user role only and its scopes gate the method: read for GET/HEAD/OPTIONS, write otherwise.
// Anything else goes to userAuth (auth-api) unchanged.
func authenticate(creds *domain.Credentials, userAuth gin.HandlerFunc) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                secret, _ := strings.CutPrefix(c.GetHeader("Authorization"), "Bearer ")
                if creds == nil || !strings.HasPrefix(secret, domain.SecretPrefix) {
                        userAuth(c)
                        return
                }
                cred, ok := resolvedCredential(c)
                var err error
                if !ok {
                        cred, err = creds.Authenticate(c.Request.Context(), secret)
                }
                if errors.Is(err, data.ErrNotFound) {
                        c.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusUnauthorized, ErrorBody{Code: "unauthorized", Message: "Missing or invalid credential.", CorrelationID: CorrelationID(c)})
                        return
                }
                if err != nil {
                        writeError(c, err, nil)
                        return
                }
                need := data.ScopeWrite
                switch c.Request.Method {
                case http.MethodGet, http.MethodHead, http.MethodOptions:
                        need = data.ScopeRead
                }
                if !slices.Contains(cred.Scopes, need) {
                        c.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusForbidden, ErrorBody{Code: "scope_missing", Message: "This credential lacks the " + need + " scope.", CorrelationID: CorrelationID(c)})
                        return
                }
                c.Set(authzViewerKey, cred.OwnerUserID)
                c.Set(authzRolesKey, []string{authz.RoleUser})
                c.Set(credentialContextKey, cred.ID)
                if !isStreamUpgrade(c.Request) { // a stream rechecks on its own heartbeat
                        defer watchCredential(c, creds, cred.ID)()
                }
                c.Next()
        }
}

// credentialRecheck is how often a running request made with a credential asks whether the
// credential is still live. A request shorter than this is never rechecked; it was authenticated
// against the store an instant earlier.
var credentialRecheck = 5 * time.Second

// watchCredential cancels the request's context, with errCredentialRevoked as the cause, once the
// credential stops being live (revoked, deleted, owner denied, or the gate closed), so an
// in-flight request does not outlive it by more than credentialRecheck. Store errors count like a
// stream's: fail closed after maxGuardErrors in a row. The returned func stops the watcher.
func watchCredential(c *gin.Context, creds *domain.Credentials, id string) (stop func()) {
        ctx, cancel := context.WithCancelCause(c.Request.Context())
        c.Request = c.Request.WithContext(ctx)
        guard := liveGuard(func(ctx context.Context) (bool, error) { return creds.Live(ctx, id) })
        every := credentialRecheck // read here, on the request's goroutine
        go func() {
                t := time.NewTicker(every)
                defer t.Stop()
                for {
                        select {
                        case <-ctx.Done():
                                return
                        case <-t.C:
                                if guard(ctx) != nil {
                                        cancel(errCredentialRevoked)
                                        return
                                }
                        }
                }
        }()
        return func() { cancel(nil) }
}

func isStreamUpgrade(r *http.Request) bool {
        return r.Method == http.MethodGet && r.URL.Path == "/streams"
}

// userOnly rejects integration credentials: account-level routes need the user's own bearer
// (they forward it to entitlements-api, PADR-0037), and a credential must not manage credentials.
func userOnly(c *gin.Context) {
        if _, ok := c.Get(credentialContextKey); ok {
                c.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusForbidden, ErrorBody{Code: "user_token_required", Message: "This request needs your own sign-in, not an integration credential.", CorrelationID: CorrelationID(c)})
                return
        }
        c.Next()
}

type credentialHandlers struct {
        svc Services
}

func setupCredentialHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, s Services) {
        h := credentialHandlers{s}
        g.POST("/credentials", h.create)
        g.GET("/credentials", h.list)
        g.POST("/credentials/:id/revoke", h.revoke)
}

type credentialBody struct {
        Label  string   `json:"label"`
        Scopes []string `json:"scopes"`
}

// create returns the secret once, alongside the stored record.
func (h credentialHandlers) create(c *gin.Context) {
        var b credentialBody
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        cred, secret, err := h.svc.Credentials.Create(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), b.Label, b.Scopes, checkerFor(c, h.svc.Entitlements))
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusCreated, gin.H{"credential": cred, "secret": secret})
}

func (h credentialHandlers) list(c *gin.Context) {
        page, err := h.svc.Credentials.List(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), data.Query{Cursor: c.Query("cursor")})
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": page.Items, "next_cursor": page.NextCursor})
}

func (h credentialHandlers) revoke(c *gin.Context) {
        cred, err := h.svc.Credentials.Revoke(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("id"))
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, cred)
}

// setupAdminCredentialHandlers registers the administrator's reopen. The group takes no integration
// credentials, so only an admin's own bearer reaches it.
func setupAdminCredentialHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, creds *domain.Credentials) {
        g.POST("/admin/credentials/reopen", func(c *gin.Context) {
                actor := authz.Viewer(c)
                if err := creds.Reopen(c.Request.Context(), actor); err != nil {
                        writeError(c, err, nil)
                        return
                }
                slog.WarnContext(c.Request.Context(), "audit: integration credentials reopened", "audit", true, "actor", actor, "correlation_id", CorrelationID(c))
                c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"open": true})
        })
        g.GET("/admin/credentials", func(c *gin.Context) { adminListCredentials(c, creds) })
        g.GET("/admin/credentials/deletions", func(c *gin.Context) { adminDeletions(c, creds) })
        g.DELETE("/admin/credentials/:id", func(c *gin.Context) { adminDeleteCredential(c, creds) })
}

// pageQuery reads ?cursor and ?limit; it answers 400 and returns false on a bad limit.
func pageQuery(c *gin.Context) (data.Query, bool) {
        q := data.Query{Cursor: c.Query("cursor")}
        if v := c.Query("limit"); v != "" {
                n, err := strconv.Atoi(v)
                if err != nil || n < 1 {
                        badRequest(c, "limit must be a positive integer")
                        return q, false
                }
                q.Limit = n
        }
        return q, true
}

// adminListCredentials lists any owner's credentials; ?owner= narrows it. Secrets and hashes are
// never in the model's JSON.
func adminListCredentials(c *gin.Context, creds *domain.Credentials) {
        q, ok := pageQuery(c)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        page, err := creds.ListAll(c.Request.Context(), c.Query("owner"), q)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": page.Items, "next_cursor": page.NextCursor})
}

func adminDeletions(c *gin.Context, creds *domain.Credentials) {
        q, ok := pageQuery(c)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        page, err := creds.Deletions(c.Request.Context(), c.Query("owner"), q)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": page.Items, "next_cursor": page.NextCursor})
}

// adminDeleteCredential deletes a credential permanently. The reason is required; the audit entry
// is stored before the credential goes and is logged as well.
func adminDeleteCredential(c *gin.Context, creds *domain.Credentials) {
        var b struct {
                Reason string `json:"reason"`
        }
        // A missing or malformed body is the same 422 as an empty reason, not bindJSON's 400.
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&b); err != nil || strings.TrimSpace(b.Reason) == "" {
                c.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, ErrorBody{Code: "reason_required", Message: "A reason is required to delete a credential.", CorrelationID: CorrelationID(c)})
                return
        }
        actor := authz.Viewer(c)
        entry, err := creds.DeletePermanently(c.Request.Context(), actor, c.Param("id"), b.Reason)
        if errors.Is(err, data.ErrNotFound) || errors.Is(err, data.ErrInvalid) {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        if err != nil {
                slog.ErrorContext(c.Request.Context(), "credential deletion did not complete", "actor", actor, "credential_id", c.Param("id"), "error", err, "correlation_id", CorrelationID(c))
                c.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, ErrorBody{Code: "deletion_incomplete", Message: "The deletion did not complete and may be partly done. Retry the same request.", CorrelationID: CorrelationID(c)})
                return
        }
        slog.WarnContext(c.Request.Context(), "audit: integration credential deleted", "audit", true, "actor", actor,
                "credential_id", entry.CredentialID, "owner", entry.OwnerUserID, "label", entry.Label, "reason", entry.Reason, "correlation_id", CorrelationID(c))
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, entry)
}

package server

import (
        "errors"
        "net/http"
        "strconv"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
)

type encounterViewHandlers struct {
        svc      *domain.Encounters
        sessions *domain.Sessions
}

// setupEncounterViewHandlers registers the read routes for encounters. Every response is a
// domain.EncounterView built for the caller's role; no handler here serializes an encounter any
// other way (initiative-api-resources, "Role-aware responses"). They sit in the user-only group,
// like the session routes.
func setupEncounterViewHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, svc *domain.Encounters, sessions *domain.Sessions) {
        h := encounterViewHandlers{svc, sessions}
        g.GET("/sessions/:id/encounters", h.list)
        g.POST("/sessions/:id/encounters", h.create)
        g.GET("/sessions/:id/encounters/:eid", h.get)
        g.GET("/sessions/:id/encounters/:eid/combatants/:cid", h.combatant)
        g.PUT("/sessions/:id/encounters/:eid/combatants/:cid/controllers", h.setControllers)
}

// viewer authorizes the caller on the session in the path. A caller who is not a member gets 403
// before anything about the session's contents is looked up.
func (h encounterViewHandlers) viewer(c *gin.Context) (data.Viewer, bool) {
        v, err := h.sessions.Viewer(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("id"))
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return data.Viewer{}, false
        }
        return v, true
}

// create makes an encounter in the session, owned by its GM. The session decides who may and
// whether the GM's saved-encounters limit has room; the encounter service then runs the same
// validation as for a private encounter, and the response is built for the caller's role.
func (h encounterViewHandlers) create(c *gin.Context) {
        var b encounterCreate
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        if b.Name == nil {
                badRequest(c, "request body must be a JSON encounter with a name")
                return
        }
        ctx := c.Request.Context()
        grant, err := h.sessions.AuthorizeEncounterCreate(ctx, authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("id"))
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        e := b.encounter()
        for _, cb := range b.Combatants {
                e.Combatants = append(e.Combatants, cb.combatant())
        }
        view, err := h.svc.CreateInSession(ctx, grant, e)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        writeView(c, http.StatusCreated, view, nil)
}

func (h encounterViewHandlers) list(c *gin.Context) {
        v, ok := h.viewer(c)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        views, err := h.svc.ViewsForSession(c.Request.Context(), v, 100)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": views})
}

func (h encounterViewHandlers) get(c *gin.Context) {
        v, ok := h.viewer(c)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        view, err := h.svc.ViewForSession(c.Request.Context(), v, c.Param("eid"))
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        writeView(c, http.StatusOK, view, nil)
}

// combatant answers from the encounter view, so a combatant the caller may not see is absent from
// the lookup and gets the same 404 as one that does not exist.
func (h encounterViewHandlers) combatant(c *gin.Context) {
        v, ok := h.viewer(c)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        view, err := h.svc.ViewForSession(c.Request.Context(), v, c.Param("eid"))
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        cv, found := view.Combatant(c.Param("cid"))
        if !found {
                writeError(c, data.ErrNotFound, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, cv)
}

// writeView sends view with an ETag: the stored revision for a GM, and for everyone else the digest
// of what they can see, so a hidden edit never changes what a player's client observes.
func writeView(c *gin.Context, status int, view domain.EncounterView, fields []string) {
        if tag, ok := view[domain.FieldViewTag].(string); ok {
                c.Header("ETag", `"`+tag+`"`)
        } else if rev, ok := view["revision"].(int64); ok {
                c.Header("ETag", `"r`+strconv.FormatInt(rev, 10)+`"`)
        }
        c.JSON(status, projected(view, fields))
}

type controllersBody struct {
        ControllerUserIDs []string `json:"controller_user_ids"`
        Revision          int64    `json:"revision"`
}

// setControllers assigns a combatant's controllers. Only the session's GM and co-GMs may; every
// listed user must be a session member who can control (not an observer). The revision comes from
// If-Match, the query, or the body, and a stale one is a 409 carrying the current view.
func (h encounterViewHandlers) setControllers(c *gin.Context) {
        var b controllersBody
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        if b.ControllerUserIDs == nil {
                badRequest(c, "controller_user_ids is required (an empty list clears the controllers)")
                return
        }
        rev, ok := revisionOf(c, b.Revision)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        actor := authz.Viewer(c)
        view, err := h.svc.AssignControllers(c.Request.Context(), h.sessions, actor, c.Param("id"), c.Param("eid"), c.Param("cid"), b.ControllerUserIDs, rev)
        if err != nil {
                var current any
                if errors.Is(err, data.ErrRevisionConflict) {
                        if v, viewerr := h.sessions.Viewer(c.Request.Context(), actor, c.Param("id")); viewerr == nil {
                                if cur, cerr := h.svc.ViewForSession(c.Request.Context(), v, c.Param("eid")); cerr == nil {
                                        current = cur
                                }
                        }
                }
                writeError(c, err, current)
                return
        }
        writeView(c, http.StatusOK, view, nil)
}

package server

import (
        "context"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/entitlements"
)

type encounterHandlers struct {
        svc     *domain.Encounters
        rosters *domain.Rosters
        ent     *entitlements.Client
}

// setupEncounterHandlers registers the owner's private encounters: reads, writes, combatants, turn
// actions, and the roster transfers. They sit in the group that accepts integration credentials.
// Every encounter response is a domain.EncounterView written by respond, the only place these
// handlers serialize an encounter (initiative-api-resources, "Role-aware responses").
func setupEncounterHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, svc *domain.Encounters, rosters *domain.Rosters, ent *entitlements.Client) {
        h := encounterHandlers{svc, rosters, ent}
        g.GET("/encounters", h.list)
        g.POST("/encounters", h.create)
        g.GET("/encounters/:id", h.get)
        g.PATCH("/encounters/:id", h.update)
        g.DELETE("/encounters/:id", h.remove)
        g.POST("/encounters/:id/combatants", h.addCombatant)
        g.PATCH("/encounters/:id/combatants/:cid", h.updateCombatant)
        g.DELETE("/encounters/:id/combatants/:cid", h.removeCombatant)
        g.POST("/encounters/:id/combatants/:cid/restore", h.restoreCombatant)
        g.POST("/encounters/:id/start", h.turnAction(svc.Start))
        g.POST("/encounters/:id/end", h.turnAction(svc.End))
        g.POST("/encounters/:id/reset", h.turnAction(svc.Reset))
        g.POST("/encounters/:id/advance", h.turnAction(svc.Advance))
        g.POST("/encounters/:id/back", h.turnAction(svc.StepBack))
        if rosters != nil {
                g.POST("/rosters/:id/encounters", h.fromRoster)
                g.POST("/encounters/:id/save-as-roster", h.saveAsRoster)
        }
}

// encounterWrite is a domain write on one encounter at a revision.
type encounterWrite func(ctx context.Context, owner, actor, id string, rev int64) (data.Encounter, error)

type conditionBody struct {
        Name            string `json:"name"`
        RoundsRemaining *int   `json:"rounds_remaining"`
}

type combatantBody struct {
        Name             string            `json:"name"`
        Kind             string            `json:"kind"`
        Initiative       *float64          `json:"initiative"`
        Health           map[string]int    `json:"health"`
        Conditions       []conditionBody   `json:"conditions"`
        Notes            string            `json:"notes"`
        ExternalRefs     map[string]string `json:"external_refs"`
        Hidden           bool              `json:"hidden"`
        HealthVisibility string            `json:"health_visibility"`
        NotesShared      bool              `json:"notes_shared"`
}

func conditionsOf(in []conditionBody) []data.Condition {
        out := make([]data.Condition, 0, len(in))
        for _, c := range in {
                out = append(out, data.Condition{Name: c.Name, RoundsRemaining: c.RoundsRemaining})
        }
        return out
}

func (b combatantBody) combatant() data.Combatant {
        return data.Combatant{Name: b.Name, Kind: b.Kind, Initiative: b.Initiative, Health: b.Health, Conditions: conditionsOf(b.Conditions),
                Notes: b.Notes, ExternalRefs: b.ExternalRefs, Hidden: b.Hidden, HealthVisibility: b.HealthVisibility, NotesShared: b.NotesShared}
}

// encounterSettings are the fields of an encounter a client sets. Every one is optional.
type encounterSettings struct {
        Name         *string `json:"name"`
        GameSystemID *string `json:"game_system_id"`
        Ordering     *string `json:"ordering"`
        TieBreaker   *string `json:"tie_breaker"`
        Labels       *bool   `json:"labels"`
        Colorblind   *bool   `json:"colorblind"`
        ThemeID      *string `json:"theme_id"`
        Position     *int    `json:"position"`
}

// encounterCreate is the body of POST /encounters: the settings plus the starting combatants.
type encounterCreate struct {
        encounterSettings
        Combatants []combatantBody `json:"combatants"`
}

// encounterPatch is the body of PATCH /encounters/:id. Combatants change through their own routes.
type encounterPatch struct {
        encounterSettings
        Revision int64 `json:"revision"`
}

// settings is the set of fields the request names, keyed as the data layer's update list.
func (b encounterSettings) settings() map[string]any {
        set := map[string]any{}
        if b.Name != nil {
                set["name"] = *b.Name
        }
        if b.GameSystemID != nil {
                set["game_system_id"] = *b.GameSystemID
        }
        if b.Ordering != nil {
                set["ordering"] = *b.Ordering
        }
        if b.TieBreaker != nil {
                set["tie_breaker"] = *b.TieBreaker
        }
        if b.Labels != nil {
                set["labels"] = *b.Labels
        }
        if b.Colorblind != nil {
                set["colorblind"] = *b.Colorblind
        }
        if b.ThemeID != nil {
                set["theme_id"] = *b.ThemeID
        }
        if b.Position != nil {
                set["position"] = *b.Position
        }
        return set
}

// encounter builds a new draft from the settings, defaulting the ordering mode and tie breaker.
func (b encounterSettings) encounter() data.Encounter {
        e := data.Encounter{Ordering: data.OrderHighToLow, TieBreaker: data.TieRandom}
        if b.Name != nil {
                e.Name = *b.Name
        }
        if b.GameSystemID != nil {
                e.GameSystemID = *b.GameSystemID
        }
        if b.Ordering != nil {
                e.Ordering = *b.Ordering
        }
        if b.TieBreaker != nil {
                e.TieBreaker = *b.TieBreaker
        }
        if b.Labels != nil {
                e.Labels = *b.Labels
        }
        if b.Colorblind != nil {
                e.Colorblind = *b.Colorblind
        }
        if b.ThemeID != nil {
                e.ThemeID = *b.ThemeID
        }
        if b.Position != nil {
                e.Position = *b.Position
        }
        return e
}

// encounterFields are the names ?fields= may select from an encounter view: the stored fields
// the spec lists plus the derived count.
var encounterFields = append(append([]string{}, data.EncounterSpec.Projectable...), "combatant_count")

// projected keeps the fields the client selected; identity keys always stay.
func projected(view domain.EncounterView, fields []string) map[string]any {
        return projectMap(view, fields, "id", "revision", domain.FieldViewTag)
}

// respond is the one place these handlers write an encounter: the caller's view of enc.
func (h encounterHandlers) respond(c *gin.Context, status int, enc data.Encounter) {
        view, err := h.svc.ViewWritten(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), enc)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        writeView(c, status, view, nil)
}

// fail writes err; a revision conflict carries the encounter's current view.
func (h encounterHandlers) fail(c *gin.Context, err error, id string) {
        var current any
        if errors.Is(err, data.ErrRevisionConflict) {
                if v, verr := h.svc.ViewOwned(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), id); verr == nil {
                        current = v
                }
        }
        writeError(c, err, current)
}

func (h encounterHandlers) list(c *gin.Context) {
        q, ok := parseQuery(c, map[string]string{"position": typeInt})
        if !ok || !checkFields(c, q.Fields, encounterFields) {
                return
        }
        // Projected after the view is built (below), never in the query: a view built from a partly
        // loaded encounter would show zero values for the fields left out.
        fields := q.Fields
        q.Fields = nil
        page, err := h.svc.ViewsOwned(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), q)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        items := make([]map[string]any, 0, len(page.Items))
        for _, v := range page.Items {
                items = append(items, projected(v, fields))
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": items, "next_cursor": page.NextCursor})
}

func (h encounterHandlers) get(c *gin.Context) {
        fields, ok := fieldsOf(c, encounterFields)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        view, err := h.svc.ViewOwned(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("id"))
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        writeView(c, http.StatusOK, view, fields)
}

func (h encounterHandlers) create(c *gin.Context) {
        var b encounterCreate
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        if b.Name == nil {
                badRequest(c, "request body must be a JSON encounter with a name")
                return
        }
        if !requireRoom(c, h.ent, domain.KeySavedEncounters, h.svc.Count) {
                return
        }
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        e := b.encounter()
        for _, cb := range b.Combatants {
                e.Combatants = append(e.Combatants, cb.combatant())
        }
        enc, err := h.svc.Create(c.Request.Context(), owner, owner, e)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        h.respond(c, http.StatusCreated, enc)
}

func (h encounterHandlers) update(c *gin.Context) {
        var b encounterPatch
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        rev, ok := revisionOf(c, b.Revision)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        enc, err := h.svc.UpdateSettings(c.Request.Context(), owner, owner, c.Param("id"), rev, b.settings())
        if err != nil {
                h.fail(c, err, c.Param("id"))
                return
        }
        h.respond(c, http.StatusOK, enc)
}

func (h encounterHandlers) remove(c *gin.Context) {
        rev, ok := revisionOf(c, 0)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        if err := h.svc.Delete(c.Request.Context(), owner, owner, c.Param("id"), rev); err != nil {
                h.fail(c, err, c.Param("id"))
                return
        }
        c.Status(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (h encounterHandlers) addCombatant(c *gin.Context) {
        var b struct {
                combatantBody
                Revision int64 `json:"revision"`
        }
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        rev, ok := revisionOf(c, b.Revision)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        enc, err := h.svc.AddCombatant(c.Request.Context(), owner, owner, c.Param("id"), rev, b.combatant())
        if err != nil {
                h.fail(c, err, c.Param("id"))
                return
        }
        h.respond(c, http.StatusCreated, enc)
}

func (h encounterHandlers) updateCombatant(c *gin.Context) {
        var b struct {
                Revision         int64            `json:"revision"`
                Name             *string          `json:"name"`
                Kind             *string          `json:"kind"`
                Initiative       *float64         `json:"initiative"`
                Notes            *string          `json:"notes"`
                Health           map[string]int   `json:"health"`
                Conditions       *[]conditionBody `json:"conditions"`
                Hidden           *bool            `json:"hidden"`
                HealthVisibility *string          `json:"health_visibility"`
                NotesShared      *bool            `json:"notes_shared"`
        }
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        rev, ok := revisionOf(c, b.Revision)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        patch := domain.CombatantPatch{Name: b.Name, Kind: b.Kind, Initiative: b.Initiative, Notes: b.Notes, Health: b.Health,
                Hidden: b.Hidden, HealthVisibility: b.HealthVisibility, NotesShared: b.NotesShared}
        if b.Conditions != nil {
                conds := conditionsOf(*b.Conditions)
                patch.Conditions = &conds
        }
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        enc, err := h.svc.UpdateCombatant(c.Request.Context(), owner, owner, c.Param("id"), rev, c.Param("cid"), patch)
        if err != nil {
                h.fail(c, err, c.Param("id"))
                return
        }
        h.respond(c, http.StatusOK, enc)
}

func (h encounterHandlers) removeCombatant(c *gin.Context) {
        h.combatantAction(c, h.svc.RemoveCombatant)
}

func (h encounterHandlers) restoreCombatant(c *gin.Context) {
        h.combatantAction(c, h.svc.RestoreCombatant)
}

// combatantAction runs a revisioned action on one combatant and returns the encounter.
func (h encounterHandlers) combatantAction(c *gin.Context, fn func(ctx context.Context, owner, actor, id string, rev int64, combatantID string) (data.Encounter, error)) {
        rev, ok := revisionOf(c, bodyRevision(c))
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        enc, err := fn(c.Request.Context(), owner, owner, c.Param("id"), rev, c.Param("cid"))
        if err != nil {
                h.fail(c, err, c.Param("id"))
                return
        }
        h.respond(c, http.StatusOK, enc)
}

// turnAction serves the revisioned actions that change combat state: start, end, reset, advance
// and back. The body is optional; the revision may come from If-Match or the query.
func (h encounterHandlers) turnAction(fn encounterWrite) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                rev, ok := revisionOf(c, bodyRevision(c))
                if !ok {
                        return
                }
                owner := authz.Viewer(c)
                enc, err := fn(c.Request.Context(), owner, owner, c.Param("id"), rev)
                if err != nil {
                        h.fail(c, err, c.Param("id"))
                        return
                }
                h.respond(c, http.StatusOK, enc)
        }
}

// bodyRevision reads an optional JSON body's revision field, 0 when there is no usable body.
func bodyRevision(c *gin.Context) int64 {
        var b struct {
                Revision int64 `json:"revision"`
        }
        if c.Request.Body != nil {
                _ = json.NewDecoder(c.Request.Body).Decode(&b)
        }
        return b.Revision
}

// fromRoster creates an encounter with one combatant per character of the roster in the path.
func (h encounterHandlers) fromRoster(c *gin.Context) {
        var b encounterSettings
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        if b.Name == nil {
                badRequest(c, "request body must be a JSON encounter with a name")
                return
        }
        if !requireRoom(c, h.ent, domain.KeySavedEncounters, h.svc.Count) {
                return
        }
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        e := b.encounter()
        enc, err := h.rosters.CreateEncounterFromRoster(c.Request.Context(), owner, owner, c.Param("id"), e)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        h.respond(c, http.StatusCreated, enc)
}

// saveAsRoster stores a roster from the encounter in the path. The response is the roster, which
// holds no combatant visibility data.
func (h encounterHandlers) saveAsRoster(c *gin.Context) {
        var b struct {
                Name string `json:"name"`
        }
        if c.Request.ContentLength != 0 && !bindJSON(c, &b) { // the name is optional: it defaults to the encounter's
                return
        }
        if !requireRoom(c, h.ent, domain.KeySavedRosters, h.rosters.Count) {
                return
        }
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        r, err := h.rosters.SaveAsRoster(c.Request.Context(), owner, owner, c.Param("id"), b.Name)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusCreated, r)
}

package server

import (
        "context"
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/entitlements"
)

// entitlementPrefix selects every Initiative key in the catalog.
const entitlementPrefix = "initiative."

// EntitlementItem is one key as the caller holds it. Value is a bool for flags and an integer
// for limits. Usage is the caller's count against a limit; it is omitted for flags and for limits
// whose resource this service does not store yet.
type EntitlementItem struct {
        Key   string `json:"key"`
        Type  string `json:"type"`
        Value any    `json:"value"`
        Usage *int64 `json:"usage,omitempty"`
}

type entitlementHandlers struct {
        svc   Services
        usage map[string]func(ctx context.Context, owner string) (int64, error)
}

func setupEntitlementHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, s Services) {
        h := entitlementHandlers{svc: s, usage: map[string]func(context.Context, string) (int64, error){
                "initiative.theme_slots":      s.Themes.Count,
                "initiative.saved_rosters":    s.Rosters.Count,
                "initiative.saved_encounters": s.Encounters.Count,
        }}
        g.GET("/entitlements", h.list)
}

// list shows the caller's Initiative entitlements with usage so clients can render state and
// upgrade prompts. Clients never use it to decide anything; enforcement stays in the handlers
// that create things.
func (h entitlementHandlers) list(c *gin.Context) {
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        results, err := h.svc.Entitlements.Prefix(c.Request.Context(), owner, authz.Token(c), entitlementPrefix)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        if len(results) > 0 {
                if uc, ok := checkerFor(c, h.svc.Entitlements).(userChecker); ok && uc.record != nil {
                        fresh := make([]entitlements.Result, 0, len(results))
                        for _, r := range results {
                                if !r.Stale && !r.Degraded {
                                        fresh = append(fresh, r)
                                }
                        }
                        uc.remember(c.Request.Context(), fresh...)
                }
        }
        items := make([]EntitlementItem, 0, len(results))
        for _, r := range results {
                it := EntitlementItem{Key: r.Key, Type: r.Type}
                if r.Type == "flag" {
                        it.Value = r.Flag
                } else {
                        it.Value = r.Limit
                }
                if count, ok := h.usage[r.Key]; ok {
                        n, err := count(c.Request.Context(), owner)
                        if err != nil {
                                writeError(c, err, nil)
                                return
                        }
                        it.Usage = &n
                }
                items = append(items, it)
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": items})
}

package server

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "log/slog"
        "net/http"
        "strings"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/entitlements"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/events/stream"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/relations"
)

// ErrorBody is the typed error response: a stable code, a safe message, the correlation ID, and
// for entitlement denials the key and limit. Current carries the stored record on a 409.
type ErrorBody struct {
        Code          string `json:"code"`
        Message       string `json:"message"`
        CorrelationID string `json:"correlation_id,omitempty"`
        Key           string `json:"key,omitempty"`
        Limit         *int   `json:"limit,omitempty"`
        Current       any    `json:"current,omitempty"`
}

// writeError maps err to a status and typed body. current is returned with revision conflicts.
// Unrecognized errors are logged and reported as an opaque 500.
func writeError(c *gin.Context, err error, current any) {
        if errors.Is(context.Cause(c.Request.Context()), errCredentialRevoked) {
                err = errCredentialRevoked // the request was cut short by the credential's end, whatever the store reported
        }
        body, status := classifyError(err)
        body.CorrelationID = CorrelationID(c)
        if status == http.StatusConflict && body.Code == "revision_conflict" {
                body.Current = current
        }
        if status >= http.StatusInternalServerError && status != http.StatusServiceUnavailable {
                slog.ErrorContext(c.Request.Context(), "request failed", "error", err, "correlation_id", body.CorrelationID)
        }
        if body.Code == "membership_sync_pending" || body.Code == "rate_limited" || body.Code == "timeout" || body.Code == "shutting_down" || body.Code == "too_many_streams" || body.Code == "streams_unavailable" {
                c.Header("Retry-After", "5")
        }
        c.AbortWithStatusJSON(status, body)
}

func classifyError(err error) (ErrorBody, int) {
        var ee *domain.EntitlementError
        var er *domain.EntitlementRequiredError
        var he *data.HealthError
        switch {
        case errors.As(err, &he):
                // Typed validation failure (initiative-api-resources, "Typed error responses"): the code is
                // stable and the key names the offending health field, so a client can mark it.
                if he.Unknown {
                        return ErrorBody{Code: "unknown_field", Message: "That health field is not defined by the game system.", Key: he.Field}, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity
                }
                return ErrorBody{Code: "field_out_of_range", Message: "That health value is outside the allowed range for the game system.", Key: he.Field}, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity
        case errors.As(err, &ee):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "entitlement_limit_reached", Message: "Your plan's limit for this item has been reached.", Key: ee.Key, Limit: &ee.Limit}, http.StatusForbidden
        case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrReadOnly):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "packaged_theme_read_only", Message: "Packaged themes cannot be changed. Create a custom theme from it instead."}, http.StatusForbidden
        case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrCredentialsClosed):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "credentials_unavailable", Message: "Integration credentials are temporarily unavailable."}, http.StatusServiceUnavailable
        case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrOwnerLimitsUnknown):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "owner_entitlements_pending", Message: "The session owner has not connected yet. Try again after they do."}, http.StatusServiceUnavailable
        case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrSyncPending):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "membership_sync_pending", Message: "Your change was saved, but access is still being updated. Repeat the request in a moment."}, http.StatusServiceUnavailable
        case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrLimitsNotSynced):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "entitlements_unavailable", Message: "Your plan limits have not been synced yet. Open Initiative once with your own sign-in, then try again."}, http.StatusServiceUnavailable
        case errors.Is(err, relations.ErrDenied):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "forbidden", Message: "You may not do that."}, http.StatusForbidden
        case errors.Is(err, relations.ErrRejected):
                // A malformed request is our bug and retrying cannot help; not 503. It falls to the opaque
                // 500 and writeError logs it.
                return ErrorBody{Code: "internal", Message: "Something went wrong."}, http.StatusInternalServerError
        case errors.Is(err, relations.ErrConflict):
                // The member record is already saved, and the reconciler writes tuples from Mongo through
                // the internal path, which the 200-tuple cap does not cover, so a refused member is let in
                // on the next pass. Accepted: the members_per_session entitlement caps a session at 20
                // members, and only a direct caller of auth-api can reach the tuple cap.
                return ErrorBody{Code: "session_full", Message: "The session has reached its member limit."}, http.StatusConflict
        case errors.Is(err, relations.ErrLimited):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "rate_limited", Message: "Too many requests. Try again shortly."}, http.StatusTooManyRequests
        case errors.Is(err, relations.ErrUnavailable):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "authorization_unavailable", Message: "Session access cannot be checked right now. Try again shortly."}, http.StatusServiceUnavailable
        case errors.Is(err, relations.ErrUnauthorized):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "unauthorized", Message: "Your session is not valid."}, http.StatusUnauthorized
        case errors.Is(err, entitlements.ErrUnavailable):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "entitlements_unavailable", Message: "Entitlements are temporarily unavailable. Try again shortly."}, http.StatusServiceUnavailable
        case errors.Is(err, errCredentialRevoked):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "unauthorized", Message: "This credential has been revoked."}, http.StatusUnauthorized
        case errors.Is(err, entitlements.ErrUnauthorized):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "unauthorized", Message: "Your session is not valid."}, http.StatusUnauthorized
        case errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "timeout", Message: "The request took too long. Try again shortly."}, http.StatusServiceUnavailable
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrStreamUnavailable):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "streams_unavailable", Message: "Event streams cannot be opened right now. Try again shortly."}, http.StatusServiceUnavailable
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrStreamLimit):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "too_many_streams", Message: "You have too many open event streams. Close one and try again."}, http.StatusTooManyRequests
        case errors.Is(err, stream.ErrShuttingDown):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "shutting_down", Message: "This server is restarting. Reconnect shortly."}, http.StatusServiceUnavailable
        case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrTicketInvalid):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "ticket_invalid", Message: "The stream ticket is not valid. Request a new one."}, http.StatusUnauthorized
        case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrChangeStale):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "change_request_stale", Message: strings.TrimPrefix(err.Error(), domain.ErrChangeStale.Error()+": ")}, http.StatusConflict
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrNotFound):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "not_found", Message: "Not found."}, http.StatusNotFound
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrLocked), errors.As(err, &er):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "entitlement_required", Message: "This needs an entitlement your account does not currently hold.", Key: domain.KeyGameSystemsExtra}, http.StatusForbidden
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrInactive):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "item_inactive", Message: "This item is over your plan's limit. You can still view or delete it; choose which items stay active to use it again."}, http.StatusForbidden
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrNotMember):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "forbidden", Message: "You are not a member of this session."}, http.StatusForbidden
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrForbidden):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "forbidden", Message: "You do not own this record."}, http.StatusForbidden
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrRevisionConflict):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "revision_conflict", Message: "The record changed since you loaded it."}, http.StatusConflict
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrConflict):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "conflict", Message: "The request conflicts with an existing record."}, http.StatusConflict
        case errors.Is(err, data.ErrInvalid):
                return ErrorBody{Code: "invalid", Message: strings.TrimPrefix(err.Error(), data.ErrInvalid.Error()+": ")}, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity
        }
        return ErrorBody{Code: "internal", Message: "Something went wrong."}, http.StatusInternalServerError
}

// badRequest reports a malformed request body or parameter.
func badRequest(c *gin.Context, msg string) {
        c.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusBadRequest, ErrorBody{Code: "bad_request", Message: msg, CorrelationID: CorrelationID(c)})
}

package server

import (
        "log/slog"
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/entitlements"
)

type gameSystemHandlers struct {
        systems *data.SystemRepo
        ent     *entitlements.Client
}

// setupGameSystemHandlers registers the read-only game-system resource. It sits in the group that
// accepts integration credentials (read scope).
func setupGameSystemHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, systems *data.SystemRepo, ent *entitlements.Client) {
        h := gameSystemHandlers{systems, ent}
        g.GET("/game-systems", privateNoStore, h.list)
        g.GET("/game-systems/:id", privateNoStore, h.get)
}

// privateNoStore keeps a shared cache from serving one caller's entitlement-dependent answer to
// another.
func privateNoStore(c *gin.Context) { c.Header("Cache-Control", "private, no-store") }

// gameSystemView is what a client receives: the definition and nothing about how it is stored
// (no revision, hash or audit fields).
type gameSystemView struct {
        ID      string              `json:"id"`
        Name    string              `json:"name"`
        Family  string              `json:"family"`
        Aliases []string            `json:"aliases"`
        Gated   bool                `json:"gated"`
        Fields  []data.TrackerField `json:"fields"`
}

func viewOf(s data.GameSystem) gameSystemView {
        aliases, fields := s.Aliases, s.Fields
        if aliases == nil {
                aliases = []string{}
        }
        if fields == nil {
                fields = []data.TrackerField{}
        }
        return gameSystemView{ID: s.ID, Name: s.Name, Family: s.Family, Aliases: aliases, Gated: s.Gated, Fields: fields}
}

// gameSystemFields are the names ?fields= may select.
var gameSystemFields = []string{"id", "name", "family", "aliases", "gated", "fields"}

// entitled reports whether the caller holds initiative.game_systems_extra, under policy. degraded
// is true when the answer is not a fresh known value (outage, no value, or an expired cached one).
// A list passes FailOpen: anything short of a known "yes" reads as "no" and the caller sees No
// System rather than an error. A get of a gated system passes FailClosed: an outage is the typed
// 503, with no stale fallback, so a revoked or newly bought flag is never answered from cache.
func (h gameSystemHandlers) entitled(c *gin.Context, policy entitlements.Policy) (flag, degraded bool, err error) {
        res, err := checkerFor(c, h.ent).Check(c.Request.Context(), domain.KeyGameSystemsExtra, policy)
        switch {
        case err != nil:
                return false, true, err
        case res.Degraded:
                return false, true, entitlements.ErrUnavailable
        }
        return res.Flag, res.Stale, nil
}

func (h gameSystemHandlers) list(c *gin.Context) {
        q, ok := parseQuery(c, map[string]string{"gated": typeBool})
        if !ok || !checkFields(c, q.Fields, gameSystemFields) {
                return
        }
        fields := q.Fields
        q.Fields = nil
        entitled, degraded, err := h.entitled(c, entitlements.FailOpen)
        if err != nil {
                slog.WarnContext(c.Request.Context(), "entitlement unknown for the game-system list; listing No System only", "error", err)
        }
        if degraded {
                c.Header("X-Entitlements-Degraded", "true")
        }
        page, err := h.systems.ListAvailable(c.Request.Context(), q, entitled)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        items := make([]map[string]any, 0, len(page.Items))
        for _, s := range page.Items {
                items = append(items, projectMap(toMap(viewOf(s)), fields, "id"))
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": items, "next_cursor": page.NextCursor})
}

// get resolves an ID or an alias (a Foundry system ID, for one). A gated system is refused to a
// caller without the flag with the typed entitlement error; when the flag cannot be read at all
// the answer is the typed 503, never a guess.
func (h gameSystemHandlers) get(c *gin.Context) {
        fields, ok := fieldsOf(c, gameSystemFields)
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        id := c.Param("id")
        if err := data.CheckText("game system id", id, data.MaxNameLen); err != nil {
                writeError(c, data.ErrNotFound, nil)
                return
        }
        sys, err := h.systems.Get(c.Request.Context(), id)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        if sys.Gated {
                entitled, _, err := h.entitled(c, entitlements.FailClosed)
                if err != nil {
                        writeError(c, err, nil)
                        return
                }
                if !entitled {
                        writeError(c, &domain.EntitlementRequiredError{Key: domain.KeyGameSystemsExtra}, nil)
                        return
                }
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, projectMap(toMap(viewOf(sys)), fields, "id"))
}

// Package server wires the HTTP routes and middleware.
package server

import (
        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/constants"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/entitlements"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/events/stream"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/relations"
)

// Services are the dependencies the resource handlers need.
type Services struct {
        // Ready is the deep dependency check behind /status/health; nil reports healthy.
        Ready        *Readiness
        Authz        *authz.Client
        Entitlements *entitlements.Client
        // OwnerLimits records the owner's entitlement values for integration credentials, which carry
        // no user bearer. Optional; without it a credential's limit checks fail closed.
        OwnerLimits *data.OwnerEntitlements
        Themes      *domain.Themes
        Credentials *domain.Credentials
        Limits      *domain.Limits
        Locks       *domain.Locks
        Rosters     *domain.Rosters
        Encounters  *domain.Encounters
        Sessions    *domain.Sessions
        // ChangeRequests handles player-submitted combatant changes; it needs Encounters and Sessions.
        ChangeRequests *domain.ChangeRequests
        Systems        *data.SystemRepo
        // Relations answers session authorization checks (PADR-0035). Without it every session
        // request fails closed with 503.
        Relations *relations.Client
        // Streams and Hub serve the event stream; without both there is no /streams route. Stream
        // holds its transport settings.
        Streams *domain.Streams
        Hub     *stream.Hub
        // NotificationPrefs holds each member's muted notice types per session.
        NotificationPrefs *domain.NotificationPrefs
        Stream            StreamOptions
        // Leases counts a user's open streams across pods; without it the per-user cap is not applied.
        Leases *data.StreamLeases
}

// ProtectedGroup returns a route group whose every route requires a bearer token that auth-api
// verifies (signature, expiry, iss, aud) for this service and whose user holds one of roles
// (default: RoleUser). Missing or invalid tokens get 401, a missing role gets 403. Routes added
// to the group inherit this; handlers never repeat the check. The caller's own token is the only
// credential, per PADR-0037 (no service tokens), and a client application identity alone is a 401.
func ProtectedGroup(r *gin.Engine, client *authz.Client, creds *domain.Credentials, roles ...string) *gin.RouterGroup {
        if len(roles) == 0 {
                roles = []string{authz.RoleUser}
        }
        return r.Group("", typedErrors(), authenticate(creds, authz.RequireAnyRole(client, constants.ServiceName, roles...)))
}

// SetupHandlers registers every route group on r.
func SetupHandlers(r *gin.Engine, s Services) {
        setupStatusHandlers(r, s.Ready)

        g := ProtectedGroup(r, s.Authz, s.Credentials)
        g.Use(limitBody(), withOrigin(), withOwnerRecord(s.OwnerLimits), withChecker(s.Entitlements), withGate(s.Relations))
        if s.Limits != nil || s.Locks != nil {
                g.Use(reconcileLimits(s))
        }
        u := g.Group("", userOnly)
        setupThemeHandlers(u, s.Themes, s.Entitlements)
        setupEntitlementHandlers(u, s)
        setupCredentialHandlers(u, s)
        setupActiveItemHandlers(u, s)
        if s.Sessions != nil {
                setupSessionHandlers(u, s.Sessions)
        }
        if s.NotificationPrefs != nil {
                setupNotificationPrefHandlers(u, s.NotificationPrefs)
        }
        if s.Encounters != nil && s.Sessions != nil {
                setupEncounterViewHandlers(u, s.Encounters, s.Sessions)
        }
        if s.ChangeRequests != nil {
                setupChangeRequestHandlers(u, s.ChangeRequests)
        }
        if s.Encounters != nil {
                setupEncounterHandlers(g, s.Encounters, s.Rosters, s.Entitlements)
        }
        if s.Rosters != nil {
                setupRosterHandlers(g, s.Rosters, s.Entitlements)
        }
        if s.Systems != nil {
                setupGameSystemHandlers(g, s.Systems, s.Entitlements)
        }
        setupRefHandlers(g, s.Encounters)
        if s.Streams != nil && s.Hub != nil {
                setupStreamHandlers(r, u, s)
        }
        if s.Credentials != nil {
                setupAdminCredentialHandlers(ProtectedGroup(r, s.Authz, nil, authz.RoleAdmin), s.Credentials)
        }
}

// withGate gives the data layer the relation checker, and the domain auth-api's session surface,
// both with the caller's own bearer token (PADR-0037), so session data can be authorized and
// membership tuples written per request. A nil client leaves ctx without a gate, which
// makes session data fail closed.
func withGate(c *relations.Client) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(ctx *gin.Context) {
                if c != nil {
                        token := authz.Token(ctx)
                        rc := data.WithGate(ctx.Request.Context(), c, token)
                        ctx.Request = ctx.Request.WithContext(domain.WithAuthority(rc, c, token))
                }
                ctx.Next()
        }
}

package server

import (
        "log/slog"
        "net/http"
        "strings"
        "sync"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/entitlements"
)

// reconcileEvery bounds how often one owner's limits are re-read on ordinary requests. The
// entitlements client caches each key for its own TTL, so a changed limit applies within the
// larger of the two.
var reconcileEvery = 30 * time.Second

// reconcileLimits applies the owner's current limits (inactive items beyond a reduced limit,
// reactivation on a raise) on requests carrying the owner's own bearer: that bearer is the only
// way to read their entitlements (PADR-0037), so integration credentials are skipped. A delete or
// a revoke that succeeded reconciles at once, because it freed a place. Failures never fail the
// request.
func reconcileLimits(s Services) gin.HandlerFunc {
        var last sync.Map // owner -> time of the last reconcile
        run := func(c *gin.Context, owner string) {
                last.Store(owner, time.Now())
                chk := checkerFor(c, s.Entitlements)
                if s.Limits != nil {
                        if err := s.Limits.Reconcile(c.Request.Context(), owner, chk); err != nil {
                                slog.WarnContext(c.Request.Context(), "limit reconcile failed", "error", err)
                        }
                }
                if s.Locks != nil {
                        if err := s.Locks.Reconcile(c.Request.Context(), owner, chk); err != nil {
                                slog.WarnContext(c.Request.Context(), "game-system lock reconcile failed", "error", err)
                        }
                }
        }
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                // The handler (and the authenticate middleware before it) set the viewer, so read it first.
                owner := authz.Viewer(c)
                _, viaCredential := c.Get(credentialContextKey)
                if owner == "" || viaCredential {
                        c.Next()
                        return
                }
                // Before the handler, so the response already reflects the current limits.
                if prev, ok := last.Load(owner); !ok || time.Since(prev.(time.Time)) >= reconcileEvery {
                        run(c, owner)
                }
                c.Next()
                // A delete or revoke freed a place; an inactive item takes it now.
                if c.Writer.Status() < http.StatusMultipleChoices &&
                        (c.Request.Method == http.MethodDelete || strings.HasSuffix(c.FullPath(), "/revoke")) {
                        run(c, owner)
                }
        }
}

// withChecker puts the caller's entitlement checker on the request context for domain rules that
// check a flag mid-operation, such as choosing a gated game system.
func withChecker(ent *entitlements.Client) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                c.Request = c.Request.WithContext(domain.WithChecker(c.Request.Context(), checkerFor(c, ent)))
                c.Next()
        }
}

type activeItemBody struct {
        IDs []string `json:"ids"`
}

func setupActiveItemHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, s Services) {
        if s.Limits == nil {
                return
        }
        g.PUT("/entitlements/active/:kind", func(c *gin.Context) {
                var b activeItemBody
                if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                        return
                }
                if b.IDs == nil {
                        badRequest(c, "request body must be JSON with an ids array")
                        return
                }
                active, err := s.Limits.Choose(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("kind"), b.IDs, checkerFor(c, s.Entitlements))
                if err != nil {
                        writeError(c, err, nil)
                        return
                }
                c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"kind": c.Param("kind"), "active": active})
        })
}

package server

import (
        "context"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/entitlements"
)

// requireRoom refuses a create that would exceed the caller's entitled count for key. Inactive
// items count toward the total owned, and an entitlements outage fails closed (503), while edits
// of existing items never come here (initiative-entitlement-gating, "Limit reached", "Creating
// while over the limit", "Service unavailable behavior"). It writes the error and returns false.
func requireRoom(c *gin.Context, ent *entitlements.Client, key string, owned func(ctx context.Context, owner string) (int64, error)) bool {
        owner := authz.Viewer(c)
        res, err := checkerFor(c, ent).Check(c.Request.Context(), key, entitlements.FailClosed)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return false
        }
        n, err := owned(c.Request.Context(), owner)
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return false
        }
        if n >= int64(res.Limit) {
                writeError(c, &domain.EntitlementError{Key: key, Limit: res.Limit}, nil)
                return false
        }
        return true
}

package server

import (
        "context"
        "log/slog"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        sloggin "github.com/samber/slog-gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/constants"
        "go.opentelemetry.io/contrib/instrumentation/github.com/gin-gonic/gin/otelgin"
)

// Middleware is the chain every request runs first, in the order that matters: correlation, then
// ticket redaction (before anything that logs the URL), then the access log, recovery and tracing.
// main and the tests build the router from this one list, so the order cannot drift apart.
func Middleware(accessLog *slog.Logger, requestTimeout time.Duration) []gin.HandlerFunc {
        return []gin.HandlerFunc{
                Correlation(),
                RedactTicket(),
                sloggin.New(accessLog),
                gin.Recovery(),
                RequestDeadline(requestTimeout),
                otelgin.Middleware(constants.ServiceName),
        }
}

// RequestDeadline bounds every request but the event stream's upgrade (a long-lived connection by
// design) to d, so a slow dependency cannot hold a request, and the goroutine behind it, open. A
// store call that runs past it returns context.DeadlineExceeded, which the error mapper reports as
// 503 timeout.
func RequestDeadline(d time.Duration) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                if d <= 0 || isStreamUpgrade(c.Request) {
                        c.Next()
                        return
                }
                ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(c.Request.Context(), d)
                defer cancel()
                c.Request = c.Request.WithContext(ctx)
                c.Next()
        }
}

package server

import (
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
)

type prefHandlers struct{ svc *domain.NotificationPrefs }

// setupNotificationPrefHandlers registers a member's per-session notification mutes. They sit in
// the user-only group: preferences belong to a person.
func setupNotificationPrefHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, svc *domain.NotificationPrefs) {
        h := prefHandlers{svc}
        g.GET("/sessions/:id/notification-preferences", h.get)
        g.PUT("/sessions/:id/notification-preferences", h.put)
}

type prefBody struct {
        Muted []string `json:"muted"`
}

func writePrefs(c *gin.Context, muted []string, err error) {
        if err != nil {
                writeError(c, err, nil)
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"muted": muted, "types": data.NoticeTypes})
}

func (h prefHandlers) get(c *gin.Context) {
        muted, err := h.svc.Get(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("id"))
        writePrefs(c, muted, err)
}

func (h prefHandlers) put(c *gin.Context) {
        var b prefBody
        if !bindJSON(c, &b) {
                return
        }
        muted, err := h.svc.Set(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Param("id"), b.Muted)
        writePrefs(c, muted, err)
}

package server

import (
        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
)

// clientIDHeader carries the opaque id a client picks for itself, so it can recognise the echo of
// its own writes on the event stream.
const clientIDHeader = "X-Initiative-Client-Id"

// withOrigin records who is writing, on the request context, for the event each commit emits. A
// client id that is not [A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,64} is ignored, never rejected.
func withOrigin() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                o := data.Origin{Kind: data.OriginUser, ActorID: authz.Viewer(c)}
                if _, viaCredential := c.Get(credentialContextKey); viaCredential {
                        o.Kind = data.OriginIntegration
                }
                if id := c.GetHeader(clientIDHeader); data.ValidClientID(id) {
                        o.ClientID = id
                }
                c.Request = c.Request.WithContext(data.WithOrigin(c.Request.Context(), o))
                c.Next()
        }
}

package server

import (
        "strconv"
        "strings"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
)

// Field value types for filter coercion. A query string carries only text, so the resource says
// which of its filterable fields are not strings.
const (
        typeBool = "bool"
        typeInt  = "int"
)

// parseQuery reads the list conventions (PADR-0030) from the request: limit, cursor, sort
// (comma-separated field names, a leading "-" for descending), fields (projection), and
// filter[field]=value, filter[field][in]=a,b, filter[field][contains]=text. It checks shapes only;
// which fields may be filtered, sorted or projected is decided by the resource's data.Spec in the
// data layer, which rejects anything else. types names the non-string filterable fields. It writes
// the 400 and returns false on a malformed value.
func parseQuery(c *gin.Context, types map[string]string) (data.Query, bool) {
        q := data.Query{Cursor: c.Query("cursor")}
        if v := c.Query("limit"); v != "" {
                n, err := strconv.Atoi(v)
                if err != nil || n < 1 {
                        badRequest(c, "limit must be a positive integer")
                        return q, false
                }
                q.Limit = n
        }
        for _, f := range splitList(c.Query("sort")) {
                q.Sort = append(q.Sort, data.SortKey{Field: strings.TrimPrefix(f, "-"), Desc: strings.HasPrefix(f, "-")})
        }
        q.Fields = splitList(c.Query("fields"))
        for key, values := range c.Request.URL.Query() {
                if !strings.HasPrefix(key, "filter") {
                        continue
                }
                field, op, ok := filterKey(key)
                if !ok {
                        badRequest(c, "unrecognized filter parameter "+key+": use filter[field], filter[field][in] or filter[field][contains]")
                        return q, false
                }
                f := data.Filter{Field: field, Op: op}
                for _, raw := range values {
                        parts := []string{raw}
                        if op == data.OpIn {
                                parts = splitList(raw)
                        }
                        for _, p := range parts {
                                v, ok := coerce(p, types[field])
                                if !ok {
                                        badRequest(c, "filter "+field+" has a value of the wrong type")
                                        return q, false
                                }
                                f.Values = append(f.Values, v)
                        }
                }
                q.Filters = append(q.Filters, f)
        }
        return q, true
}

// filterKey parses filter[field] and filter[field][op].
func filterKey(key string) (field string, op data.Op, ok bool) {
        rest, found := strings.CutPrefix(key, "filter[")
        if !found {
                return "", "", false
        }
        field, rest, found = strings.Cut(rest, "]")
        if !found || field == "" {
                return "", "", false
        }
        switch rest {
        case "":
                return field, data.OpEq, true
        case "[in]":
                return field, data.OpIn, true
        case "[contains]":
                return field, data.OpContains, true
        }
        return "", "", false
}

func coerce(s, typ string) (any, bool) {
        switch typ {
        case typeBool:
                b, err := strconv.ParseBool(s)
                return b, err == nil
        case typeInt:
                n, err := strconv.ParseInt(s, 10, 64)
                return n, err == nil
        }
        return s, true
}

func splitList(s string) []string {
        var out []string
        for _, p := range strings.Split(s, ",") {
                if p = strings.TrimSpace(p); p != "" {
                        out = append(out, p)
                }
        }
        return out
}

package server

import (
        "strings"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/gomodule/redigo/redis"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/api-core.go/ratelimit"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
)

// resolvedCredentialKey holds the credential the limiter step already looked up, so authenticate
// does not query it twice.
const resolvedCredentialKey = "ratelimit.credential"

// RateLimit returns the per-client limiter (PADR-0036) preceded by a step that sets its client key.
// The shared limiter keys on X-API-Key, else the IP, and both are caller-controlled, so that step
// never lets the caller's header through:
//   - a bearer that resolves to a live integration credential is keyed by the credential's ID,
//     so credentials are distinct clients;
//   - everything else, including a made-up "ic_" secret, is keyed by IP.
func RateLimit(pool *redis.Pool, opts ratelimit.Options, creds *domain.Credentials) []gin.HandlerFunc {
        return []gin.HandlerFunc{typedErrors(), clientKey(creds), ratelimit.Middleware(pool, opts)}
}

func clientKey(creds *domain.Credentials) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                c.Request.Header.Del("X-API-Key")
                secret, _ := strings.CutPrefix(c.GetHeader("Authorization"), "Bearer ")
                if creds != nil && strings.HasPrefix(secret, domain.SecretPrefix) {
                        // Any failure (unknown, revoked, store error) leaves the IP key; authenticate then
                        // rejects or reports it.
                        if cred, err := creds.Authenticate(c.Request.Context(), secret); err == nil {
                                c.Set(resolvedCredentialKey, cred)
                                c.Request.Header.Set("X-API-Key", "cred:"+cred.ID)
                        }
                }
                c.Next()
        }
}

func resolvedCredential(c *gin.Context) (data.IntegrationCredential, bool) {
        v, ok := c.Get(resolvedCredentialKey)
        if !ok {
                return data.IntegrationCredential{}, false
        }
        cred, ok := v.(data.IntegrationCredential)
        return cred, ok
}

package server

import (
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
)

// setupRefHandlers serves the integration lookup of the caller's combatants by external reference.
func setupRefHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, enc *domain.Encounters) {
        g.GET("/combatants", func(c *gin.Context) {
                items, err := enc.FindByExternalRef(c.Request.Context(), authz.Viewer(c), c.Query("ref_key"), c.Query("ref_value"))
                if err != nil {
                        writeError(c, err, nil)
                        return
                }
                c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": items})
        })
}

package server

import (
        "strconv"
        "strings"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

// revisionOf reads the revision a write is based on: If-Match (`"r3"` or `r3`, the form the
// encounter ETag takes), else the `revision` query parameter, else bodyRevision (the `revision` field
// of a JSON body, 0 when absent). A write without one is a 400, so a client can never overwrite
// blindly (PADR "Revisioned writes"). It writes the 400 and returns false when none is usable.
func revisionOf(c *gin.Context, bodyRevision int64) (int64, bool) {
        if h := strings.TrimSpace(c.GetHeader("If-Match")); h != "" {
                h = strings.TrimPrefix(strings.Trim(h, `"`), "r")
                if n, err := strconv.ParseInt(h, 10, 64); err == nil && n >= 1 {
                        return n, true
                }
                badRequest(c, "If-Match must be the revision as returned in ETag")
                return 0, false
        }
        if v := c.Query("revision"); v != "" {
                if n, err := strconv.ParseInt(v, 10, 64); err == nil && n >= 1 {
                        return n, true
                }
                badRequest(c, "revision must be a positive integer")
                return 0, false
        }
        if bodyRevision >= 1 {
                return bodyRevision, true
        }
        badRequest(c, "the current revision is required: send If-Match, a revision query parameter, or a revision field")
        return 0, false
}

package server

import (
        "errors"
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/authz-client.go/authz"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/data"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/domain"
        "github.com/sweetrpg/initiative-api/entitlements"
)

type rosterHandlers struct {
        svc *domain.Rosters
        ent *entitlements.Client
}

// setupRosterHandlers registers rosters and their characters. They sit in the group that accepts
// integration credentials (read scope for GET, write scope otherwise) because integrations use the
// same endpoints as first-party clients.
func setupRosterHandlers(g *gin.RouterGroup, svc *domain.Rosters, ent *entitlements.Client) {
        h := rosterHandlers{svc, ent}
        g.GET("/rosters", h.list)
        g.POST("/rosters", h.create)
        g.GET("/rosters/:id", h.get)
        g.PATCH("/rosters/:id", h.update)
        g.DELETE("/rosters/:id", h.remove)
        g.POST("/rosters/:id/characters", h.addCharacter)
        g.PUT("/rosters/:id/characters/:cid", h.replaceCharacter)
        g.DELETE("/rosters/:id/characters/:cid", h.removeCharacter)
}

type characterBody struct {
        Name         string            `json:"name"`
        Kind         string            `json:"kind"`
        Notes        string            `json:"notes"`
        Health       map[string]int    `json:"health"`
        ExternalRefs map[string]string `json:"external_refs"`
}

func (b characterBody) character() data.Character {
