                if ok {
                        c.docs = append(c.docs[:i], c.docs[i+1:]...)
                        return nil
                }
        }
        return store.ErrNotFound
}

func (c *Collection) CountDocuments(_ context.Context, filter bson.D) (int64, error) {
        c.mu.Lock()
        defer c.mu.Unlock()
        var n int64
        for _, d := range c.docs {
                ok, err := match(d, filter)
                if err != nil {
                        return 0, err
                }
                if ok {
                        n++
                }
        }
        return n, nil
}

func (c *Collection) EnsureIndexes(_ context.Context, specs []store.IndexSpec) error {
        c.mu.Lock()
        defer c.mu.Unlock()
        c.Indexes = append([]store.IndexSpec(nil), specs...)
        return nil
}

// violatesUnique checks _id and every unique index (honoring partial filters) against m,
// ignoring the document at index skip.
func (c *Collection) violatesUnique(m bson.M, skip int) bool {
        for i, d := range c.docs {
                if i == skip {
                        continue
                }
                if reflect.DeepEqual(d["_id"], m["_id"]) {
                        return true
                }
                for _, ix := range c.Indexes {
                        if ix.Unique && sameKey(ix, d, m) {
                                return true
                        }
                }
        }
        return false
}

func sameKey(ix store.IndexSpec, a, b bson.M) bool {
        for _, d := range []bson.M{a, b} {
                if len(ix.Partial) > 0 {
                        if ok, _ := match(d, ix.Partial); !ok {
                                return false
                        }
                }
        }
        for _, k := range ix.Keys {
                av, bv := a[k.Key], b[k.Key]
                if arr, ok := av.(bson.A); ok { // multikey: any shared element collides
                        return anyShared(arr, bv)
                }
                if !reflect.DeepEqual(norm(av), norm(bv)) {
                        return false
                }
        }
        return true
}

func anyShared(a bson.A, b any) bool {
        barr, ok := b.(bson.A)
        if !ok {
                return false
        }
        for _, x := range a {
                for _, y := range barr {
                        if reflect.DeepEqual(norm(x), norm(y)) {
                                return true
                        }
                }
        }
        return false
}

func cloneM(m bson.M) bson.M {
        out := make(bson.M, len(m))
        for k, v := range m {
                out[k] = v
        }
        return out
}

func applyUpdate(d bson.M, update bson.D) error {
        for _, op := range update {
                fields, ok := op.Value.(bson.D)
                if !ok {
                        return fmt.Errorf("fake: update operand for %s must be bson.D", op.Key)
                }
                for _, f := range fields {
                        switch op.Key {
                        case "$set":
                                m, err := toM(bson.D{{Key: "v", Value: f.Value}})
                                if err != nil {
                                        return err
                                }
                                setPath(d, f.Key, m["v"])
                        case "$unset":
                                delete(d, f.Key)
                        case "$inc":
                                cur, _ := toFloat(norm(d[f.Key]))
                                inc, _ := toFloat(norm(f.Value))
                                d[f.Key] = int64(cur + inc)
                        default:
                                return fmt.Errorf("fake: unsupported update operator %s", op.Key)
                        }
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// setPath sets a dotted key (entries.k) inside nested documents, copying each level it touches
// so a stored document is never mutated in place.
func setPath(d bson.M, key string, v any) {
        head, rest, nested := strings.Cut(key, ".")
        if !nested {
                d[key] = v
                return
        }
        child := bson.M{}
        if cur, ok := d[head].(bson.M); ok {
                child = cloneM(cur)
        }
        setPath(child, rest, v)
        d[head] = child
}

// norm maps stored and filter values to comparable canonical forms.
func norm(v any) any {
        switch x := v.(type) {
        case time.Time:
                return int64(primitive.NewDateTimeFromTime(x))
        case primitive.DateTime:
                return int64(x)
        case int:
                return int64(x)
        case int32:
                return int64(x)
        case float64:
                if x == float64(int64(x)) {
                        return int64(x)
                }
        case *time.Time:
                if x == nil {
                        return nil
                }
                return norm(*x)
        }
        return v
}

func toFloat(v any) (float64, bool) {
        switch x := v.(type) {
        case int64:
                return float64(x), true
        case float64:
                return x, true
        }
        return 0, false
}

func cmp(a, b any) (int, bool) {
        a, b = norm(a), norm(b)
        if af, ok := toFloat(a); ok {
                if bf, ok := toFloat(b); ok {
                        switch {
                        case af < bf:
                                return -1, true
                        case af > bf:
                                return 1, true
                        }
                        return 0, true
                }
        }
        as, aok := a.(string)
        bs, bok := b.(string)
        if aok && bok {
                switch {
                case as < bs:
                        return -1, true
                case as > bs:
                        return 1, true
                }
                return 0, true
        }
        return 0, false
}

func less(a, b bson.M, sortD bson.D) bool {
        for _, k := range sortD {
                c, _ := cmp(a[k.Key], b[k.Key])
                if c == 0 {
                        continue
                }
                if dir, _ := toFloat(norm(k.Value)); dir < 0 {
                        return c > 0
                }
                return c < 0
        }
        return false
}

func match(d bson.M, filter bson.D) (bool, error) {
        for _, e := range filter {
                ok, err := matchElem(d, e)
                if err != nil || !ok {
                        return false, err
                }
        }
        return true, nil
}

func matchElem(d bson.M, e bson.E) (bool, error) {
        switch e.Key {
        case "$and", "$or":
                clauses, ok := e.Value.(bson.A)
                if !ok {
                        return false, fmt.Errorf("fake: %s needs bson.A", e.Key)
                }
                if len(clauses) == 0 {
                        return false, fmt.Errorf("fake: %s argument must be a non-empty array", e.Key)
                }
                for _, cl := range clauses {
                        sub, ok := cl.(bson.D)
                        if !ok {
                                return false, fmt.Errorf("fake: %s clause must be bson.D", e.Key)
                        }
                        m, err := match(d, sub)
                        if err != nil {
                                return false, err
                        }
                        if m == (e.Key == "$or") {
                                return m, nil
                        }
                }
                return e.Key == "$and", nil
        }
        if strings.Contains(e.Key, ".") {
                return matchPath(d, e)
        }
        if ops, ok := e.Value.(bson.D); ok && len(ops) > 0 && len(ops[0].Key) > 0 && ops[0].Key[0] == '$' {
                return matchOps(d[e.Key], ops)
        }
        return eq(d[e.Key], e.Value), nil
}

// matchPath matches a dotted key (combatants.controller_user_ids) the way Mongo does: the path
// is followed through nested documents and arrays of documents, and the condition holds if any
// value found satisfies it.
func matchPath(d bson.M, e bson.E) (bool, error) {
        for _, have := range valuesAt(d, strings.Split(e.Key, ".")) {
                if ops, ok := e.Value.(bson.D); ok && len(ops) > 0 && len(ops[0].Key) > 0 && ops[0].Key[0] == '$' {
                        m, err := matchOps(have, ops)
                        if err != nil || m {
                                return m, err
                        }
                } else if eq(have, e.Value) {
                        return true, nil
                }
        }
        return false, nil
}

func valuesAt(v any, path []string) []any {
        if len(path) == 0 {
                return []any{v}
        }
        switch x := v.(type) {
        case bson.M:
                if next, ok := x[path[0]]; ok {
                        return valuesAt(next, path[1:])
                }
        case bson.A:
                var out []any
                for _, el := range x {
                        out = append(out, valuesAt(el, path)...)
                }
                return out
        }
        return nil
}

func eq(have, want any) bool {
        if want == nil {
                return have == nil
        }
        if arr, ok := have.(bson.A); ok { // equality against an array field matches any element
                for _, x := range arr {
                        if reflect.DeepEqual(norm(x), norm(want)) {
                                return true
                        }
                }
        }
        return reflect.DeepEqual(norm(have), norm(want))
}

func matchOps(have any, ops bson.D) (bool, error) {
        var pattern, opts string
        for _, o := range ops {
                switch o.Key {
                case "$options":
                        opts, _ = o.Value.(string)
                case "$regex":
                        pattern, _ = o.Value.(string)
                }
        }
        for _, o := range ops {
                ok, err := matchOp(have, o, pattern, opts)
                if err != nil || !ok {
                        return false, err
                }
        }
        return true, nil
}

func matchOp(have any, o bson.E, pattern, opts string) (bool, error) {
        switch o.Key {
        case "$options":
                return true, nil
        case "$ne":
                return !eq(have, o.Value), nil
        case "$in":
                vals, ok := o.Value.(bson.A)
                if !ok {
                        return false, fmt.Errorf("fake: $in needs bson.A")
                }
                for _, v := range vals {
                        if eq(have, v) {
                                return true, nil
                        }
                }
                return false, nil
        case "$regex":
                s, ok := have.(string)
                if !ok {
                        return false, nil
                }
                if opts == "i" {
                        pattern = "(?i)" + pattern
                }
                re, err := regexp.Compile(pattern)
                if err != nil {
                        return false, err
                }
                return re.MatchString(s), nil
        case "$gt", "$gte", "$lt", "$lte":
                c, ok := cmp(have, o.Value)
                if !ok {
                        return false, nil
                }
                return (o.Key == "$gt" && c > 0) || (o.Key == "$gte" && c >= 0) ||
                        (o.Key == "$lt" && c < 0) || (o.Key == "$lte" && c <= 0), nil
        case "$exists":
                want, _ := o.Value.(bool)
                return (have != nil) == want, nil
        }
        return false, fmt.Errorf("fake: unsupported operator %s", o.Key)
}

// wake tells every watcher a document arrived. Callers hold c.mu.
func (c *Collection) wake() {
        for _, w := range c.watchers {
                select {
                case w.ping <- struct{}{}:
                default:
                }
        }
}

// LoseHistory makes the next Watch that asks to resume fail with store.ErrHistoryLost, as a token
// older than the oplog does.
func (c *Collection) LoseHistory() {
        c.mu.Lock()
        c.lostHistory = true
        c.mu.Unlock()
}

// BreakWatchers ends every open Watch cursor with an error, as a dropped change stream does. A
// Watch made after it works again.
func (c *Collection) BreakWatchers() {
        c.mu.Lock()
        defer c.mu.Unlock()
        for _, w := range c.watchers {
                w.broken = true
        }
        c.wake()
}

// Watch tails inserts. The resume token is the number of documents seen, so a resumed cursor
// replays what was inserted while it was down.
func (c *Collection) Watch(_ context.Context, resume bson.Raw) (store.Cursor, error) {
        c.mu.Lock()
        defer c.mu.Unlock()
        if len(resume) > 0 && c.lostHistory {
                c.lostHistory = false
                return nil, store.ErrHistoryLost
        }
        w := &cursor{c: c, ping: make(chan struct{}, 1), pos: len(c.docs)}
        if len(resume) > 0 {
                w.pos = int(resume.Lookup("i").Int32())
        }
        c.watchers = append(c.watchers, w)
        return w, nil
}

type cursor struct {
        c      *Collection
        ping   chan struct{}
        pos    int
        broken bool // guarded by c.mu
        doc    bson.Raw
        err    error
}

func (w *cursor) Next(ctx context.Context) bool {
        for {
                w.c.mu.Lock()
                switch {
                case w.broken:
                        w.err = errors.New("fake: watch broken")
                        w.c.mu.Unlock()
                        return false
                case w.pos < len(w.c.docs):
                        w.doc, w.err = bson.Marshal(w.c.docs[w.pos])
                        w.pos++
                        w.c.mu.Unlock()
                        return w.err == nil
                }
                w.c.mu.Unlock()
                select {
                case <-w.ping:
                case <-ctx.Done():
                        w.err = ctx.Err()
                        return false
                }
        }
}

func (w *cursor) Doc() bson.Raw { return w.doc }
func (w *cursor) Token() bson.Raw {
        t, _ := bson.Marshal(bson.D{{Key: "i", Value: int32(w.pos)}})
        return t
}
func (w *cursor) Err() error                  { return w.err }
func (w *cursor) Close(context.Context) error { return nil }

package store

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "time"

        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/mongo"
        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/mongo/options"
)

// MongoDatabase adapts a *mongo.Database to Database.
type MongoDatabase struct{ DB *mongo.Database }

// Collection returns the named collection.
func (m MongoDatabase) Collection(name string) Collection {
        return mongoCollection{c: m.DB.Collection(name)}
}

type mongoCollection struct{ c *mongo.Collection }

func mapErr(err error) error {
        switch {
        case err == nil:
                return nil
        case errors.Is(err, mongo.ErrNoDocuments):
                return ErrNotFound
        case mongo.IsDuplicateKeyError(err):
                return ErrDuplicate
        }
        return err
}

func (m mongoCollection) InsertOne(ctx context.Context, doc any) error {
        _, err := m.c.InsertOne(ctx, doc)
        return mapErr(err)
}

func (m mongoCollection) FindOne(ctx context.Context, filter bson.D, out any) error {
        _, err := retryRead(ctx, "find_one", func() (struct{}, error) {
                return struct{}{}, m.c.FindOne(ctx, filter).Decode(out)
        })
        return mapErr(err)
}

func (m mongoCollection) Find(ctx context.Context, filter bson.D, opts FindOptions, out any) error {
        fo := options.Find()
        if len(opts.Sort) > 0 {
                fo.SetSort(opts.Sort)
        }
        if len(opts.Projection) > 0 {
                fo.SetProjection(opts.Projection)
        }
        if opts.Limit > 0 {
                fo.SetLimit(opts.Limit)
        }
        _, err := retryRead(ctx, "find", func() (struct{}, error) {
                cur, err := m.c.Find(ctx, filter, fo)
                if err != nil {
                        return struct{}{}, err
                }
                return struct{}{}, cur.All(ctx, out)
        })
        return mapErr(err)
}

func (m mongoCollection) FindOneAndUpdate(ctx context.Context, filter, update bson.D, out any) error {
        opts := options.FindOneAndUpdate().SetReturnDocument(options.After)
        return mapErr(m.c.FindOneAndUpdate(ctx, filter, update, opts).Decode(out))
}

func (m mongoCollection) UpsertOne(ctx context.Context, filter, update bson.D) error {
        _, err := m.c.UpdateOne(ctx, filter, update, options.Update().SetUpsert(true))
        return mapErr(err)
}

func (m mongoCollection) DeleteOne(ctx context.Context, filter bson.D) error {
        res, err := m.c.DeleteOne(ctx, filter)
        if err != nil {
                return mapErr(err)
        }
        if res.DeletedCount == 0 {
                return ErrNotFound
        }
        return nil
}

func (m mongoCollection) CountDocuments(ctx context.Context, filter bson.D) (int64, error) {
        n, err := retryRead(ctx, "count", func() (int64, error) { return m.c.CountDocuments(ctx, filter) })
        return n, mapErr(err)
}

func (m mongoCollection) EnsureIndexes(ctx context.Context, specs []IndexSpec) error {
        models := make([]mongo.IndexModel, 0, len(specs))
        for _, s := range specs {
                o := options.Index().SetName(s.Name).SetUnique(s.Unique)
                if len(s.Partial) > 0 {
                        o.SetPartialFilterExpression(s.Partial)
                }
                if s.ExpireAfter > 0 {
                        o.SetExpireAfterSeconds(int32(s.ExpireAfter / time.Second))
                }
                models = append(models, mongo.IndexModel{Keys: s.Keys, Options: o})
        }
        _, err := m.c.Indexes().CreateMany(ctx, models)
        return mapErr(err)
}

// Watch tails the collection's inserts with a change stream (a replica set is required).
func (m mongoCollection) Watch(ctx context.Context, resume bson.Raw) (Cursor, error) {
        // A getMore waits at most the await time, so an idle tail answers well inside the driver's
        // socketTimeoutMS and is not mistaken for a dead connection.
        opts := options.ChangeStream().SetMaxAwaitTime(time.Second)
        if len(resume) > 0 {
                opts.SetResumeAfter(resume)
        }
        pipe := mongo.Pipeline{{{Key: "$match", Value: bson.D{{Key: "operationType", Value: "insert"}}}}}
        cs, err := m.c.Watch(ctx, pipe, opts)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, watchError(err)
        }
        return mongoCursor{cs}, nil
}

type mongoCursor struct{ cs *mongo.ChangeStream }

func (c mongoCursor) Next(ctx context.Context) bool { return c.cs.Next(ctx) }
func (c mongoCursor) Doc() bson.Raw {
        d, _ := c.cs.Current.Lookup("fullDocument").DocumentOK()
        return d
}
func (c mongoCursor) Token() bson.Raw                 { return c.cs.ResumeToken() }
func (c mongoCursor) Err() error                      { return watchError(c.cs.Err()) }
func (c mongoCursor) Close(ctx context.Context) error { return c.cs.Close(ctx) }

// watchError marks the server's history-lost answers (ChangeStreamHistoryLost 286, ChangeStreamFatalError
// 280) as ErrHistoryLost; any other error is transient and keeps its resume token.
func watchError(err error) error {
        var ce mongo.CommandError
        if errors.As(err, &ce) && (ce.HasErrorCode(286) || ce.HasErrorCode(280)) {
                return errors.Join(ErrHistoryLost, err)
        }
        return err
}

package store

import (
        "context"
        "log/slog"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/mongo"
)

// Reads are retried when a new connection to the server fails to open: pool growth or a replaced
// connection does a fresh handshake mid-run, and Atlas has been seen to refuse one with a SCRAM
// "bad auth" that the next attempt passes. A read changes nothing, so repeating it is safe; writes
// are not retried here (the driver's own retryable writes cover network errors).
const (
        readAttempts = 3
        readBackoff  = 100 * time.Millisecond
)

// readSleep waits between attempts; tests replace it.
var readSleep = func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) {
        select {
        case <-time.After(d):
        case <-ctx.Done():
        }
}

// connectErrorClass names the kind of connection failure err is, or "" when it is not one that a
// retry can fix.
func connectErrorClass(err error) string {
        if err == nil {
                return ""
        }
        m := strings.ToLower(err.Error())
        switch {
        case strings.Contains(m, "auth error") || strings.Contains(m, "bad auth") || strings.Contains(m, "authenticationfailed"):
                return "handshake_auth"
        case strings.Contains(m, "connection handshake"):
                return "handshake"
        case mongo.IsNetworkError(err):
                return "network"
        }
        return ""
}

// retryRead runs op, repeating it up to readAttempts times while it fails with a connection
// failure, and logs the class of each failure it absorbs.
func retryRead[T any](ctx context.Context, name string, op func() (T, error)) (T, error) {
        backoff := readBackoff
        for attempt := 1; ; attempt++ {
                v, err := op()
                class := connectErrorClass(err)
                if class == "" || attempt >= readAttempts || ctx.Err() != nil {
                        return v, err
                }
                slog.WarnContext(ctx, "database read failed to connect; retrying", "op", name, "class", class, "attempt", attempt, "error", err.Error())
                readSleep(ctx, backoff)
                backoff *= 2
        }
}

// Package store is the narrow MongoDB surface the data layer needs. The data layer depends on
// this interface so its owner-scoping, revision, and query logic can be tested against an
// in-memory fake (store/fake) without a database.
package store

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "time"

        "go.mongodb.org/mongo-driver/bson"
)

// Sentinel errors returned by every Collection implementation.
var (
        // ErrNotFound means no document matched the filter.
        ErrNotFound = errors.New("store: not found")
        // ErrDuplicate means a unique index rejected the write.
        ErrDuplicate = errors.New("store: duplicate key")
        // ErrHistoryLost means a change stream cannot resume from its token: the history it needs aged
        // out or the token is invalid.
        ErrHistoryLost = errors.New("store: change stream history lost")
)

// FindOptions carries the query-layer sort, projection, and limit for Find.
type FindOptions struct {
        Sort       bson.D
        Projection bson.D
        Limit      int64
}

// IndexSpec describes one index. Partial is a partialFilterExpression; empty means a full index.
type IndexSpec struct {
        Name    string
        Keys    bson.D
        Unique  bool
        Partial bson.D
        // ExpireAfter makes the index a TTL index on its single key: documents expire that long after
        // the time the key holds. Zero means no expiry.
        ExpireAfter time.Duration
}

// Collection is one MongoDB collection. Filters and updates are bson documents built from
// parameters; callers never concatenate strings into them. out arguments are pointers decoded
// from the stored document (Find takes a pointer to a slice).
type Collection interface {
        InsertOne(ctx context.Context, doc any) error
        FindOne(ctx context.Context, filter bson.D, out any) error
        Find(ctx context.Context, filter bson.D, opts FindOptions, out any) error
        // FindOneAndUpdate applies update to the first match and decodes the post-update document.
        FindOneAndUpdate(ctx context.Context, filter, update bson.D, out any) error
        // UpsertOne applies update to the first match, inserting a document seeded from the filter's
        // equality fields when nothing matches. A single atomic write: concurrent callers touching
        // different fields cannot revert one another.
        UpsertOne(ctx context.Context, filter, update bson.D) error
        // DeleteOne removes the first match for good; ErrNotFound when nothing matched.
        DeleteOne(ctx context.Context, filter bson.D) error
        CountDocuments(ctx context.Context, filter bson.D) (int64, error)
        EnsureIndexes(ctx context.Context, specs []IndexSpec) error
}

// Database hands out collections by name.
type Database interface {
        Collection(name string) Collection
}

// Watcher is implemented by collections that can be tailed for inserted documents (a MongoDB
// change stream). Use Watch to reach it from a Collection.
type Watcher interface {
        // Watch returns a cursor over documents inserted from now on, or after the position resume names
        // (a token from Cursor.Token).
        Watch(ctx context.Context, resume bson.Raw) (Cursor, error)
}

// Cursor is a tail of inserted documents.
type Cursor interface {
        // Next waits for the next inserted document; false means the cursor ended (see Err).
        Next(ctx context.Context) bool
        // Doc is the document Next found.
        Doc() bson.Raw
        // Token is the resume position after the document Next found.
        Token() bson.Raw
        Err() error
        Close(ctx context.Context) error
}

// ErrNoWatch means the collection cannot be tailed.
var ErrNoWatch = errors.New("store: collection cannot be watched")

// Watch tails c for inserts, or returns ErrNoWatch when c does not support it.
func Watch(ctx context.Context, c Collection, resume bson.Raw) (Cursor, error) {
        w, ok := c.(Watcher)
        if !ok {
                return nil, ErrNoWatch
        }
        return w.Watch(ctx, resume)
}
